### [33] [GramStatTexNet: Efficient, Interpretable, and Neuro-Inspired Texture Analysis-by-Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23755)
*Vasha DuTell,Zuyu Yun,Anne Harrington,Michael Y-S Fang,Mark Hamilton,Christian Kövesdi,Edward H. Adelson,William T. Freeman,Ruth Rosenholtz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GramStatTexNet：一个结合多尺度Gabor金字塔与Gramian相关统计的混合“分析—合成”纹理模型，在保持合成质量的同时显著提升可解释性、紧凑性，并可自然扩展到空间、时间与离心率维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度纹理合成模型逼真但参数冗余、机制不透明，与生物视觉联系较弱；传统神经科学启发的金字塔模型可解释且紧凑，但依赖人工设计的小规模统计量，泛化和扩展能力不足。因此需要兼具逼真度、生物启发、可解释性和可扩展性的纹理表示。

Method: GramStatTexNet将经典多尺度、多方向Gabor滤波器结构与Gramian-based二阶相关性结合，并组织为结构化滤波器族。每个统计量具有明确的滤波器族身份，因此可按族分解合成保真度，也可学习保留感知纹理内容的低维可解释嵌入。框架还通过空间池化扩展至外周纹理，通过时空滤波器组扩展至动态纹理。

Result: 与经典方法和深度纹理合成基线相比，在多种纹理类别和多个感知指标上表现出具有竞争力的合成质量，同时具有更紧凑、更可解释的表示，并能自然扩展到动态纹理和外周视觉建模。

Conclusion: GramStatTexNet提供了一个统一的、可解释的视觉纹理分析与建模框架，桥接生物启发纹理统计与现代生成式合成，在空间、时间和离心率维度上具有可扩展性。

Abstract: Deep networks achieve high realism in texture synthesis but often rely on opaque, over-parameterized representations that have limited direct connection to biological vision. Neuroscience-informed pyramid models are interpretable, compact, and biologically inspired, but rely on small, hand-curated statistics sets that are difficult to generalize or extend. We introduce GramStatTexNet, a hybrid analysis-by-synthesis framework that combines the multi-scale Gabor filter structure of classical models with the flexibility of Gramian-based correlations, organized into structured filter families. This structured representation enables analyses that are challenging for deep-feature methods: each statistic carries a named filter-family identity, allowing us to decompose synthesis fidelity into per-family contributions and to learn an interpretable, low-dimensional embedding that preserves perceptual texture content. We compare our framework to classical and deep texture-synthesis baselines across diverse texture categories, and on multiple perceptual metrics, highlighting the compactness and interpretability advantages of this hybrid representation, alongside competitive synthesis quality. This same framework extends naturally to peripheral textures via spatial pooling, and to dynamic textures using a spatiotemporal filter bank. GramStatTexNet provides a unified, interpretable framework for analyzing and modeling visual information with natural extensions across space, time, and eccentricity. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [34] [Mean Curvature Minimizing Foliations Of A Vector Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23765)
*Tabish A. Syed,Marc Theveneau,Benedikt Wirth,Rustum Choksi,Kaleem Siddiqi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于单一定向矢量场恢复局部平滑层状结构（sheet/foliation）的数学模型与并行算法，并通过合成与生物影像数据验证其在脑白质纤维和心肌细胞片层重建中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然界和生物组织中存在大量流线型或片层式结构，例如哺乳动物脑白质纤维束和心肌壁中的伸长心肌细胞排列。这些结构被认为倾向于组织为局部光滑的曲面以优化功能。然而，仅从一个与局部取向一致的矢量场中恢复这些层状曲面仍是开放问题。本文旨在解决如何从定向结构测量中推断底层sheet/foliation。

Method: 作者建立了一种“sheet”的数学模型和相应推断算法。给定输入矢量场，算法估计一个与其正交的单位矢量场，并要求由这两个矢量构成的分布满足Lie bracket为零，从而保证其可积并生成局部光滑曲面。为平衡sheet似然性与表面面积，作者进一步最小化恢复曲面的平均曲率。通过计算相关能量泛函的一阶变分，得到可用于数值优化的梯度信息，并实现了高效、可并行的算法。

Result: 在合成数据和生物数据上均验证了该方法的有效性。应用对象包括脑图谱中的白质纤维束和透明化心肌壁组织的共聚焦显微镜图像。尤其在心肌组织样本中，该方法能够以微米级精度重建与局部心肌细胞方向一致的结构片层。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种从单矢量场恢复局部可积分片层结构的数学框架和可并行数值算法，能够在不直接测量二维面元的情况下推断sheet。其在神经影像和心肌微观结构重建等医学成像应用中具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Streamline patterns are ubiquitous in nature. Prominent examples include white matter fibre tracts in the mammalian brain and arrangements of elongated cells, such as cardiomyocytes in the heart wall. It has been posited that such organisations foliate into locally smooth surfaces to optimise function. However, the recovery of these foliations from a single vector field aligned with the local orientation of the underlying sheets remains an open problem. We address this challenge by introducing a mathematical model of a sheet and developing an algorithm for sheet inference, from measurements of the oriented structures. Given an input vector field, our model estimates a second vector field that is orthonormal to it, while also requiring the Lie bracket of the two to vanish. To balance sheet likelihood against low surface area, we also reduce the mean curvature of the recovered local surface. We compute the first variation of an associated energy and provide a numerical method which admits an efficient, parallel implementation for sheet estimation. We demonstrate our approach on both synthetic and biological examples, motivated by applications to medical imaging. These include white matter fibre tracts from a brain atlas and confocal microscopy images of cleared heart wall tissue. For the latter we are able to reconstruct sheets aligned with local cardiomyocyte directions at unprecedented (micrometre) scale.

</details>


### [35] [Keep the Reasoning, Read the Image: On-Demand Visual KV for Multi-Turn Reasoning VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23769)
*Ziyi Wang,Xiyue Zhang,Bing Hu,Guangmin Song,Chenyi Zhang,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Reasoning VLMs prune KV cache during long thinking, but in multi-turn conversations the shared cache budget causes reasoning entries to evict visual entries. ShelfKV is a training-free, retention-policy-agnostic method that keeps visual KV entries in CPU memory and dynamically swaps the most query-relevant visual entries into GPU attention slots, replacing low-scoring text entries. This protects the image without permanently occupying cache slots and substantially improves conversational accuracy at matched GPU memory.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV-cache retention policies are used to control memory cost as reasoning VLMs generate thousands of reasoning tokens. The authors observe that these policies can harm visual understanding in multi-turn conversations: image KV entries and reasoning text entries compete for the same limited cache budget, so growing reasoning text can crowd out visual information. A diagnostic chart question shows that visual degradation increases after a prior round of reasoning, suggesting that the issue is protection of visual entries, not only insufficient cache capacity.

Method: ShelfKV is a training-free add-on to existing KV-cache retention policies. It stores all visual KV entries in CPU memory rather than leaving them in the GPU cache or letting them be pruned. During decoding, at each step it identifies which visual entries are most relevant to the current query and swaps them into GPU attention slots, replacing text entries with the lowest query relevance scores. The image is therefore never evicted from CPU memory but only occupies active GPU attention slots when it is being read, decoupling visual preservation from permanent GPU cache occupancy.

Result: On four benchmarks, including three conversational settings, ShelfKV was evaluated with two retention policies and two reasoning VLMs. It improves DBTrimKV by up to 20.3 points under matched GPU memory and still leads by 8.7 points when DBTrimKV is given twice the memory. It also consistently outperforms a fixed visual-reserve policy under matched GPU memory and does not hurt accuracy on standalone questions. The earlier diagnostic shows that protecting a small visual reserve recovers much of the conversational loss, while simply adding the same slots to the shared budget recovers less.

Conclusion: The central finding is that pruning-based KV-cache management for reasoning VLMs lacks visual protection: the image is evicted not because memory capacity is the only issue, but because visual and reasoning entries share one eviction budget. ShelfKV addresses this by protecting visual entries off-GPU and retrieving them on demand. The work suggests that future retention policies should treat visual context as a distinct protected resource rather than ordinary cache entries competing with reasoning text.

Abstract: Reasoning vision-language models (VLMs) think for thousands of tokens before they answer, and retention policies keep the KV cache affordable by pruning it as the reasoning grows. We find that this pruning sacrifices the image in multi-turn conversations: the image and the reasoning share one budget, so the growing reasoning crowds the visual entries out. A chart question that loses 8 points to the full cache when asked first loses 24 points when asked after a round of reasoning. Protecting a small visual reserve from eviction recovers most of this loss, while adding the same slots to the shared budget recovers considerably less. The image therefore lacks protection, not capacity. Based on this finding, we propose ShelfKV, a training-free method that works on top of existing retention policies. It keeps every visual entry in CPU memory and, at each decoding step, swaps the visual entries that the current query scores highest into the attention slots of the text entries it scores lowest. The image is never evicted, yet it occupies attention slots only while it is read. We evaluate ShelfKV on four benchmarks, three of them conversational, with two retention policies and two reasoning VLMs. In conversations, it improves the strongest existing policy, DBTrimKV, by up to 20.3 points at matched GPU memory, and still leads by 8.7 points when DBTrimKV uses twice the memory. It also consistently outperforms a fixed visual reserve of matched GPU memory in these settings, without hurting accuracy on standalone questions. These results show that the image should be protected from the reasoning but read only on demand.

</details>


### [36] [DetectViT: Test-time Backdoor Detection for Vision Transformers via Inter-Head Attention Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23770)
*Siquan Huang,Yijiang Li,Xingfu Yan,Ningzhi Gao,Leyu Shi,Ying Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DetectViT，一种仅依赖测试时注意力模式的视觉 Transformer 后门检测方法，通过量化两种注意力劫持现象实现高效检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers 广泛用于多模态模型的视觉编码器，但使用第三方预训练权重会暴露于后门攻击。现有测试时检测方法通常依赖预测标签或中间嵌入，跨任务泛化性差且计算开销较大，因此需要不依赖模型输出、训练数据或先验触发器知识的轻量检测方案。

Method: 作者研究后门触发器对 ViT 多头注意力机制的影响，发现两类异常模式：完整注意力劫持导致头间注意力空间重叠异常高；部分注意力劫持导致注意力分布集中度的头间方差异常大。DetectViT 通过头间一致性分数和头间熵方差分数量化这两类现象，并使用少量任意来源的分布外样本估计检测阈值。

Result: 在 BadCLIP、DeiT、CLIP 和 LLaVA 等多种攻击、架构和下游任务（图像分类与图像描述）上，DetectViT 仅需 64–128 个 OOD 校准样本即可显著优于基线方法，在 BadCLIP 上达到 100% 真正例率和 0.17% 假正例率，且因复用推理中已计算的注意力权重而几乎不增加额外开销。

Conclusion: 测试时的注意力结构异常是检测 ViT 后门攻击的有效信号；DetectViT 提供了一种无需标签、训练数据或触发器先验、开销极低且具有良好任务与架构泛化性的后门检测框架。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) now underpin the visual encoders of most multimodal models, but this success comes at a cost: relying on third-party pretrained checkpoints leaves these systems open to backdoor attacks. Existing test-time detection methods rely on either the predicted label or intermediate embeddings, limiting their applicability across diverse tasks and incurring substantial computational overhead. In this work, we study how backdoor triggers affect the multi-head attention mechanism of ViTs and identify two distinct anomalous patterns: $\textit{complete attention hijacking}$, causing the attention to exhibit abnormally high inter-head spatial overlap, and $\textit{partial attention hijacking}$, causing an abnormally large variance in the concentration of the attention distribution. Motivated by these findings, we propose $\textbf{DetectViT}$, a test-time backdoor detection method that requires neither training data, model outputs, nor any prior knowledge of the trigger. DetectViT quantifies the two hijacking phenomena via an inter-head consistency score and an inter-head entropy variance score, with thresholds estimated from a small out-of-distribution (OOD) calibration set drawn from an arbitrary source. Across four representative attacks, diverse architectures (DeiT, CLIP, and LLaVA), and downstream tasks (image classification and captioning), DetectViT substantially outperforms all baselines with as few as 64--128 OOD calibration samples, achieving a true positive rate of $100\%$ and a false positive rate of $0.17\%$ on BadCLIP. Notably, it introduces negligible additional overhead by relying solely on attention weights already computed during inference. Code is available at: this https URL.

</details>


### [37] [ROBIN++: Unified Copyright Protection and Tamper Localization for Diffusion Models via Dual-Domain Synergistic Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23793)
*Huayang Huang,Siqi Zeng,Qian Wang,Bo Du,Yu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ROBIN++，通过频域版权水印与空域定位水印的解耦注入、以及验证阶段的双分支协同检测，缓解数字水印在版权鲁棒性与篡改定位敏感性之间的长期权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成内容数字水印方法通常将版权鲁棒性和篡改定位敏感性强耦合在同一信号或同一域中，导致二者相互冲突：为增强抗破坏能力而牺牲局部篡改检测精度，或为提升定位敏感性而降低版权恢复稳定性。

Method: 作者提出双域协同范式。生成阶段，采用对抗优化算法在频域注入版权水印，提高提取鲁棒性并减少视觉伪影；同时在潜空间中引入内容感知扰动生成器，在空域注入定位水印，以增强对空间篡改的敏感性并降低对版权水印的干扰。验证阶段，设计双分支协同检测器：一方面通过“频域到空域协同”利用鲁棒频谱先验作为局部异常检测的全局锚点；另一方面通过“空域到频域校正”利用预测篡改掩码修复受损潜特征。

Result: 大量实验表明，ROBIN++ 在版权信息恢复和篡改定位精度两方面均显著优于现有方法，有效缓解版权鲁棒性与定位敏感性之间的性能权衡。论文同时承诺公开代码。

Conclusion: 将冲突目标显式解耦到不同表征域，并在检测端进行跨域互补协同，是提升生成内容溯源与完整性验证水印系统性能的有效范式。ROBIN++ 通过频域版权水印与空域定位水印的双域设计，为可信 AIGC 取证提供了新的方法思路。

Abstract: Digital watermarking provides a promising solution for verifying the provenance and integrity of generated content. However, existing methods often suffer from an inherent trade-off between copyright robustness and localization sensitivity due to the entanglement of conflicting signals within a single domain. In this paper, we propose ROBIN++, a paradigm that shifts from single-domain entanglement to a Dual-Domain Synergistic design. Our key insight is to explicitly decouple robustness and fragility during injection by assigning them to distinct domains, while re-coupling them for mutual reinforcement during verification. Specifically, we introduce an Adversarial Optimization Algorithm to inject a frequency-domain copyright watermark during generation, maximizing extraction robustness without visual artifacts. Complementarily, a Content-Aware Perturbation generator is integrated into the latent space to inject the spatial-domain localization watermark, ensuring sensitivity to spatial tampering with minimal interference to the copyright watermark. During verification, we propose a dual-branch synergistic detector that facilitates Frequency-to-Spatial Synergy, where robust spectral priors act as global anchors for local anomaly detection. Conversely, a Spatial-to-Frequency Refinement strategy is employed to rectify corrupted latent features using predicted tamper masks. Extensive experiments show that ROBIN++ significantly outperforms existing approaches in both copyright recovery and tamper localization accuracy, effectively mitigating the long-standing trade-off between the two tasks. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [38] [Sightline: Long-Window Speculative Decoding for Fast SVG Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23807)
*Yanchao Li,Ben Gao,Jiaqing Xie,Yusu Zhang,Beilun Wang,Yanbo Wang,Xiaohua Xu,Nanqing Dong,Tianfan Fu,Zhongyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sightline：面向矢量图形生成模型（如 SVG）的无损推测解码方法，将验证窗口拉长到草稿模型可预测范围，并配合足够宽的草稿树，从而显著加速 SVG 代码生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVG/矢量图形生成模型逐 token 自回归书写，绘图过程较慢。已有推测解码虽无损，但窗口偏短可能截断长接受串。作者发现 SVG 中块级草稿模型的错误较少且成团，深入 block 仍保持准确，因此更长验证窗口有潜力带来更大收益。

Method: 作者提出“run-length identity”及三个命题，说明当候选分支足够宽时，延长验证窗口到草稿模型可预测范围更划算。据此构建 Sightline：使用与草稿模型 horizon 等长的窗口，并构造足够宽的 draft tree 来填满该窗口；在文本到 SVG 和图像到 SVG 任务上进行评测，并与多个无损解码方法比较。

Result: 在 MMSVG、MMSVG-2M、SArena 等基准上，针对三个模型，Sightline 在所有设置中均为比较方法中最快的无损解码器；在统一循环控制条件下仍保持最快。进一步在四个模型系列、六个模型、SVG 代码、压缩 SVG token 和 Lottie JSON 输出上几乎全部领先。分析显示优势主要来自更短窗口会截断的高收益接受轮次；同时推测解码还可加速通过渲染结果选择样本，优于基于 likelihood 的选择。

Conclusion: 矢量图形生成中的推测解码应验证到草稿模型能够看见的范围；Sightline 通过长窗口加宽草稿树提升无损生成速度，为 SVG/Lottie 等结构化图形代码生成提供了更高效的解码范式。

Abstract: Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) generation models write a graphic as code with one forward pass per token, so drawing is slow. Speculative decoding speeds them up losslessly, and block drafters guess many tokens per pass. On SVG, many of their rounds accept the whole draft and end only at the verification window. We find that their misses are rare and clumped, and that they stay accurate far deeper into a block than on natural language. A run-length identity and three propositions show that a longer window pays once the candidates are wide, up to the drafter's horizon. We propose Sightline, which pairs a window as long as the drafter's horizon with a draft tree wide enough to fill it. Experiments cover text-to-SVG and image-to-SVG with three models on six benchmarks from MMSVG, MMSVG-2M and SArena. In every setting, Sightline is the fastest of the compared lossless decoders. On SArena, it stays the fastest when every decoder shares one loop control. It also leads in almost every setting of six models from four other families, which write SVG code, compressed SVG tokens or Lottie JSON. Analyses trace the lead to rounds that shorter windows cut off, both in loop-free content and in loops. Speculative decoding also saves time for choosing samples by rendering, which beats choosing by likelihood. These results suggest that speculative decoding on vector graphics should verify as far as the drafter sees. Code and data are available at this https URL.

</details>


### [39] [PReMind: When Personal Memory Meets Visual Hazards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23808)
*Yeongtak Oh,Donghyeok Lee,Joonhee Oh,Jaewon Jeong,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出PReMind-Bench基准与PReMind两阶段强化学习框架，用于评估和提升个性化视觉语言助手在可见危险场景中对用户个人记忆与视觉证据的联合理解、记忆调用、拒答与安全应答能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化视觉语言助手需要结合视觉观察和用户个人记忆（如过敏史、疾病、行动限制）给出恰当帮助。当场景存在物理危险时，正确响应既要求模型识别可见风险，也要求其调用并正确使用与该人相关的上下文信息；现有模型常只满足其中一个方面，且单独强调安全或个性化仍无法稳定实现联合成功。

Method: 作者首先引入诊断型基准PReMind-Bench，覆盖多样危险场景与个人记忆条件，将评测分解为记忆访问、视觉定位、合理拒答和危险响应，并进一步测量依赖关系案例中的联合成功率。为改善联合表现，作者提出PReMind框架，采用两阶段强化学习，结合按需调用记忆与强制调用记忆两种交互设置，以联合训练有根据的记忆使用和面向危险的应答能力。

Result: 在4B规模模型上，PReMind将互依关系测试集中的联合成功率从基线的12.9%提升到28.6%，并改善主测试集中的危险响应适当性和正确拒答率。在强制调用记忆评测、第二个LLM评判器以及多个Phase II随机种子下，联合成功率提升仍保持；在平均多个种子后，模型在未见危险类别上的联合成功率也有提升。

Conclusion: 个性化视觉语言助手不能仅被单独优化为“安全”或“个性化”，还必须学会将视觉危险证据与相关个人上下文联合连接。PReMind-Bench和PReMind为评估与训练这类联合能力提供了诊断工具和方法路径。

Abstract: Personalized vision-language assistants use visual observations and personal memories to provide advice tailored to the user. When the observed scene contains a physical hazard, personal context, such as allergies, medical conditions, or mobility constraints, can change what help is appropriate. Responding appropriately therefore requires recognizing the visible hazard and applying the relevant person's information. We introduce PReMind-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark that evaluates this combination across diverse hazards and personal-memory conditions. It separately measures memory access, grounding, abstention, and hazard response, and assesses joint success on interdependency cases. Existing models often satisfy one requirement while failing the other, and training only for safety or personalization leaves a gap in joint success. We therefore propose PReMind, a two-phase reinforcement learning framework that combines on-demand and forced-call interactions (memory always retrieved) to jointly train grounded memory use and hazard response. At 4B, PReMind raises joint success from 12.9% to 28.6% over its base on the interdependency set and improves hazard adequacy and correct abstention on the main test. Joint-success gains persist under forced-call evaluation and under a second LLM judge across three Phase II seeds; averaged over these seeds, joint success also improves on unseen hazard groups. These findings highlight the need to evaluate and train personal assistants to connect visual evidence with the relevant person's context.

</details>


### [40] [SWIFT: Slow World, Instant Freshness, and Timely Action for Asynchronous Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23813)
*Jiazhe Wang,Tianyi Yang,Ruihang Li,Fan Lu,Xianming Zeng,Jianyun Xu,Xingliang Liu,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SWIFT 提出一种部署导向的异步“世界-动作”系统，将缓慢的世界模型更新移出观测到控制的临界路径，以在自动驾驶中同时保留生成式世界先验、保证实时新鲜度和动作及时下发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶场景在模型计算过程中持续变化，使观测新鲜度和动作交付时间成为规划核心矛盾；若把昂贵的世界生成模型直接放在实时控制链路中，会拖慢决策，因此需要在不牺牲控制速度的前提下利用丰富的生成式世界先验。

Method: SWIFT 采用双路异步设计：背景 GPU 上由较慢的 Video DiT 持续编码环视历史，生成只读的预测性世界上下文，并训练其覆盖更新间隔和后台计算时间的预测跨度；前景 GPU 上由轻量 freshness carrier 在每一步将当前环视图像编码为门控 key/value 记忆，使快速 Action DiT 能立即结合最新视觉反馈与最近完成的世界上下文，而无需等待世界模型更新。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 开环上，使用轨迹评分器时达到 94.2 PDMS，单次决策 198.4 ms；在 Bench2Drive 闭环上取得 80.30 Driving Score，前景延迟 109.9 ms；并在 Waymo E2E hidden test 达到 8.0050 Rater Feedback Score，在 nuScenes 上实现 0.54 m 平均终点误差和 0.03% 平均碰撞率。与对比世界-动作方法相比，SWIFT 在四个基准上均取得最佳精度-延迟权衡。

Conclusion: 通过将世界模型更新从观测到动作的临界路径中解耦，SWIFT 证明异步生成式世界上下文与实时视觉新鲜度可以在部署导向自动驾驶系统中协同工作，实现更强的端到端驾驶性能与更低控制延迟。

Abstract: Driving scenes keep changing during model computation, making both observation freshness and action-delivery time central to world-action planning. To retain rich generative world priors in autonomous driving without stalling real-time control, we present SWIFT (Slow World, Instant Freshness, and Timely action), a deployment-oriented asynchronous world-action system that moves world updates off the observation-to-action critical path. On a background GPU, a slow Video DiT streams surround-view history into read-only predictive world context, trained with a predictive span that covers both the update interval and background world computation. On a foreground GPU, a lightweight freshness carrier encodes current surround-view images into a gated key/value bank at every step, letting a fast Action DiT combine live visual feedback with the latest completed world context without waiting. On open-loop NAVSIM, SWIFT reaches 94.2 PDMS at 198.4 ms per decision with a trajectory scorer; on closed-loop Bench2Drive, it achieves an 80.30 Driving Score at 109.9 ms foreground latency. SWIFT further transfers to the Waymo E2E hidden test (8.0050 Rater Feedback Score) and nuScenes (0.54 m mean endpoint error, 0.03% mean collision rate). Across all four benchmarks, SWIFT achieves the best results among compared world-action methods, establishing a favorable accuracy-latency trade-off.

</details>


### [41] [Mapping the Design Space of Feature Sampling for Optical Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23822)
*Liping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出SAGE（Sampling with Anisotropic Gaussian Expectation），用逐像素各向异性高斯期望替代光流估计中的固定半径或单点相关特征采样，通过学习采样范围、方向和各向异性来提升跨数据集泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光流方法如RAFT采用固定半径的cost volume查找，WAFT采用单点特征索引，均对所有位置使用统一采样方式，无法适应不同区域的运动、纹理和遮挡特征，导致采样冗余或不足。

Method: SAGE在每个查询位置的中心光流估计附近构建固定候选网格，并用逐像素各向异性高斯期望对相关特征进行聚合。该方法通过正定精度矩阵显式控制采样尺度、各向异性和方向，使采样支撑区域成为可学习的可分析变量，单点采样为其极限特例。

Result: 实验表明，SAGE仅增加约1.5%的乘加计算量，却显著提升零样本跨数据集泛化能力。仅使用FlyingChairs和FlyingThings训练时，SAGE在Sintel clean和final上的端点误差分别降低24.2%和16.4%，在KITTI上F1-epe和F1-all分别降低7.2%和11.6%。

Conclusion: 该研究证明光流相关特征采样不应统一设计，而应根据区域特性自适应学习采样支撑范围与方向。SAGE通过各向异性高斯期望提供了更灵活、可解释且高效的采样机制，提升了光流估计的泛化性能。

Abstract: The computation of correlation features is crucial for optical flow estimation. Existing methods (e.g., RAFT) perform fixed-radius lookups on a cost volume, yielding a set of correlation features for each position; alternatively, WAFT performs single-point feature indexing, yielding one correlation feature vector per position. None of them designs a suitable correlation feature sampling scheme for each position according to regional characteristics. Our insight is that not every position requires the same sampling scheme. Applying an identical sampling scheme to every position is either redundant or insufficient and ignores the specific characteristics of the corresponding region. This paper rethinks the design of feature sampling and investigates the following question: should the support of feature sampling---i.e., the extent and directions over which evidence is aggregated---be learned separately for each query position? To study this question, we propose SAGE (Sampling with Anisotropic Gaussian Expectation). This method replaces single-point sampling with a per-pixel anisotropic Gaussian expectation on a fixed candidate grid centered on the current optical flow estimate. A positive-definite precision matrix controls the scale, anisotropy, and orientation of the sampling support, with single-point sampling included as its limiting case. Consequently, the sampling support becomes an explicit and analyzable design variable and provides a principled way to analyze how directional feature aggregation affects correspondence learning. Extensive experiments show that SAGE, while adding only 1.5% multiply-accumulate operations (MACs), effectively improves zero-shot cross-dataset generalization. When trained only on FlyingChairs and FlyingThings, SAGE reduces the end-point error (EPE) by 24.2% and 16.4% on Sintel clean and final, and reduces KITTI F1-epe and F1-all by 7.2% and 11.6%.

</details>


### [42] [ReflectScene:MLLM-Guided Closed-Loop Diffusion for 3D Indoor Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23845)
*Jiawei Shen,Jie Feng,Junpeng Zhang,Guanbin Li,Wanyi Chen,Weisheng Dong,Guangming Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReflectScene，一种由多模态大语言模型（MLLM）引导的闭环扩散框架，用于文本条件下的 3D 室内场景生成，通过场景理解、语义奖励评估和轨迹一致的决策条件扩散实现对象级修正，以提升布局的功能一致性与语义对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本条件下的 3D 室内场景生成需要同时满足几何、功能和语义约束。现有扩散方法通常依赖静态文本条件，难以根据生成过程中的场景状态持续评估语义偏差，也难以将语义层面的问题转化为可执行的对象级修正；基于 MLLM 的后验优化虽能提供语义反馈，但与去噪过程解耦，可能破坏已生成场景的一致性。

Method: ReflectScene 包含三个关键机制。第一，分层先验接地的场景理解：利用冻结的 MLLM 对齐结构化布局与多视角视觉证据，解释物体级上下文与全局场景语义。第二，MLLM 引导的语义奖励评估：将 MLLM 对候选修正方案的诊断与评估蒸馏到一个轻量级 Reward Predictor 中，以降低推理开销并支持修正决策。第三，决策条件轨迹一致扩散：通过瞬时条件注入和持续的 clean-estimate projection，将选定修正整合到去噪轨迹中，从而修正目标物体布局，同时保持非目标布局稳定。

Result: 在 3D-FRONT 数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够提升生成交付场景的功能一致性和语义对齐程度，同时保持具有竞争力的生成质量与多样性。

Conclusion: ReflectScene 将 MLLM 的语义诊断能力引入扩散生成闭环，使语义反馈能够在去噪过程中转化为可控的对象级布局修正，从而缓解静态文本条件难以评估动态生成状态以及后验修正与生成过程脱耦的问题。

Abstract: Text-conditioned 3D indoor scene generation requires layouts satisfying geometric, functional, and semantic constraints. However, existing diffusion methods rely primarily on static textual conditions, limiting their ability to assess evolving scenes and translate semantic discrepancies into executable object-level corrections. MLLM-based post-hoc refinement provides semantic feedback but remains decoupled from denoising, potentially disrupting scene this http URL propose ReflectScene, an MLLM-guided closed-loop diffusion framework. First, we introduce Hierarchical Prior-Grounded Scene Understanding that aligns structured layouts with multi-view visual evidence, to interpret object-specific context and scene global semantics via a frozen multi-modal large language model (MLLM). Second, we propose MLLM-Guided Semantic Reward Evaluation to distill MLLM's diagnoses and assessments on possible candidate corrections into a lightweight Reward Predictor, enabling correction decision making with reduced computation burden. Finally, Decision-Conditioned Trajectory-Consistent Diffusion is proposed to integrate selected corrections through transient conditioning and persistent clean-estimate projection, correcting object layout in the generated scene while preserving non-target arrangements throughout this http URL on 3D-FRONT demonstrate improved functional consistency and semantic alignment while maintaining competitive generation quality and diversity.

</details>


### [43] [Temporal Image Representations for Mouse Dynamics Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23859)
*Junyi Li,Charles Devlen,Daqing Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出用于鼠标动态认证的时序增强图像表示，包括 XYPlot 与 Pairwise Distance（PWD）两种形式，并在 location-aware 与 location-free 设置下结合预训练 2D-CNN 与 ViT 进行评估，在多个数据集上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图像的鼠标动态认证方法多将光标轨迹绘制为仅包含空间信息的图像，忽略了鼠标运动中的时间特征，而这些时间特征对区分不同用户具有重要作用。

Method: 作者提出两类鼠标事件图像表示：XYPlot 直观呈现运动轨迹，PWD 则利用整幅图像编码鼠标事件之间的关系，并将速度等时间相关信息融入图像表示。随后在 location-aware 和 location-free 两种设置下，分别使用预训练 2D-CNN 与 ViT 模型进行认证性能评估，并在 Balabit、TWOS、ChaoShen 和 DFL 数据集上实验验证。

Result: 引入时间信息后的表示优于仅包含空间信息的表示，且 location-aware 变体总体优于 location-free 变体。其中，结合时间编码的 location-aware PWD 表示与 2D-CNN 架构总体表现最佳，在 Balabit Test-1、Test-2、TWOS、ChaoShen 和 DFL 上的平均等错误率分别为 5.21%、12.70%、4.94%、6.16% 和 0.59%。

Conclusion: 将鼠标运动的时空特征编码为适合计算机视觉模型输入的图像表示，能够显著提升鼠标动态认证性能；在多种数据集和模型架构下，时间信息、位置感知设置以及 PWD 表示与 2D-CNN 的组合均有助于获得更优效果。

Abstract: Mouse dynamics authentication has traditionally relied on handcrafted features or time-series representations that capture both spatial and temporal characteristics of mouse movements. More recent approaches have modeled mouse data as images, enabling the use of powerful pretrained computer vision models. However, prior work primarily focuses on spatial-only representations where the cursor coordinates are plotted as a trajectory image. These representations discard the temporal characteristics which can help discriminate users. In this work, we propose image representations that incorporate speed and velocity information for mouse dynamics authentication. We investigate two mouse data representations, a XYPlot representation that intuitively shows the path of movement, and a Pairwise Distance (PWD) representation that utilizes the entire image canvas to encode relationships between mouse events. Both representations are evaluated under both location-aware and location-free settings using pretrained 2D-CNN and ViT architectures. We found that the incorporation of temporal information improved performance over spatial representations alone, and that the location-aware variants generally outperformed the location-free. We achieve state-of-the-art performance on two datasets, Balabit and TWOS, and extend our evaluation to two other datasets. We found that in general, location-aware PWD with temporal encoding combined with the 2D-CNN architecture provided the best results, with mean EERs of 5.21% and 12.70% on the Balabit Test-1 and Test-2 sets, respectively, 4.94% on TWOS, 6.16% on ChaoShen, and 0.59% on DFL.

</details>


### [44] [MobileWeave: Program-First Synthesis of Dependency-Linked Tasks for Training Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23860)
*Ziyi He,Xian Wu,Qiyuan Chen,Haitao Li,Cehao Yang,Yinghao Zhu,Xichen Zhang,Xiao Chen,Yushi Feng,Lequan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MobileWeave提出以可执行程序形式合成移动GUI训练任务，利用类型化能力图与跨应用组合任务，让智能体学习子目标间的依赖关系，从而提升复杂工作流的组合完成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生移动GUI智能体需要大规模训练轨迹，覆盖单个应用功能和跨应用多目标任务流程。现有自动任务合成方法通常只生成自由文本任务，隐含了子目标之间如何依赖。然而，任务组合本身是一项关键技能：智能体可能分别完成各子任务，却无法把它们串联成完整工作流，因此需要显式表达子目标依赖。

Method: MobileWeave将每个任务合成为从可控模拟器中的应用实现派生的可执行程序。它将每个应用抽象为带有跨应用共享语义类型的类型化能力图，通过显式的值依赖和对象依赖，把原子程序组合为单应用和多应用复合程序，并通过原生执行验证每个程序，从而同时生成任务指令和完成判定标准。训练使用这些任务上的教师轨迹，以及在策略自身停滞时的恢复监督信号。

Result: MobileWeave-8B相比基座模型在MobileGym上提升26.7分，并在AndroidWorld、AndroidLab和MobileWorld上分别提升8.6、21.3和17.6分；4B规模模型也表现出相近的增益。此外，实验表明任务结构本身能够教会策略进行组合，显式的子目标依赖是有效训练信号。

Conclusion: 移动GUI智能体的多目标工作流能力不仅取决于单项子任务技能，还取决于子目标之间的组合与衔接。MobileWeave通过将任务显式程序化并引入跨应用依赖结构，提供了可扩展且可验证的训练数据，并证明组合能力可由任务结构有效学习。

Abstract: Native mobile GUI agents require large-scale training trajectories covering both individual app functions and multi-goal workflows within and across apps, which makes automatic task synthesis a promising way to scale training data. Existing synthesis pipelines, however, produce each task as free-form text, leaving implicit how its subgoals depend on one another. This matters because composition is a skill in its own right: a policy may complete each subtask of a workflow on its own, yet fail to chain these subtasks into the complete workflow. We present MobileWeave, which instead synthesizes every task as an executable program derived from the app implementations of a controllable simulator. MobileWeave abstracts each app into a typed capability graph with semantic types shared across apps, composes atomic programs into single- and cross-app composite programs through explicit value and object dependencies, and verifies each program by native execution, deriving both its instruction and its completion check from the same program. Trained on teacher trajectories collected for these tasks and on recovery supervision at the policy's own stalls, MobileWeave-8B improves its base model by 26.7 points on MobileGym and by 8.6, 21.3, and 17.6 points on AndroidWorld, AndroidLab, and MobileWorld, with comparable gains at 4B. Beyond these gains, we find that composition is learnable from task structure: explicit dependencies between subgoals act as a training signal that teaches a policy to compose skills it already masters. We release data, model, and code at this https URL .

</details>


### [45] [On Demand, Not Uniformity: Adaptive Expert Orchestration for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23865)
*Mengxin Qin,Kun Wei,Weitao An,Jiahua Li,Xu Yang,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出CIRA-MoE，用于多模态持续指令微调：通过可学习阈值路由产生统一的专家覆盖信号，在覆盖不足的层选择性注入新专家，并基于历史与当前专家的相对证据调节token级残差贡献，从而在保留旧知识的同时适应新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MoE的多模态持续指令微调方法通常冻结历史专家并新增专家以缓解灾难性遗忘，但忽视了不同任务在不同层和不同token上的适配需求差异。对输入任务的探测显示，梯度压力在层间呈现异质性，预测难度在token间也明显不同，因此需要进一步解决“额外容量应加在哪些层”以及“新专家对不同token应以多强方式参与”的问题。

Method: 作者提出Coverage-Calibrated Expert Injection and Residual Arbitration framework（CIRA-MoE）。其核心是用可学习阈值路由推导专家接受边际，形成统一的覆盖信号。历史接受证据用于指导在覆盖不足的层中选择性注入新专家；历史专家与当前专家之间的相对证据用于调节token级残差贡献；同时引入仲裁一致正则化，促进专家专业化。

Result: 在UCIT和CoIN两个多模态持续指令微调基准上的实验表明，CIRA-MoE优于现有SOTA方法，能够在持续学习新指令数据的同时更好地保留先前知识。

Conclusion: CIRA-MoE通过显式建模层间容量需求和token级专家贡献强度，改进了MoE在MCIT中的专家扩展与使用方式，为缓解灾难性遗忘并提升持续适应能力提供了有效机制。

Abstract: Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) enables Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to adapt to sequential instruction data while retaining previously acquired knowledge. While recent Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-based methods mitigate forgetting by freezing historical experts and introducing new ones, they largely overlook the varying adaptation demands across tasks. Probing incoming tasks reveals pronounced heterogeneity in layer-wise gradient pressure and token-wise prediction difficulty. This observation highlights two unresolved decisions: where additional capacity is needed and how strongly new experts should contribute to individual tokens. To address both questions, we propose the Coverage-Calibrated Expert Injection and Residual Arbitration framework (CIRA-MoE), which derives expert acceptance margins via learnable threshold routing to serve as a unified coverage signal. Specifically, historical acceptance evidence guides the selective injection of new experts into insufficiently covered layers. Meanwhile, relative evidence between historical and current experts modulates token-wise residual contributions, coupled with arbitration-consistent regularization to promote expert specialization. Comprehensive experiments on UCIT and CoIN demonstrate that CIRA-MoE outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in MCIT.

</details>


### [46] [HOForce: Motion and Force Reconstruction of Hand-Object Interaction Using a Single-View RGB-D Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23871)
*Jiahui Wang,Wanqing Du,Yuanbin Zou,Zhefeng Xu,Feng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HOForce 是一种从单视角 RGB-D 视频中同时重建手物交互运动、接触力与物体间作用力的方法，能够提升被遮挡区域的手物跟踪精度，并显著改善指尖法向力估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单相机手物交互重建便于大规模采集，但手与物体之间频繁发生的严重遮挡使运动估计困难；仅重建几何姿态不足，进一步恢复交互力可为遮挡区域提供物理约束，并增强手物跟踪与仿真迁移能力。

Method: 提出 HOForce，输入单视角 RGB-D 视频和已知物体属性，联合跟踪手和物体运动，并恢复交互力。对于双手操作，同时建模两只手的指尖力；当场景中存在两个物体时，进一步建模物体间接触力。所恢复的力作为姿态约束反馈到跟踪过程，以改善遮挡区域中的手物运动重建。

Result: 在 DexYCB 和 TACO 上，HOForce 比基线更准确地恢复手和物体运动，并降低手物穿透问题。在配备触觉传感器记录的指尖力数据集上，相比最强基线，法向力平均绝对误差降低 24.5%。恢复出的力还能在 MuJoCo 中复现物体运动，并在适配后迁移到其他物理引擎。

Conclusion: HOForce 证明了从单视角 RGB-D 视频中同时恢复手物运动与交互力的可行性；将接触力建模作为运动重建与物理仿真的桥梁，可提升遮挡条件下手物交互跟踪的准确性，并增强结果在物理引擎中的可迁移性。

Abstract: Reconstructing hand-object interactions is an important task, and achieving it with a single camera makes large-scale capture practical but challenging due to heavy occlusions. We introduce HOForce to track hands and objects, and further recover interaction forces from single-view RGB-D video and known object properties. For two-hand manipulation, we jointly model fingertip forces of both hands and the contact forces between objects when two objects are presented. The reconstructed forces also improve motion tracking by giving pose constraints in the occluded regions. HOForce recovers hand and object motion more accurately on DexYCB and TACO and produces less hand-object penetration than interaction baselines on DexYCB. On our dataset of fingertip forces recorded with tactile sensors, HOForce reduces normal-force MAE by 24.5% relative to the strongest baseline. The recovered forces reproduce object motion in MuJoCo and transfer to other engines after adaptation.

</details>


### [47] [Keep the Image in Mind: A Visual Board for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23872)
*Haiming Zhu,Zhiwei Chen,Ding Cao,Tianxiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种以“视觉板（Visual Board）”为中心的多步视觉推理框架，通过将观察、不确定解释和推理过程分离到可修改的结构化工作区，降低早期视觉错误被错误延续的风险，并提升无训练条件下的推理性能与 token 效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步视觉推理的瓶颈不仅在于模型收集视觉证据是否充分，还在于这些证据如何在后续推理中被表示。传统做法把观察和推理不断追加到对话或自由笔记中，容易导致早期的视觉误解成为后续推理的错误前提。因此，作者希望设计一种机制，让每个前提都可显式审计，便于发现“所见”与“所推”之间的冲突，并触发对原图的定向复查。

Method: 论文引入 Visual Board-centered harness：在推理过程中维护一个结构化、容量受限且可修订的工作区，将视觉观察、带有不确定性的解释和派生推理分开表示。这种分离使各前提显式化，可由纯文本 critic 检测视觉证据与推理结论之间的冲突，并调用原图进行针对性重查。该方法不需要任务特定训练；作者进一步用 harness 轨迹进行监督微调，以改善直接回答和无需 critic 的工具调用能力。

Result: 在 4B、8B、Qwen3-VL-Flash 和 32B 等多种模型配置下，该方法在 Euclid30K、SolidGeo 和 ChartQA 条形图任务上均稳定优于独立模型，提升幅度最高分别达到 11.68、18.42 和 24.82 个百分点。在工具、critic 和交互预算相同的条件下，结构化 Visual Board 比共享自由笔记高 6.67 到 13.00 个百分点，比无 Board 重试高 7.35 到 18.80 个百分点，同时 token 用量减少 7.9% 到 38.2%。基于 harness 轨迹的监督微调还能提升直接回答和无 critic 工具使用，并正向迁移到图表推理。

Conclusion: 论文表明，多步视觉推理的性能不仅取决于“收集多少视觉信息”，也取决于“如何表示这些信息”。将视觉证据、不确定解释和推理结果结构化分离，能够增强可审计性、冲突检测和局部复查能力，从而在不依赖任务训练的情况下显著提升准确性并降低计算与上下文成本。

Abstract: Multi-step visual reasoning suffers when early visual misinterpretations persist as incorrect premises in subsequent reasoning. We argue that how visual evidence is represented during reasoning matters as much as how much of it is gathered. Rather than appending observations and inferences to an ever-growing dialogue, we introduce a Visual Board-centered harness that separates visual observations, uncertain interpretations, and derived reasoning within a structured, capacity-bounded, and revisable workspace. This separation makes each premise explicit and auditable, so that conflicts between what is seen and what is derived become detectable even by a text-only critic and can trigger targeted reinspection of the original image. Without task-specific training, our harness consistently outperforms standalone models across 4B, 8B, Qwen3-VL-Flash, and 32B configurations on Euclid30K, SolidGeo, and ChartQA bar charts, with gains of up to 11.68, 18.42, and 24.82 percentage points, respectively. Under identical tools, critic, and interaction limits, the structured Board outperforms shared free-form notes by 6.67 to 13.00 points and no-Board retries by 7.35 to 18.80 points while using 7.9\% to 38.2\% fewer tokens. Supervised fine-tuning on harness trajectories further improves direct answering and critic-free tool use, with positive transfer to chart reasoning.

</details>


### [48] [NullTok: empirical-null token selection for training-free long video understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23881)
*Zhang Ziyue,Juwei Jian,Baiyang Song,Yunshan Zhong,Jun Peng,Yiyi Zhou,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出免训练方法 NULLTOK，通过构建经验零分布并对目标相关性进行 p 值校准，实现长视频中跨帧 patch 级的证据保留与 token 选择；在受限视觉 token 预算下，结合视频去重与联合时空预算分配，可显著提升多模态大模型在长视频理解基准上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解面临视觉 token 数量爆炸的问题。现有主流范式包括帧选择和 token 选择，二者可串联使用，但若先按帧筛选再剪枝 token，帧级相关性估计噪声可能导致关键证据被不可逆地丢弃。因此需要从查询相关证据的时空分布角度，设计更稳健、免训练且高召回的视觉 token 选择机制。

Method: NULLTOK 对视频中每一帧的所有 patch 构建经验零分布：使用 64 个组匹配的无关控制查询，估计无目标证据时各位置的分数分布。随后将查询与 patch 的目标相关性校准为同位置参考下的右尾蒙特卡洛 p 值，这些 p 值在条件可交换性假设下呈现离散超均匀特性。基于校准后的筛选结果和高召回语义守卫机制，筛选候选 token，再通过确定性视觉去重和跨帧、跨区域的联合视觉 token 预算分配，形成紧凑输入。

Result: 作者在两个代表性多模态大模型上进行大量视频问答实验，验证该方法在受限视觉 token 预算下的有效性。在 Video-MME 上，使用冻结的 LLaVA-Video-7B 达到 65.81%，使用 Qwen3-VL-8B 达到 68.85%，且均在 64 个完整帧等效（FFE）token 预算、不使用字幕和音频的条件下超过所有评估基线。

Conclusion: NULLTOK 通过经验零分布校准和语义保护，有效缓解了长视频中“先选帧再剪 token”导致关键证据丢失的问题，能够在较低视觉 token 预算下保留查询相关时空信息，从而提升多模态大模型的长视频理解能力。

Abstract: Long video understanding remains a daunting challenge for existing multimodal large language models (MLLMs), mainly due to the large number of visual tokens accumulated in long videos. Currently, frame selection and token selection are two main paradigms for addressing this issue, both aiming to select relevant visual information to form a compact input for MLLMs. A natural approach combines these two paradigms by selecting frames before pruning tokens, but noisy frame-level relevance estimates can cause irreversible evidence loss. In this paper, we study this problem from the perspective of assessing the spatiotemporal distribution of query-specific evidence and propose a novel and training-free approach termed NULLTOK. For every patch across all frames, we construct an empirical-null distribution from 64 group-matched irrelevant control queries, capturing the scores expected in the absence of target-specific evidence. Target affinities are calibrated against these same-position references into right-tail Monte Carlo p-values, which are discrete super-uniform under a conditional exchangeability assumption. Calibrated screening and a high-recall semantic guard produce candidate tokens for deterministic visual deduplication and joint spatiotemporal budget allocation. To validate NullTok, we apply it to two representative MLLMs and conduct extensive experiments on a set of video benchmarks. The experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness under a bounded visual token budget. For example, it achieves 65.81% and 68.85% on Video-MME using frozen LLaVA-Video-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B backbones, respectively, at a cap of 64 full-frame equivalents (FFE), without subtitles or audio. These results outperform all evaluated baselines on Video-MME under the same budget for both backbones. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [49] [Can AI Understand Indonesian Children's Book Illustrations? Compliance, Safety, and the Cultural Alignment Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23887)
*Rian Adam Rajagede,Rochana Prih Hastuti,Fitri Hasanah Amhar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估视觉语言模型在儿童绘本插画评估与生成中的可靠性，发现其在合规性和安全性等显式标准上表现较好，但在文化契合度等隐性标准上存在系统性盲区；AI生成的插画也表现出类似不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童绘本插画具有发展、教育、安全和文化层面的重要责任，并通常受行业或政府指南约束。随着AI系统被引入儿童出版流程，需要判断其能否可靠理解和应用这些结构化标准，尤其是在文化敏感性方面。

Method: 作者以印尼官方发布的《图书分级指南》作为评估框架，该指南为四个阅读级别提供了明确视觉标准。研究首先使用真实出版的儿童绘本页面评估视觉语言模型的判断能力，并与人类基线比较；随后利用视觉语言模型作为评判器，对两个AI生成模型产出的插画进行评估，并邀请部分人类插画师进行交叉评审。

Result: 结果表明，视觉语言模型在显式标准如指南合规性和安全性上较为可靠，但在文化契合度上表现不足，经常遗漏人类评审认为文化不适宜的内容。AI生成的插画同样满足结构和安全性要求，却难以捕捉人类能识别的隐含文化细节。

Conclusion: AI系统目前能够胜任儿童绘本插画中较明确、规则化的评估任务，但在隐性文化语境理解方面仍不可靠。因此，在AI进入儿童出版流程时，不能仅依赖模型自动评估，还需要结合人类专家判断，尤其是涉及文化适配和内容敏感性的环节。

Abstract: Children's book illustrations carry developmental, pedagogical, and cultural responsibilities that are often formally codified in guidelines. As AI systems begin entering children's publishing pipelines, both as evaluators and as generators, a natural question arises: can they reliably meet the structural, safety, and cultural standards required? We investigate this across three criteria: guideline compliance, safety, and cultural alignment, using Indonesia's Book Leveling Guideline as a government-issued evaluation framework with explicit visual criteria for four reading levels. To understand how well VLMs can identify and apply these guideline criteria, we first evaluate them on real published children's book pages and compare their assessments against a human baseline. To then examine whether AI-generated illustrations meet the same standards, we apply the VLM judge to images produced by two generation models and cross-check them against a subset of human illustrator raters. We find that VLMs are reliable on explicit criteria such as compliance and safety, but fall short on cultural alignment, systematically missing content that human raters flag as culturally inappropriate. AI-generated illustrations show the same blind spot: they satisfy structural and safety criteria but fail to capture implicit cultural details that human raters consistently identify.

</details>


### [50] [Beyond Additive Composition: Interventional Modeling of State-Dependent Cross-Degradation Responses for Composite Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23889)
*Zongda Tang,Jie Feng,Wenda Zhao,Mingtao Feng,Yuping Liang,Ronghua Shang,Weisheng Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出PROBE，通过显式建模多重复合退化之间的状态依赖相互作用来提升复合图像复原能力，并构建了覆盖15种复合退化组合的新基准SCD-15。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有复合图像复原方法通常将多种退化拆解为独立因素并利用各自先验指导复原，但实际中共存的退化之间存在动态耦合：一种退化的表现形式会随其他退化状态变化，产生非加性交互，单独建模难以准确捕捉。

Method: 首先将视觉和文本中的退化—干净偏移对齐，构建统一的退化坐标轴；随后通过校准坐标头估计当前退化状态；再沿坐标轴注入双向特征扰动，探测不同退化之间的跨坐标响应；通过与强度匹配的单退化参考结果进行对比，量化固有的非加性偏差；最终利用响应描述符和状态坐标共同指导图像复原。同时提出SCD-15基准，覆盖4种退化因子的全部15种非空组合及3个严重程度等级。

Result: 在CDD-11、AWMM-100K和SCD-15复合复原数据集上，PROBE取得了领先性能，并且采用紧凑模型即可实现较低GPU显存需求下的优异效果。

Conclusion: 复合图像复原的关键不仅在于识别单个退化因素，更在于显式建模退化之间的状态依赖相互作用；PROBE通过干预式跨退化响应探测和统一退化坐标表示，有效捕捉非加性交互，提升了复合退化复原性能，并为该领域提供了更全面的评测基准。

Abstract: Composite image restoration aims to recover images corrupted by multiple coexisting degradations. Existing methods predominantly decompose composite corruptions into individual factors, relying on these isolated priors to guide restoration. However, coexisting degradations are inherently interdependent: the manifestation of one degradation dynamically shifts with the states of others, inducing non-additive interactions that factor-wise paradigms fail to capture. To bridge this gap, we formulate state-dependent cross-degradation responses and propose PROBE, an interventional framework that explicitly models how coexisting degradations influence one another. Specifically, we first construct unified degradation axes by aligning visual and textual degraded-clean offsets. Along these axes, a calibrated coordinate head estimates current degradation states, while bidirectional feature perturbations are injected to probe cross-coordinate responses under the current states. Contrasting these responses with corresponding intensity-matched single-degradation references quantifies intrinsic non-additive deviations, allowing the resulting response descriptors and state coordinates to jointly guide restoration. For comprehensive evaluation, we introduce SCD-15, a new benchmark covering all 15 nonempty combinations of four degradation factors at three severity levels. Experiments demonstrate that PROBE achieves state-of-the-art performance on the CDD-11, AWMM-100K, and SCD-15 composite restoration datasets using a compact model with modest GPU memory requirements.

</details>


### [51] [SixthView: A Benchmark for Hidden-View Reconstruction and 3D Assembly Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23891)
*Pooya Khosravi,Jen-Feng Chang,Fnu Pramono*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SixthView，一个要求模型从五个正交视图和部件信息预测第六个隐藏视图的 3D 装配推理基准，并评估多模态输入对 VLM 空间推理能力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 装配中隐藏侧视图的推断依赖于部件放置和遮挡推理。由于同一组观测视图可能对应多个满足可见信息但隐藏视图不同的合法装配，目标往往存在歧义。因此需要一个可验证、可密集评测的基准来测试模型能否进行符号化装配和坐标级空间推理。

Method: 作者构建 SixthView，包含 602 个合成 polycube 装配，每个装配含 2–10 个部件。任务给定五个正交视图和部件形状信息，要求模型预测缺失的第六个视图，并输出密集网格形式的部件标签。为保证目标视图明确，作者对所有与精确部件几何和观测标签网格一致的合法装配进行穷举验证，确认它们均产生同一目标视图。图像-only 变体进一步要求模型从渲染视图中恢复形状，从而引入遮挡导致的视觉歧义。实验评估三个视觉语言模型在四种输入条件下的表现。

Result: 在 552 个完整评测样本中，使用图像加精确文本输入时，exact-grid 准确率介于 33.2% 到 85.4% 之间。相比仅提供图像，加入精确文本可使准确率提升 19.7–63.0 个百分点；而在已有精确文本的基础上再加入图像，准确率仅变化 -0.5 到 +2.1 个百分点。此外，将图像拆分为独立面板比合并成单张图提升 6.7–11.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: 该任务的性能高度依赖模型能否访问精确的符号化几何信息；一旦几何被明确描述，图像带来的额外帮助有限。SixthView 揭示了当前 VLM 在 3D 装配、遮挡处理和坐标级空间推理方面的不足，也说明仅靠视觉输入难以稳定恢复隐藏结构。

Abstract: Inferring the unseen side of a 3D assembly requires reasoning about part placement and occlusion. Different assemblies can match the observed views while producing different hidden views, leaving the target ambiguous. We introduce SixthView, a benchmark in which models predict a sixth orthographic view from five observed views and information about the component shapes. The benchmark contains 602 synthetic polycube assemblies with 2--10 parts and requires a dense grid of part labels for the missing view, allowing both exact-view and cell-level evaluation. For each item, we exhaustively verify that all legal assemblies consistent with the exact component geometry and observed label grids yield the same target view. With these inputs, the task tests symbolic assembly and coordinate reasoning. The image-only variants additionally require shape recovery from rendered views, where occlusion can make different 3D shapes look identical. We evaluate three vision-language models under four input conditions. Across 552 items with complete evaluations, exact-grid accuracy with images plus exact text ranges from 33.2% to 85.4%. Adding exact text to separate images improves accuracy by 19.7--63.0 percentage points, whereas adding images to exact text changes it by only $-0.5$ to $+2.1$ points. Separate image panels outperform a merged sheet by 6.7--11.1 points. The results show that performance depends strongly on access to exact symbolic information, with little further improvement from images once the geometry is specified. GitHub: this https URL

</details>


### [52] [ProxySearch: Decoupled Inference-Time Scaling for Diffusion Models via Asymmetric Noise-Rank Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23929)
*Seungwook Kim,Jongmin Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究在文本到图像扩散模型的推理时噪声搜索中，蒸馏得到的少步模型能否作为教师模型的高效代理。作者发现代理与教师在“可解析图像统计量”上的排序高度一致，但在“学习型语义评分器”上几乎不一致，并提出 PROXYSEARCH：用代理快速筛选符合统计目标的一阶段候选，再由教师选择语义奖励最高的候选，从而在不牺牲语义目标的情况下显著提升统计约束满足度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声搜索可以在推理阶段改善扩散模型生成质量，但通常需要大量多步教师模型采样，成本很高。当前少步蒸馏模型已被广泛发布，但它们在噪声搜索中作为教师代理的潜力尚未被系统刻画。作者因此想回答：蒸馏代理的哪些排序信号能够可靠迁移到教师，哪些不能，以及如何利用这种不对称性设计更高效的推理搜索方法。

Method: 作者在三种蒸馏设定和三种主干架构（DiT、MMDiT、UNet）上测量代理与教师之间的排序一致性。结果显示：闭式图像统计量的排序能从代理迁移到教师，Spearman ρ 最高可达 0.80；而学习型语义评分器的排序迁移很弱，ρ ≤ 0.12。配对零对照实验进一步表明，粗粒度统计量的一致性主要来自首步去噪，细粒度统计量的一致性更多来自蒸馏本身。基于这一不对称性，作者提出 PROXYSEARCH：在联合约束下使用两个目标，一个语义奖励控制内容，一个数值目标控制图像统计量。代理模型仅用一步筛选统计匹配的候选，教师模型只在存活候选上评估并返回语义奖励最高者，不需要用一个权重在两个目标之间做显式权衡。

Result: 在 FLUX-1024 上，PROXYSEARCH 在相同教师轨迹数量下，将统计目标约束收紧 21% 至 23%，同时未检测到被优化语义奖励的损失。

Conclusion: 蒸馏少步模型并非在所有评分信号上都适合作为教师代理，但在闭式图像统计目标上具有高效筛选价值。利用“统计量可迁移、语义评分不可迁移”的不对称性，可以把低成本代理用于统计过滤，把高成本教师保留给语义选择，从而提升推理时噪声搜索的效率与可控性。

Abstract: Noise search at inference time improves text-to-image diffusion but requires many multi-step teacher rollouts per query. Distilled few-step models are now routinely released, yet their potential as proxies inside noise search is uncharacterized. We measure proxy-teacher rank agreement across three distillation regimes and three backbones (DiT, MMDiT, UNet) and find a sharp asymmetry. On our main pool, closed-form image statistics transfer from the proxy to the teacher (Spearman $\rho$ up to 0.80), while learned semantic scorers do not ($\rho \le$ 0.12). Paired null controls trace this rank to the first denoising step for coarse statistics and to distillation for finer ones. We use the asymmetry for noise search under a joint constraint: a semantic reward specifies content, and a numeric target specifies image statistics. In PROXYSEARCH, the proxy filters candidates by statistical match in 1 step, and the teacher returns the survivor with the highest semantic reward. No weight trades one objective against the other. On FLUX-1024, PROXYSEARCH tightens the statistical target by 21-23% at the same number of teacher trajectories, with no detected loss on the optimized reward.

</details>


### [53] [TRoPE: Topology-aware Relative Positional Encoding for 4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23933)
*Chengzhuo Gui,Zhenglin Zhou,Fan Ma,Zongxin Yang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视频到4D网格生成中的“粘连伪影”，本文提出拓扑感知相对位置编码TRoPE和尾部风险损失，并构建MeshSticking-4D基准，显著降低跨部件运动泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式Video-to-4D网格生成方法容易在空间上接近但网格连接上相隔较远的顶点之间错误传递运动信息，导致sticking artifacts。作者认为其根源是cross-part motion leakage，即运动解码器未能区分空间邻近性与网格拓扑连通性。

Method: 提出TRoPE，通过图拉普拉斯提取谱特征，并利用多尺度扩散特征之间的距离构建顶点与锚点之间的成对拓扑关系，再以rotary phase调制vertex–anchor attention，使网格连接结构显式参与运动检索。同时提出vertex-wise tail-risk objective，聚焦持续高误差顶点以抑制粘连。另构建MeshSticking-4D基准用于刻画和量化该失效模式。

Result: 在MeshSticking-4D上，TRoPE能抑制粘连伪影并提升整体运动精度；相比基线，最差顶点误差降低39.4%，分离失败率从49.35%降至34.90%。在Motion-80和Motion324-Subset上也观察到更低尾部误差，在Mesh4D上的迁移实验表明方法具有跨骨干适用性。

Conclusion: 显式建模网格拓扑关系并优化高误差尾部顶点，是缓解Video-to-4D网格生成中空间邻近导致跨部件运动泄漏的有效路径，可提升动态网格恢复的局部分离准确性。

Abstract: Video-to-4D mesh generation has made substantial progress in recovering dynamic 3D geometry and motion from monocular videos. However, commonly used feed-forward approaches remain prone to sticking artifacts, where geodesically distant vertices move together incorrectly when they become spatially close. We attribute this failure to cross-part motion leakage: spatially nearby yet structurally distant vertices may retrieve similar motion information when motion decoding fails to distinguish spatial proximity from mesh connectivity. To address this issue, we introduce TRoPE, a topology-aware relative positional encoding that makes mesh connectivity explicit in the interaction between vertex queries and mesh-supported motion sources. TRoPE derives spectral features from the graph Laplacian and constructs pairwise topological relations from distances between multi-scale diffusion features, using these relations to modulate vertex--anchor attention through rotary phases. We further introduce a vertex-wise tail-risk objective that emphasizes persistently high-error vertices to explicitly suppress sticking artifacts. To better characterize and quantify these failures, we introduce MeshSticking-4D, a dedicated benchmark for this failure mode. Experiments on MeshSticking-4D show that TRoPE suppresses sticking artifacts while improving overall motion accuracy. Compared with the baseline, it reduces the worst-vertex error by 39.4% and lowers the separation failure rate from 49.35% to 34.90%. Additional evaluations on Motion-80 and Motion324-Subset show lower tail errors on diverse objects, while transfer to Mesh4D demonstrates applicability across backbones.

</details>


### [54] [What Right-Angle Corners Reveal About an Uncalibrated Camera: Convex Cones, Certified Bounds and Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23934)
*Michael Werman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究分析图像中由三条相互垂直边缘交汇形成的“墙角型”角点，如何用于约束未标定相机内参，尤其是在仅焦距未知时形成可计算、可组合的凸可行集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未标定相机的内参估计是计算机视觉基础问题。实际场景中常见墙角、箱体角等三条正交边交汇结构，其在图像中表现为三条线段共点。研究这类简单但广泛存在的局部结构能否为相机内参，特别是焦距，提供有效且可计算的约束，具有理论和应用价值。

Method: 将每个三垂直边交汇的图像角点建模为对相机内参的约束。首先证明每个角点对应一个关于相机参数的显式凸集合；在仅焦距未知的情形下，该集合退化为一个闭区间。这些约束只依赖图像测量，因此多个角点联合标定可转化为凸可行性问题。进一步刻画单个角点何时给出焦距下界或上界，以及多个角点如何唯一确定焦距。若已知角点的三维位置，还可对边方向测量误差进行有界认证。

Result: 结果表明，几乎所有角点都能为焦距提供下界；研究还给出了何时可得上界、以及多个角点何时能唯一确定焦距的条件。该方法不依赖场景尺度信息，具有显式、凸性和可验证的误差分析优势。

Conclusion: 墙角型角点可作为未标定相机内参估计的有效约束源。通过将每个角点转化为焦距的凸可行集，多角点标定问题可自然表述为凸可行性问题，为简单、局部且鲁棒的相机标定方法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The corner of a box or a room, where three perpendicular edges meet, appears in an image as three segments meeting at a point. We ask what such junctions reveal about an uncalibrated camera's intrinsic parameters. Each junction is consistent with an explicit convex set of cameras; when only the focal length is unknown, this set is an interval in closed form. These sets are computed from the image alone, so calibrating from many junctions is a convex feasibility problem. Almost every junction bounds the focal length from below; we characterize when one also bounds it from above, and when many junctions determine it exactly. For a known vertex, the bounds can be certified against any bounded error in the measured edge directions.

</details>


### [55] [Angular Conditional Distillation: Overcoming Classifier Geometry Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23941)
*Xuefei Zhang,Ruoqi Wang,Lei Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Angular Conditional Distillation（ACD），通过角度坐标接口和尺度不变条件蒸馏，缓解知识蒸馏中由 logit 参数化导致的分类器几何失配问题，实现更可靠的语义知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在知识蒸馏中，分类器输出常被用作知识接口，但标准 logit 损失会混合可迁移的语义关系与架构相关的参数化偏差，如幅值和 bias 效应，从而造成教师与学生分类器之间的几何不匹配，使直接迁移分类器知识不可靠。

Method: ACD 包含两个组件：Angular Coordinate Interface（ACI）将学生分类器重构到角度坐标空间，抑制输出幅值和 bias 影响，提供几何兼容的迁移空间；Scale-Invariant Conditional Distillation（SICD）对输出进行标准化，仅迁移条件化的非目标类别关系，过滤不可迁移的绝对置信度。ACI 解决“在哪里迁移”，SICD 解决“迁移什么”。

Result: 大量实验表明，ACD 相较于已有知识蒸馏方法取得领先性能；作为通用即插即用模块，它能稳定提升现有蒸馏流程，并且不需要特征适配器，也不增加推理时延。

Conclusion: 将分类器知识迁移限定在几何一致且尺度不相关的条件关系上，可以有效提升知识蒸馏的可靠性和通用性；ACD 为分类器输出口提供了实用的几何感知蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Classifier outputs provide a fundamental and univer- sally available knowledge interface for knowledge distil- lation (KD). However, standard logit objectives entangle transferable semantic relations with architecture-dependent parameterization artifacts, causing a classifier geometry mismatch that makes direct knowledge transfer unreliable. To address this, we propose Angular Conditional Distilla- tion (ACD). First, the Angular Coordinate Interface (ACI) reconstructs the student classifier using angular coordi- nates, suppressing magnitude and bias effects to provide a geometry-compatible space. Then, Scale-Invariant Condi- tional Distillation (SICD) standardizes outputs and exclu- sively transfers conditional non-target relations, filtering out untransferable absolute confidence. Together, ACI de- fines where to transfer, while SICD defines what to transfer. Extensive experiments show that ACD achieves state-of-the- art performance over existing KD methods. Moreover, as a universal drop-in replacement, it consistently improves existing distillation pipelines while requiring no feature adapters or additional inference-time cost. Code is avail- able at this https URL.

</details>


### [56] [SIGNTOK: A Text-Aligned Continuous Pose Tokenizer for Sign Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23944)
*Nabeela Khan,Gaurav Rai,Bowen Wu,Sihan Tan,Benjamin Yen,Takeshi Ashizawa,Kazuhiro Nakadai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SIGNTOK是一种面向文本条件手语生成（SLG）的连续姿态tokenizer，通过运动学、句子级和词级对齐头重塑潜空间，使表示更按语言内容组织而非按身份组织，从而提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLG方法直接在姿态空间、量化码空间或重建导向潜空间中生成，这些空间主要被签署者身份而非语言内容主导。论文指出，在CSL-Daily上，一个测试片段在原始姿态空间中的最近邻仅有2%来自不同人签署的同一句子，说明现有表示不利于文本到签译语义对齐。

Method: 提出SIGNTOK，一种连续姿态tokenizer，仅通过训练阶段的三个辅助头塑造潜空间：运动学头预测动作动态，句子级头将整体签署动作与文本句子对齐，词级头将短时动作窗口与内容词对齐。目标是在保持姿态重建/生成能力的同时，让潜空间按语言语义组织。

Result: SIGNTOK将跨签署者同一句子的最近邻率从2%提升到82%。在三个手语基准上，使用相同生成器但在SIGNTOK潜空间中生成，相比在姿态空间中生成，可在保持相近关节准确率的同时，在六个benchmark-scorer设置中的五个提升回译BLEU-4。词级对齐在每一个基准上的回译分数都高于句子级对齐。

Conclusion: 手语生成中的表示空间组织方式对文本条件生成至关重要。SIGNTOK通过语义对齐目标把潜空间从身份主导向内容主导转变，显著改善跨签署者语义一致性和生成质量，其中词级对齐比句子级对齐更有效。

Abstract: Text-conditioned sign language generation (SLG) depends not only on the generator but also on the space in which signing is generated. Existing approaches generate directly in pose space, in quantized code spaces, or in learned latent spaces trained for reconstruction. We find that these spaces are organized largely by signer identity rather than linguistic content: on CSL-Daily, a test clip's nearest neighbor in raw pose space is the same sentence signed by another person in only 2% of cases. To address this, we propose SIGNTOK, a continuous pose tokenizer whose latent space is shaped by three training-only heads: a kinematics head that predicts motion dynamics, a sentence-level head that aligns signing with the overall text, and a word-level head that aligns short motion windows with content words. Together, these objectives raise the same-sentence cross-signer nearest-neighbor rate to 82%. On three sign language benchmarks, the same generator trained in the SIGNTOK latent instead of pose space raises back-translation BLEU-4 in five of six benchmark-scorer settings at comparable joint accuracy, and word-level alignment gives higher back-translation scores than sentence-level alignment on every benchmark.

</details>


### [57] [How Complete Must an Environment Be to Train a Capable GUI Agent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23946)
*Jingxing Wang,Teng Wang,Xingyu Lou,Zihan Guo,Jun Wang,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究GUI智能体训练环境中“环境覆盖度”的影响，发现过度简化奖励路径和数据会降低性能，而合理裁剪无关功能反而能提升成功率，并提出低成本的MobileGym-Lite。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体环境为降低构建成本和人工标注负担，往往在不同维度上删减环境信息，但这些删减对训练效果的影响未被系统测量。本文旨在回答：训练一个有能力的GUI智能体需要多完整的环境，以及应在哪些维度上保持或裁剪环境。

Method: 作者将环境简化程度分解为奖励覆盖度、数据覆盖度和功能覆盖度三个维度，在MobileGym中分别调整这些维度，训练Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct，并统一在原始完整环境中评估策略。通过比较不同环境裁剪方式下智能体在完整环境中的成功率，分析各维度对训练效果的影响，并进一步构建简化但覆盖度合理的MobileGym-Lite。

Result: 实验表明：只奖励单条参考轨迹的验证器会使成功率下降10.53个百分点；删除无关数据记录也会降低成功率；但裁剪无关应用功能反而使成功率从51.61%提升到56.13%，且提升发生在未被改动的任务上。该收益受训练采样权重影响：每轮episode等权会逆转收益，仅平衡被裁剪任务的episode时收益消失。基于发现，MobileGym-Lite保持完整奖励覆盖和数据覆盖，同时裁剪无关功能，在完整环境中以55.48%优于完整环境的51.61%，并降低构建成本。

Conclusion: 训练GUI智能体时，环境并不总是越完整越好，但关键覆盖度不可随意删减：奖励路径的多样性和数据覆盖度对性能至关重要，而无关功能可能带来干扰，合理裁剪反而提升泛化。MobileGym-Lite说明通过保持关键覆盖、移除冗余功能，可以在降低环境构建成本的同时获得更好的训练效果。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents are widely trained in executable GUI environments. To lower cost, build environments in batches, and reduce manual effort, each work trades away part of the environment through its construction choices. We describe these choices along three dimensions: reward coverage, the goal-reaching trajectories the verifier rewards; data coverage, the application data the agent is exposed to beyond what the task reads or writes; and feature coverage, the application features beyond what the task uses. Existing works set each dimension on engineering grounds, but they do not measure its effect on training. How complete must an environment be to train a capable agent, and along which dimensions? In MobileGym, we adjust each dimension separately, train Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct, and evaluate every policy in the original full environment. A verifier that rewards only one reference path lowers success by 10.53 points, and removing unneeded records also lowers success. Pruning unneeded features instead raises success from 51.61% to 56.13%, entirely on tasks whose environments it left unchanged. The gain reverses when each episode is weighted equally and vanishes when only episodes on the pruned tasks are balanced. Without pruning, length weighting there reinforces long detour successes that erode skills on other tasks. Following these findings, we build MobileGym-Lite, which keeps reward and data coverage complete and prunes unneeded features. It outperforms Full in the full environment (55.48% versus 51.61% success) with lower construction cost.

</details>


### [58] [VOL-Agent: Programming Your Film via Video Orchestration Language Agentic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23950)
*Hongji Yang,Yucheng Zhou,Lingting Zhu,Shengju Qian,Xinye Li,Huan Zheng,Dongyue Lu,Wei Chow,Xin Wang,Cheng-zhong Xu,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 VOL-Agent：一种基于视频编排语言（VOL）的可编程视频智能体，通过静态编译与校验、运行时动态加载、技能驱动执行、结构化记忆和批评反馈，生成长达分钟级且保持叙事一致性的视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基础模型通常只能生成高质量短片段，而视频智能体多依赖自然语言规划或预设流程，难以编程化、定制化和扩展化，导致分钟级、故事连贯的电影化长视频生成仍然困难。

Method: 作者引入视频编排语言 VOL，将视频生成流程表示为可编程结构，并采用静态编译与验证保证可靠性、运行时动态加载保证灵活性；通过技能驱动执行器从开放世界技能库中动态解析工具与技能，也支持自然语言定义的用户技能；利用结构化记忆记录与召回世界和实体状态，保持镜头间实体与场景一致；结合批评反馈进行局部修订，并持续积累可复用的导演技能与制作经验。

Result: 在三个基准实验上，VOL-Agent 能够生成分钟级视频，在整体质量上表现较强，同时成本较低。

Conclusion: 将可编程视频编排语言、动态技能执行、结构化记忆和反馈修订相结合，可以显著提升长视频生成的故事连贯性、一致性、可控性、可扩展性与性价比，为自动化电影级长视频制作提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent video foundation models generate high-quality and limited-length clips, but orchestrating them into minute-level, story-coherent films remains an open problem. Existing video agents highly rely on natural-language planning or predefined workflows, which make their production hard to program, customize, and extend. Therefore, we introduce VOL-Agent, a programmable video agent built on a Video Orchestration Language (VOL). With static compilation and verification, followed by runtime dynamic loading, VOL-Agent decouples structural reliability from execution flexibility. Through the skill-driven executor, VOL-Agent dynamically resolves tools and skills from an open-world repository and supports user-defined skills in natural language. Moreover, VOL-Agent is capable of crafting new director skills from learned knowledge and improves itself through feedback to accumulate filmmaking knowledge. To overcome the disconnect between different segments, we apply a structural memory mechanism to record and recall world and entity states, sustaining entity and scene consistency across different shots. Critic feedback supports local revision, while reusable filmmaking knowledge and experiences can be retained for subsequent tasks. Experiments on three benchmarks show that VOL-Agent generates minute-scale videos with strong overall quality and limited cost.

</details>


### [59] [SimplePro: A Simple Baseline for Proactive Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23954)
*Yilin Wang,Xiangxi Zheng,Dongxing Mao,Linjie Li,Zhengyuan Yang,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出SIMPLEPRO，一种无需训练的训练冻结VLM方法，用于主动视频理解。它通过显式汇报规则和结构化流式上下文，使模型在视频持续到达时判断何时回答、何时保持沉默。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动视频理解不仅要求视觉语言模型回答视频内容，还要求其在线流式场景中决定何时需要响应、何时继续观察。现有VLM擅长离线长视频理解，但在主动流式设定中表现不佳，因此以往方法常依赖任务特定强化学习或辅助触发模块。

Method: SIMPLEPRO在每个决策步组织结构化输入：近期高分辨率帧、有界历史观测和此前答案记录；模型据此判断新信息是否值得响应，并在必要时生成简洁答案。随后系统过滤连续重复答案，同时保留原始输出作为后续决策上下文，全程不更新模型参数，也不引入辅助触发器。

Result: 在OVO-Bench、StreamingBench-PO和ProactiveVideoQA上，基于4B模型的SIMPLEPRO持续优于专用主动视频理解系统，并且随着骨干模型从2B扩展到32B，整体性能随模型规模提升。

Conclusion: 显式汇报规则和结构化流式上下文足以显著增强冻结VLM的主动视频理解能力。SIMPLEPRO作为简单、无需训练但有效的基线，有望推动后续流式主动视频理解研究。

Abstract: Proactive video understanding requires vision-language models (VLMs) to decide not only what to answer as a video unfolds, but also when newly arrived evidence warrants a response and when to remain silent. Although modern VLMs excel at offline long-video understanding, they struggle in proactive streaming settings, leading prior work to introduce task-specific reinforcement learning or auxiliary triggering modules. We find that explicit reporting rules and structured streaming context substantially improve a frozen VLM's proactive response performance. We exploit this observation to build SIMPLEPRO, a training-free baseline for proactive video understanding. At each decision step, the model combines recent high-resolution frames, bounded historical observations, and prior answer records to determine whether new information warrants a response and, when needed, generate a concise answer. The system then filters consecutive duplicate answers while retaining the raw outputs as context for subsequent decisions. Without any parameter updates or auxiliary triggers, SIMPLEPRO with a 4B backbone consistently outperforms specialized proactive systems across OVO-Bench, StreamingBench-PO, and ProactiveVideoQA. SIMPLEPRO shows overall performance gains as backbone size increases from 2B to 32B. We hope this simple yet effective method will serve as a strong baseline for proactive video understanding, and we will release our code and inference pipeline to support future research.

</details>


### [60] [DORMS: Image Segmentation via Depth-Ordered Region Merge Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23958)
*Furkan Mumcu,Yasin Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DORMS，一种完全基于算法的深度序区域合并图像分割方法，仅使用单目深度图即可实现区域分割，无需神经网络或可学习的分割参数，并在多个数据集上优于经典基线，在某些场景下可与零样本神经分割模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像分割方法通常依赖大规模神经网络或可学习模型，成本高且可解释性有限。本文旨在探索是否可以通过纯算法的几何推理，从单目深度图中直接生成高质量图像分割结果，从而在不使用学习式分割模型的情况下获得有效分割。

Method: 该方法首先使用现成的单目深度估计器生成深度图，然后构建深度序合并树，通过确定性 union-find 扫描合并区域。合并过程结合区域面积、深度持续性和空间接触约束。系统还进行全局深度去趋势处理以更好分离局部几何结构，并通过阈值搜索根据目标区域数量确定分割粒度。

Result: 在 COCO Panoptic、Cityscapes 和 ADE20K 上的实验表明，DORMS 相较经典分割基线取得一致提升，并与零样本神经分割模型具有竞争力。尤其在 Cityscapes 上，该方法超过所有评估的零样本模型，在 COCO 和 ADE20K 上也与若干方法表现相当。

Conclusion: DORMS 表明纯算法式的几何推理能够从单目深度图中实现高质量图像分割，为无需学习式分割模型的分割路径提供了可能，在可解释性、无需训练参数以及依赖几何结构方面具有优势。

Abstract: We introduce DORMS (Depth-Ordered Region Merging for Segmentation), a purely algorithmic approach to image segmentation from monocular depth maps, requiring no neural networks or learned parameters in the segmentation process. An off-the-shelf monocular depth estimator provides the input depth maps. Our method constructs depth-ordered merge trees through a deterministic union-find sweep, combining component area, depth persistence, and spatial contact constraints to guide region merging. A threshold search determines the segmentation granularity according to a specified target region count, while global depth detrending improves the separation of local geometric structures. Extensive experiments on COCO Panoptic, Cityscapes, and ADE20K demonstrate consistent improvements over classical segmentation baselines and competitive performance against zero-shot neural segmentation models. Notably, our method outperforms all evaluated zero-shot models on Cityscapes, while remaining competitive with several approaches on COCO and ADE20K. These results highlight the potential of purely algorithmic geometric reasoning for image segmentation without learned segmentation models.

</details>


### [61] [SEE: Unlocking the Hidden Perception of Frozen VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23962)
*Alin-Ionut Popa,Tuo Zhang,Dimitrios Dimitriadis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Self-Elicited Evidence (SEE)，一种无需训练、无需外部检测器或OCR引擎的方法，让冻结的视觉-语言模型（VLM）在低置信度时根据自己“指向”的区域进行放大复查并聚合答案，从而提升小物体、细节密集图像等任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM能理解图像整体内容，但常忽略问题所依赖的细小视觉细节，如大图中的小物体或密集信息图中的数字。尽管模型最终答案错误，其注意或指向的区域往往包含正确证据，因此应利用这种内生定位能力进行二次推理。

Method: SEE在初始答案置信度较低时提示冻结VLM提出可能包含证据的区域，对这些区域进行放大裁剪并重新回答问题；同时利用模型自身的文本转录能力处理文字密集图像；最后通过置信度加权投票将复查答案与原始预测聚合。该方法训练无关，不依赖独立目标检测器或OCR引擎。

Result: 在五个不同骨干模型上评估，SEE在所有小物体基准上提升2.3至13.1个百分点，并在小物体任务上优于五个训练无关基线。结合SEE后，Qwen3.5-4B可超过Qwen3.5-9B的直接回答性能，并达到参数量约6倍大的Qwen3.5-27B的水平。

Conclusion: SEE表明：通过激发冻结VLM自身的区域定位与复查能力，可以显著改善其对细粒度视觉证据的利用，是一种高效、通用且不增加训练成本的视觉推理增强方法。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) grasp the overall content of an image but often miss the fine details a question hinges on, such as a small object in a large photo or a number in a dense infographic. Yet even when their answer is wrong, the region they point to often contains the evidence. We introduce Self-Elicited Evidence (SEE), a training-free method that lets a frozen VLM look again where it points. When the initial answer has low confidence, SEE prompts the model to propose regions where the details may appear, answers the question on these enlarged crops, and aggregates the answers with the original prediction through confidence-weighted voting. The model also answers with its own transcript, which helps on text-rich images. SEE needs no detector or OCR engine. Across five backbones, SEE improves every small-object benchmark by 2.3-13.1 points and outperforms five training-free baselines on small objects. With SEE, Qwen3.5-4B surpasses the direct answer of Qwen3.5-9B and matches the 6 times larger Qwen3.5-27B.

</details>


### [62] [Dual-Manifold Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23964)
*Hongxu Chen,Chun Wang,Yanghao Wang,Hongxiang Li,Zhen Wang,Zhixiang Zhang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DM-VAE：用低维支路提取平滑拓扑骨架，作为内部正则约束高维 VAE 潜空间，缓解高维 latent 流形病态导致的 off-manifold 问题，无需依赖外部基础模型即可取得 SOTA 重建与生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在潜在扩散模型中扩大 VAE 潜维度可提升重建上限，但无显式结构约束的高维潜空间易形成粗糙、病态的流形，使生成潜码偏离真实数据流形。已有方法用 DINOv2 等外部基础模型平滑流形，但受预训练分布和语义偏置限制，在分布外域上可能无法保留真实视觉细节。

Method: 构建双流形 VAE：高维重建支路负责细节重建，低维支路形成更平滑、适合生成的拓扑骨架，并将高维流形锚定到该低维子空间。训练阶段引入拓扑表示对齐 TRA，监督扩散生成器中间特征；推理阶段提出带拓扑骨架的内部引导 IG_TS，引导结构生成同时保留高频细节。该框架不依赖外部基础模型，也可与 DINOv2 正交结合。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 256×256 上，DM-VAE 达到 rFID 0.12；无 CFG 时生成 FID50K 为 1.28，有 CFG 时为 1.20；与 DINOv2 结合后进一步降至 1.11。

Conclusion: 低维潜空间的平滑拓扑可作为高维 VAE 的内部结构正则，优于固定外部先验带来的泛化限制；DM-VAE 为高维潜扩散生成提供了更高效、可解释且可组合的流形约束思路。

Abstract: In latent diffusion models, expanding the latent dimension of variational autoencoders (VAEs) is essential to push the upper bound of reconstruction quality. However, without explicit structural constraints, such unregularized expansion easily yields a rugged and ill-conditioned latent manifold, causing generated latents to drift off the manifold during generation (i.e., off-manifold problem). Recent works smooth this manifold by using external foundation models (e.g., DINOv2) as a topological regularizer. However, we argue that tying the latent space to frozen external priors inherently restricts generalization: bound by their pre-training distributions and semantic biases, they may fail to capture faithful visual details on out-of-distribution domains. Observing that low-dimensional latents naturally form a smoother, generation-friendly manifold, we propose dual-manifold VAE (DM-VAE) to leverage internal representations as a topological regularizer. Specifically, DM-VAE pairs a high-dimensional reconstructive branch with a low-dimensional branch that extracts a clean topological skeleton, anchoring the high-dimensional manifold to this smooth subspace. Building upon this framework, we further introduce topological representation alignment (TRA) to supervise intermediate generator features during training, and internal guidance with topological skeleton (IG_TS) to guide structural components while preserving high-frequency details at inference. On ImageNet-1K (256x256), our foundation-model-free DM-VAE achieves a state-of-the-art rFID of 0.12 and a generation FID50K of 1.28 (w/o CFG) and 1.20 (w/CFG), and further reaches 1.11 when orthogonally combined with DINOv2.

</details>


### [63] [TOMAS: Topology-Aware Operation Modeling and Adaptive Staging for Egocentric Hand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23970)
*Guanzhong Sun,Junyi Ma,Yuxuan Wu,Erhang Zhang,Chenyang Xu,Yanzi Miao,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TOMAS，一个面向第一视角（egocentric）视频的未来手部运动预测框架。其核心是拓扑感知的操作建模与自适应阶段划分机制，通过将手部骨骼结构、关节动态与物体语义及空间关系关联起来，推断潜在交互目标和操作类型，并以此条件化手部轨迹预测；同时引入带有可学习时间边界的有序潜在阶段，并利用阶段相关的几何子目标优化轨迹。多个公开基准上的实验表明，TOMAS 在多维度、多目标手部运动预测任务上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角视频中的未来手部运动预测对理解人类意图和手-物交互具有重要意义。然而，即使与同一物体交互，不同的操作意图也可能导致显著不同的手部轨迹。现有方法尚未充分探索手部关节运动与物体语义之间的内在关系，因而难以捕捉由操作类型决定的运动模式。

Method: 作者提出 TOMAS 框架，包含两个关键机制：（1）拓扑感知的手-物推理机制，将手部骨骼结构、关节动态与物体语义和空间关系联合建模，以推断潜在交互目标与隐藏操作；这些操作相关表示作为未来手部轨迹预测的条件，并根据不同数据集的标注形式进行自适应监督。（2）自适应阶段划分机制，将未来手部运动组织为带有可学习时间边界的有序潜在阶段，并在各阶段中引入几何子目标，依据底层操作模式引导轨迹优化。

Result: 在多个公开基准数据集上的实验结果显示，TOMAS 在多维度、多目标手部运动预测任务中达到了最先进的性能（state-of-the-art），验证了物体-操作联合建模和阶段性子目标机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模手-物交互中的物体语义、操作模式和阶段性运动结构，TOMAS 能够更准确地预测第一视角下的未来手部轨迹。该研究强调了操作相关表示与阶段化几何子目标对手部运动预测的重要性，并为理解人类操作意图和手-物交互提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Forecasting future hand movements from egocentric videos plays an important role in understanding human intentions and hand-object interactions. However, different manipulation intentions can lead to substantially different hand trajectories even when interacting with the same object. Existing methods have not sufficiently explored the intrinsic relationships between articulated hand movements and object semantics to capture such operation-dependent motion patterns. Therefore, we propose TOMAS, a topology-aware operation modeling and adaptive staging framework for egocentric hand forecasting. Specifically, we develop a topology-aware hand-object reasoning mechanism that associates hand skeletal structures and articulation dynamics with object semantics and spatial relationships to jointly infer potential interaction targets and latent manipulation operations. The resulting object-operation representations provide interaction conditions for future hand trajectory prediction, with supervision adapted to the annotations available in each dataset. Furthermore, we introduce an adaptive staging mechanism that organizes future hand movements into ordered latent stages with learnable temporal boundaries. Stage-specific geometric subgoals are then incorporated to guide trajectory refinement according to the underlying manipulation patterns. Experimental results on multiple publicly available benchmarks demonstrate that TOMAS achieves state-of-the-art performance in multi-dimensional and multi-target hand motion forecasting.

</details>


### [64] [Agreement Is Not Accuracy: LiDAR Elevation Errors Hidden in LiDAR-Camera Extrinsics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23972)
*Yeon Kun Lee,Dongseok Yang,Deok-Kyeong Jang,Choongho Chung,Junyoung Lee,Hyeongseok So*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文利用同步LiDAR强度与相机估计的棋盘格位姿，联合估计外参数和传感器相关的高度角校正，揭示LiDAR内部几何偏差可被标定外参吸收，从而改善真实几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR与相机可提供互补的三维视觉信息，但外参标定后的观测一致并不能证明LiDAR内部几何正确。系统性LiDAR仰角偏差可能被误补偿进外参，影响三维定位、平面角度估计和图像边缘对齐，因此需要独立估计并验证LiDAR内部几何校正。

Method: 该方法同步采集LiDAR强度和相机棋盘格位姿，对每段记录估计外参，并跨记录共享传感器特定的高度角校正。通过射线-平面交点在棋盘格上定位图案，避免直接使用LiDAR测距值导致误差影响查表。在匹配的强度模型和校正模型下，比较该查表方法与测量点查表方法的相机一致性残差，并在多组同构传感器装置、独立稠密相机扫描参考结构和滚转条件下验证校正效果。

Result: 结果显示，在六组使用相同传感器模型的标准化装置中，基于排除当前记录所得的校正将平均平移参考距离从7.57 mm降低到3.04 mm。在独立结构上，校正降低了相对于稠密相机扫描参考值的平面夹角误差，并改善图像边缘对齐。滚转装置时，获得最大角度改善的平面对符合仅高度角校正的预期。虽然标定目标投影和回环一致性残差变化较小，但几何精度显著提升。

Conclusion: 外参标定后观测一致不能代表LiDAR内部几何正确。同步的常规棋盘格观测提供了无需大型专用标定场即可估计和检验LiDAR高度角校正的实用途径，有助于恢复更可靠的传感器内部几何并提升三维测量一致性。

Abstract: LiDAR and cameras provide complementary measurements for 3D vision, yet agreement between their observations does not establish the accuracy of either sensor's internal geometry. We show that systematic LiDAR elevation biases can be absorbed into the estimated extrinsics. Using synchronized LiDAR intensities and camera-estimated checkerboard poses, we jointly estimate a sensor-specific elevation correction shared across recordings and extrinsics for each recording. Ray-plane intersections locate the checker pattern without using reported ranges, preventing range errors from directly moving the lookup. Under matched intensity and correction models, this lookup gives smaller camera-consistency residuals than measured-point lookup. Across six identically designed rigs using the same sensor models, recording-excluded corrections reduce the mean translation-reference distance from 7.57 to 3.04 mm. On separate structures, they reduce inter-plane angle errors against references from independent dense camera scans and improve image-edge alignment. Rolling the rigs shifts the plane pairs exhibiting the largest angular improvements, as predicted for an elevation-only correction. Within the ray-plane model, calibration-target projections and loop-consistency residuals change little despite these geometric improvements. These findings show why agreement after extrinsic calibration need not establish correct internal LiDAR geometry. Synchronized observations of a familiar checkerboard provide a practical route to estimating and testing elevation corrections without a dedicated large calibration field. We plan to release code and data for reproducing the main results.

</details>


### [65] [SIRAZ: Sparse Inter-Layer Residual Aggregation for Zero-Shot Visual Neural Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23991)
*Neha,Mudasir Ganaie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出SIRAZ框架，通过模仿人类视觉层级、融合生物启发的早期视觉表示与神经信号时序动态，提升零样本脑信号到图像检索/视觉解码的跨模态对齐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经视觉解码方法学到的神经表示与生物视觉系统的层级表征差异较大；需要更符合人类视觉通路结构的模型，以更好地从EEG/MEG等神经信号中推断视觉信息。
