Method: 提出SIRAZ：利用中央凹通路和磁细胞通路启发的Difference-of-Gaussians变换建模早期视觉过程；通过提取多层图像中间表示并采用残差门控适配器机制对齐语义与神经信息；引入方向感知多尺度时序注意力编码器刻画神经信号的时间动态。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG数据集的被试内零样本图像检索中取得92.5% top-1和99.27% top-5；在THINGS-MEG数据集上取得50.25% top-1和75% top-5。

Conclusion: 将生物视觉层级表示与神经动态的结构化建模相结合，可增强脑信号与视觉语义的跨模态对齐，提高零样本视觉解码和图像检索性能。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces aim to establish direct communication between neural activity and external systems, making interpretation of brain signals an important problem. In this context, neural visual decoding seeks to infer visual information from neural activity by learning a mapping between brain responses and the corresponding visual stimuli. It investigates how visual information emerges from neural signals and relates to perceptual processing while enabling the reconstruction or retrieval of visual stimuli from neural signals. Our brain primarily follows a hierarchical pathway to process visual information captured by the eye. However, neural representations learned by existing methods differ fundamentally from those produced by the biological visual system. To bridge this gap and mimic the human vision hierarchy, we introduce Sparse Inter-layer Residual Aggregation for Zero-shot (SIRAZ). SIRAZ is a framework that aligns neural visual representations by jointly modeling early visual processes via fovea- and magnocellular-pathway-inspired Difference-of-Gaussians transformations. To align the semantic information with neural information, it extracts multiple intermediate representations of the image at multiple depths and passes them through the Residual Gated Adapter Mechanism. To study the temporal dynamics of neural signals, the Direction-aware Multi-Scale Temporal Attention Encoder is introduced to align the learned neural representation with the final visual representation. The proposed framework achieves 92.5% top-1 and 99.27% top-5 accuracy in the intra-subject setting on the THINGS-EEG dataset, and 50.25% and 75% top-1 and top-5 accuracy in the THINGS-MEG dataset. The results indicate that combining biologically motivated visual representations with the structured modalities of neural dynamics improves cross-modal alignment and zero-shot image retrieval.

</details>


### [66] [WaveView: The Novel View Is in the Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23994)
*Kaiwen Wang,Jan-Hendrik Pauls,Yinzhe Shen,Frank Bieder,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 WaveView，通过将源图像/视频的重投影结果注入预训练生成模型初始噪声的相位中，实现低开销、强几何约束的生成式新视角合成，并在静态和动态 NVS 上均取得性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式新视角合成方法通常依赖显式 warp 或隐式编码相机参数/3D 线索来引导生成。前者需要视频扩散模型处理重投影信息，后者需要额外条件建模，二者都会增加推理开销；同时，生成结果的空间布局仍可能偏离真实场景，导致视图不一致或几何漂移。因此，需要在不修改生成器架构、不显著增加推理成本的前提下，将源视图的空间结构更自然地注入生成过程。

Method: WaveView 的核心思想是将重投影后的源视图信息注入预训练生成器初始噪声的相位中。该方法利用双树复小波包变换（Dual-Tree Complex Wavelet Packet Transform, DT-CWPT），实现局部且具有各向异性的相位注入，使生成器在不同区域受到不同程度的结构约束。在可见区域，模型能够保留更强的源视图几何和外观线索；在遮挡区域（disoccluded regions）以及沿极线方向，生成器受到较弱约束，从而保留自由生成和合理补全的能力。WaveView 不改变原有生成器架构，因此推理开销几乎可以忽略。

Result: 在 DL3DV 基准上，WaveView 在所有 overlap bins 中均优于所有基线方法。在最困难的 overlap bin 中，相比共享同一生成器架构的 MetaView，WaveView 在共视区域上提升了 0.84 dB PSNR，同时推理速度约为其 2.1 倍。在动态新视角合成任务中，使用相同噪声机制微调 AnyView 后，其在 AnyViewBench 上的 zero-shot PSNR 从 11.58 dB 提升到 12.35 dB。

Conclusion: WaveView 通过相位空间中的重投影注入，为预训练生成模型提供了一种轻量且有效的几何条件化方式。它无需改变生成器架构即可改善视图合成的布局一致性和重建质量，同时在静态与动态 NVS 场景中均表现出明显优势，尤其在遮挡和共视混合的困难区域兼顾了结构保持与生成灵活性。

Abstract: Generative novel view synthesis (NVS) employs a diffusion model to render a static or dynamic scene from a target camera view. Explicit warp methods complete a reprojection of the source with video diffusion models, and implicit methods encode the camera parameters or 3D cues. Both add inference overhead, and the generated layout often drifts from the ground truth. We present WaveView, which injects the warped source into the phase of the initial noise of a pretrained generator. The Dual-Tree Complex Wavelet Packet Transform (DT-CWPT) allows local and anisotropic phase injection. The generator is thus constrained less in disoccluded regions and along the epipolar line. WaveView keeps the architecture and adds negligible overhead. On the DL3DV benchmark, it outperforms all baselines in every overlap bin. In the hardest bin, it gains 0.84 dB PSNR on the covisible region over MetaView, which shares its generator, while running 2.1 times faster. For dynamic NVS, fine-tuning AnyView with the same noise raises its zero-shot PSNR on AnyViewBench from 11.58 to 12.35 dB.

</details>


### [67] [ReSynth: Constructing Synthetic Supervision for Multi-Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23996)
*Yilin Wang,Xin Yu,Xinyi Guo,Xiangxi Zheng,Dongxing Mao,Linjie Li,Zhengyuan Yang,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ReSynth，利用单图像感知与可控图像生成构造跨图像关系监督数据，以提升视觉语言模型对多视图、前后变化和场景关系理解的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们常通过比较不同视角或不同状态来理解世界，但现有 VLM 将不同视图作为独立图像处理，难以建立它们之间的关系；同时，这类任务所需的大规模成组图像关系数据，如相同物体多视角、同一场景变化前后，难以从真实照片集合中获取。

Method: 方法 ReSynth 将单图感知与可控生成转化为多图关系监督。首先利用 Qwen3-VL-8B 的描述、定位和计数能力验证物体数量、属性等单图事实；再结合 Qwen-Image-2.1 的生成与编辑能力构造前后对比对、同一实例多视角以及可比较计数和属性的场景内容；最后基于这些经过验证的事实构建多图问答数据，并使用约 440K 条问题通过 GRPO 训练 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 和 Qwen3-VL-8B，训练过程中不使用真实图像。

Result: 在 BLINK、MUIRBench 和 VLM2-Bench 上，Qwen2.5-VL-7B 和 Qwen3-VL-8B 的平均性能分别提升 17.73 和 6.10 分。Qwen2.5-VL-7B 的单图能力基本保持，Qwen3-VL-8B 在四个评测基准上的单图性能均有提升。此外，经过该监督训练后的 8B 模型在 BLINK 和 MUIRBench 上可超过更大的 Qwen3-VL-235B。

Conclusion: 通过单图感知验证与可控生成相结合，ReSynth 能够大规模生成可靠的跨图像关系监督数据，从而显著增强 VLM 的多图像比较与理解能力，并在不依赖真实成组图像的情况下实现高性能训练。

Abstract: People often understand the world by comparing different views of it. Vision-language models (VLMs), however, receive these views as separate images and still struggle to relate them. One problem is data. Training on these tasks requires groups of images with a known relationship: the same object from different viewpoints, or the same scene before and after a change. Such examples are hard to collect at scale from existing photo collections. We introduce ReSynth, which turns single-image perception and controllable image generation into cross-image relational supervision. Localized editing produces before-and-after pairs, viewpoint generation produces multiple views of the same instance, and verified scene contents support comparisons of counts and attributes. ReSynth combines Qwen3-VL-8B's captioning, grounding, and counting capabilities with Qwen-Image-2.1's generation and editing capabilities. It verifies single-image facts such as object counts and attributes, then combines these facts to construct multi-image QA. Using GRPO on approximately 440K ReSynth questions, with no real images during training, Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B improve by 17.73 and 6.10 average points on BLINK, MUIRBench, and VLM2-Bench, while single-image performance is broadly preserved on Qwen2.5-VL-7B and improves across all four evaluated benchmarks on Qwen3-VL-8B. Notably, this supervision enables the 8B perception model itself to outperform Qwen3-VL-235B on BLINK and MUIRBench.

</details>


### [68] [Choosing Supervised Post-Processing for Frozen Person Detectors: Information, Object Changes, and Complete Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24003)
*Tianyi Zhou,Zijun Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究在冻结检测器之后，如何为监督式后处理选择信息丰富的输出读取（readout），在 CrowdHuman 和 MOT17Det 上比较候选得分、类别分布、几何信息、原生集合摘要及邻域元分类等特征，并评估检测性能、误检代价与推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结检测器后仍可通过监督后处理提升最终输出，但读取模块应选择哪些可用信息、其处理成本如何，直接影响检测收益、误检数量和部署可行性。传统只看特征维度或净检测增益不足以判断方案是否值得采用。

Method: 作者在统一的候选生成、监督标签和运行点条件下，比较多种输出读取特征，包括候选置信度、类别分布、几何关系、原生集合摘要以及适配的邻域元分类。实验在 CrowdHuman 上进行冻结评估，并进一步做源数据冻结迁移到 MOT17Det，同时测量执行优化后的推理时间。

Result: 在 320 张 CrowdHuman 保留图像上，向 97 维读取中加入原生集合上下文可多匹配 275 个对象，但也多产生 96 个未匹配预测。在另 319 张图像上，Full100 匹配 6004 个对象，高于 raw WBF 的 5848 和 MetaDetect 风格适配的 6002；相对 raw WBF 新增 300 个发现、丢失 144 个发现。优化后 Full100 与元统计读取均约 95 ms/完整 warm request。MOT17Det 迁移中部分点指标有提升，但主 MIAP 未改善。

Conclusion: 输出读取应具备足够信息量，优化后的元分类读取仍是具有竞争力的替代方案。特征维度和单一净检测增益并不能可靠指导采用，需要同时考虑误检变化、跨数据集迁移和实际推理成本。

Abstract: A frozen detector computes hypotheses that can support better final outputs through supervised post-processing. Choosing a readout requires knowing which available information improves detection and what its processing costs. We compare candidate scores, class distributions, geometry, native-set summaries and adapted neighborhood meta classification under common candidate, supervision and operating-point conditions. On 320 held-out CrowdHuman images, adding native-set context to a 97-dimensional readout yields 275 more matches and 96 more unmatched predictions. On a separate 319-image cohort, Full100 matches 6004 objects,versus 5848 for source-selected raw WBF and 6002 for a MetaDetect-style adaptation. Its gain over raw contains 300 added and 144 lost discoveries. After execution-equivalent optimization, Full100 and the meta-statistics readout both take approximately 95 ms per complete warm request on a fixed 64-image workload. Source-frozen transfer gives positive point results on 223 MOT17Det frames from seven sequences, while the main MIAP result shows no improvement. The study supports using sufficiently informative output readouts while retaining optimized meta classification as a competitive option. It also shows why feature dimension and net detection gain alone are unreliable guides to adoption.

</details>


### [69] [Freeforward4d: Annotation-free feed-forward 4d reconstruction of driving scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24007)
*Luosong Guo,Xiang Yang,Chaoyang Li,Ao Gao,Jiangming Shi,Yanyun Qu,Zhizhong Zhang,Jingyu Gong,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeForward4D 提出一种仅依赖 RGB 视频的前馈式 4D 驾驶场景重建方法，通过统一视觉先验中的相机、深度和目标轨迹几何，实现无需数据集标注的可渲染自动驾驶仿真场景构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式 4D 重建方法通常依赖数据集提供的相机参数、LiDAR 深度或 3D 框标注，难以应用于未标注驾驶视频；预训练视觉模型虽能仅从 RGB 预测相机、深度和 2D 目标轨迹，但这些预测存在尺度不一致、窗口独立、坐标系统不统一等几何矛盾。

Method: FreeForward4D 将多视角短窗口预测与单目度量深度进行校准，使重叠预测窗口被注册到统一的度量和时间坐标系中；在该几何基础上，跨相机关联 2D 轨迹以形成 3D 目标轨迹，并采用“共享目标速度 + 局部高斯修正”的层级运动模型进行动态物体建模，从而训练和推理仅需 RGB 视频。框架还可通过 RGB-only 微调迁移到 nuScenes 和 Argoverse 2。

Result: 在 Waymo 数据集上，FreeForward4D 达到 27.66 dB PSNR 和 0.836 SSIM，并能恢复度量尺度的相机和深度；在全图 PSNR 上超过依赖数据集标注的 STORM、SLARM-F、UFO、DGGT 和 StreetForward，在 8 秒窗口上获得最佳 RGB 重建保真度。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，只要将预训练视觉模型产生的相机、深度和轨迹先验统一到一致的度量几何中，就可以替代传统数据集标注，实现高效、可渲染、仅基于 RGB 视频的自动驾驶 4D 场景重建，并具备跨数据集适应能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward 4D reconstruction enables efficient creation of renderable driving scenes for autonomous driving simulation. However, most existing methods rely heavily on dataset-provided cameras, LiDAR depth, or 3D box annotations, limiting their applicability to unannotated driving videos. Pretrained visual models can predict cameras, depth, and object tracks from RGB alone, but their geometry is mutually inconsistent: multi-view predictions come in short windows with independent scales and frames, while monocular depth is metric only per image. Our key insight is that, once reconciled into a single metric frame, these priors can replace dataset annotations for feed-forward 4D reconstruction. We present \textbf{FreeForward4D}, which calibrates overlapping prediction windows against monocular metric depth and registers them into one frame across time. On this geometry, 2D tracks are associated across cameras in 3D, and objects move through hierarchical motion that combines a shared object velocity with local Gaussian corrections, so that both training and inference use only RGB videos. On Waymo, FreeForward4D achieves 27.66 dB PSNR and 0.836 SSIM while recovering metric-scale cameras and depth without scale alignment. It surpasses STORM, SLARM-F, UFO, DGGT, and StreetForward, which rely on dataset annotations, in full-image PSNR and attains the best RGB fidelity on 8-second windows. RGB-only fine-tuning further adapts the framework to nuScenes and Argoverse 2. Code will be released.

</details>


### [70] [HHW-Ego: DSLR-Quality Enhancement for Multi-Source Wearable Ego Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24010)
*Yunfei Tong,Qianqian Sun,Jixiang Luo,Dell Zhang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文面向可穿戴第一视角图像画质增强问题，提出了一条模拟可穿戴相机退化的配对数据生成流程、一个两阶段增强框架，并发布了包含高光谱、高动态范围和可穿戴第一视角数据的多源配对数据集HHW-Ego。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴第一视角图像在智能眼镜和具身智能等场景中需求增长，但由于缺乏与真实穿戴拍摄条件匹配的高质量配对参考图像，以及专用增强系统，其画质明显落后于智能手机拍摄图像。

Method: 首先设计定制化退化流程，生成与可穿戴相机图像相匹配的配对训练样本；随后构建两阶段增强框架：第一阶段在配对数据上微调高效模型以提升真实可穿戴图像质量，第二阶段利用高光谱数据校正色温以进一步改善色彩质量；同时构建并发布了HHW-Ego多源配对数据集，涵盖室内外与日夜场景。

Result: 在留出的配对数据上，第一阶段微调能够提升Real-ESRGAN和PFT-SR的PSNR、SSIM、Lab距离和LPIPS指标；在真实可穿戴图像上，通过无参考指标、人类偏好评价、色域分析和视觉对比进一步验证了第二阶段色温校正的作用。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，针对可穿戴第一视角图像设计退化流程和两阶段增强框架能够有效提升画质，尤其是结合高光谱信息进行色温校正有助于改善真实场景下的感知质量。

Abstract: Wearable ego-centric images are now in high demand for scenarios ranging from daily smart glass usage to embodied intelligence. But the image quality is far behind smart phone due to the lacking paired high-quality reference images and the dedicated enhancement systems. To solve this, a customized degradation pipeline is designed to generate paired samples matching wearable camera images, boosting the upper limit of enhancement performance. Besides a two-stage enhancement framework is further built: first, tuning an efficient model on the paired dataset to enhance real wearable images; second, using hyperspectral data to refine color temperature for better quality. Moreover we present HHW-Ego, a multi-source paired dataset integrating with Hyperspectral, High-dynamic range and Wearable Ego-centric data, which includes 5,000 image groups spanning indoor/outdoor and day/night scenes. On held-out paired data, Stage-1 fine-tuning improves PSNR, SSIM, Lab distance, and LPIPS for Real-ESRGAN and PFT-SR. On real wearable images, no-reference metrics, human preference, gamut analysis, and visual comparisons characterize the effects of Stage 2.

</details>


### [71] [FlyVQA: Seeing Video Frame Interpolation Quality through Fly Eyes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24012)
*Baoliang Chen,Lingyu Zhu,Zhiwei Zhong,Shiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究利用冷冻的果蝇视觉神经回路，对帧插值视频进行全参考质量评估，通过量化参考序列与失真序列在细胞类型特异活动和兴奋性/抑制性突触动态上的差异，获得与人类感知评分更一致的视觉质量分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉质量评估的理想目标是复现人类视觉神经计算并实现与人类感知的高度对齐，但人类视觉系统过于复杂，难以直接建模。果蝇视觉连接组规模较小且运动视觉通路特征明确，可作为可行的生物起点，用于评估时间失真对视频感知质量的影响。

Method: 论文构建了一个共享且冻结的果蝇视觉回路，用于对帧插值视频进行全参考质量评估。首先通过复眼采样图将参考视频和失真视频映射到该神经回路中；随后采用考虑连接组结构的读出机制，量化不同细胞类型活动以及兴奋性/抑制性突触动态之间的差异；最后通过参考引导的时间池化和轻量级回归头，将这些差异整合为整体质量分数。

Result: 在多种插值方法的实验中，该方法与人类质量评分的一致性优于参数量相近的CNN、RNN和Transformer基线模型，表明基于果蝇视觉回路的模型能够更有效地捕捉帧插值导致的时间失真对感知质量的影响。

Conclusion: 使用已表征清楚的果蝇视觉运动通路作为计算模型，可以为时间失真相关的视频质量评估提供一种生物学可解释且感知对齐的建模路线。尽管果蝇与人类视觉存在差异，其简洁的神经连接组仍为构建更接近人类感知的视觉质量评估模型提供了有价值的基础。

Abstract: The ultimate ambition of visual quality assessment is to reproduce the neural computations of human vision and thereby achieve full alignment with human perception. However, the immense complexity of human visual circuitry makes this goal difficult to realize. Recently released fly visual connectomes provide a tractable biological starting point. Despite differences between fly and human vision, the fly's well-characterized motion pathways motivate us to investigate their potential for assessing the perceptual impact of temporal distortions. In this paper, we use a shared, frozen fly visual circuit to perform full-reference quality assessment of frame-interpolated videos, whose artifacts can disrupt temporal consistency. Specifically, compound-eye sampling maps reference and distorted sequences into the circuit, while a connectome-aware readout quantifies discrepancies in cell-type-specific activity and excitatory--inhibitory synaptic dynamics. Reference-guided temporal pooling and a lightweight regression head integrate these differences into a quality score. Experiments across multiple interpolation methods demonstrate better agreement with human ratings than CNN, RNN, and Transformer baselines with comparable parameters, supporting a biologically grounded approach to perceptual quality modeling.

</details>


### [72] [Semantic-aware Contrasting with Explicitly Disentangled Representations for AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24038)
*Jiaquan Yu,Jingyi Xie,Zeyu Zhang,Yunhang Qian,Xiaobin Hu,Jiawei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向AI生成视频检测的语义对齐数据集SAVGE，并设计EDSC训练框架，通过显式抑制语义干扰、结合组内对比学习，使检测器更关注生成伪影，从而提升检测鲁棒性与跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成视频检测基准存在根本缺陷：真实视频与虚假视频往往来自不同语义分布，导致检测器可能依赖物体、场景或动作等虚假相关特征，而非生成模型产生的内在伪影，进而影响模型在复杂或新场景下的可靠性。

Method: 作者构建大规模语义对齐数据集SAVGE，包含236.98万个视频，其中42.41万个真实视频、194.57万个生成视频；利用真实视频配对的文本描述作为提示生成对应虚假视频，形成真实—虚假语义组与文本语义锚点。进一步提出与模型无关的EDSC框架：先提取视觉语义特征并与文本表示对齐，再在伪影分支中显式抑制语义信息，并在同一语义组内对真实与虚假样本进行对比学习；通过两阶段优化，融合字幕引导的语义监督与组内对比学习，学习聚焦生成伪影的表示。

Result: 在SAVGE和GenVideo上的大量实验表明，EDSC能够提升多数主干—指标组合的检测性能，增强困难评估设置下的鲁棒性，并改善跨数据集泛化能力。结果说明语义对齐在AI生成视频检测中非常重要，显式分离语义信息与生成伪影有助于训练更鲁棒的检测器。

Conclusion: AIGV检测的关键难点不仅是判别真假，更是避免模型被语义分布偏差误导。SAVGE通过真实—虚假语义配对缓解虚假相关，EDSC通过显式语义抑制与组内对比学习提升伪影建模能力，验证了“语义对齐+语义—伪影解耦”是提升AI生成视频检测器泛化性和鲁棒性的有效策略。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of AI-generated videos (AIGVs) has raised critical concerns about content authenticity. Existing benchmarks suffer from a fundamental limitation: real and fake videos are often collected or generated from different semantic distributions. As a result, detectors may rely on spurious correlations involving objects, scenes, or actions rather than intrinsic artifacts introduced by generative models. To this end, we introduce SAVGE, a large-scale semantically aligned AIGV detection dataset comprising 2,369,822 videos, including 424,115 real videos and 1,945,707 generated videos. SAVGE organizes samples into real-fake semantic groups by using captions paired with real videos as prompts for text-to-video generation, thereby providing both group-level correspondence and textual semantic anchors. Based on SAVGE, we further propose Explicit Disentangling and Semantic-aware Contrasting (EDSC), a model-agnostic training framework for learning artifact-focused representations with reduced semantic interference. EDSC first extracts and aligns visual semantic features with textual representations, and then explicitly suppresses semantic information in the artifact branch while contrasting real and fake samples within each semantic group. By integrating caption-guided semantic supervision with intra-group contrastive learning in a two-phase optimization strategy, EDSC encourages detectors to capture generation-related cues under matched semantic conditions. Extensive experiments on SAVGE and GenVideo demonstrate that EDSC improves most evaluated backbone-metric combinations, enhances robustness under challenging evaluation settings, and strengthens cross-dataset generalization. These results highlight the importance of semantic alignment in AIGV detection and validate explicit semantic-artifact disentanglement as an effective strategy for robust detector learning.

</details>


### [73] [CURVTON-205K: A Mask-Free Difficulty-Stratified Synthetic Dataset for Robust and Scalable Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24042)
*Dipan Bartaula,Anuj Shrestha,Ankit Belbase,Anish Bishwakarma,Saroj Poudel,Samip Ghimire,Shashank Bhatta,Eric Arazo,Binod Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出面向2D虚拟试穿的规模化数据生成流程，构建了包含20.5万三源样本的CURVTON-205K数据集，强调更广泛的外观、姿态、遮挡和视觉条件覆盖，并通过三个难度层级支持可控评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D VTON研究多聚焦架构创新，但在真实场景中对遮挡、非标准姿态、长尾服饰和外观因素的鲁棒性仍不足。作者认为这些问题与数据集设计和长尾数据分布密切相关，因此需要从数据层面增强多样性与难度可控性。

Method: 构建可扩展的合成数据生成管线，联合专用编辑/生成模型与视觉语言模型（VLM）指导，自动生成“人物图像、目标服饰、试穿结果”三元组，无需人体解析掩码或稠密姿态标注；随后整理出CURVTON-205K，并按难度划分为三个层级以支持受控实验。

Result: 对数据集多样性进行分析，并在四种VTON架构上验证其应用潜力，表明该数据可提升试穿保真度并增强跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献在于证明高质量、多样化且难度可控的合成数据对提升2D VTON真实世界鲁棒性具有重要作用，为面向长尾场景的数据驱动建模提供了新路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in 2D virtual try-on (VTON) have largely focused on architectural innovation, yet robust performance in diverse real-world conditions remains challenging. We argue that failure modes in VTON, such as inaccurate occlusion handling, unconstrained poses, and poor coverage of under-represented garments and appearance factors, are tightly coupled to dataset design choices and long-tail data regimes. We present a scalable data generation pipeline that coordinates specialized editing and generation models with vision-language model (VLM) guidance to synthesize triplets without requiring parsing masks or dense-pose annotations. Using this pipeline, we create CURVTON-205K, a curated synthetic dataset of 205K triplets: source person, target garment, and corresponding try-on image. CURVTON-205K is explicitly designed to broaden coverage across garments, person appearance, poses, occlusions, and visual conditions. Additionally, CURVTON-205K is organized into three difficulty tiers, enabling controlled evaluation under varying levels of difficulty and supporting difficulty-aware experimentation. We analyze the dataset diversity and demonstrate its potential to improve try-on fidelity across four VTON architectures and its cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [74] [R$^{3}$Sieve: Suppressing Unconditional Information from Attention to Answers for High-Resolution VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24060)
*Yifan Cao,Yawen Zhang,Xiaocui Yang,Faxian Wan,Shi Feng,Daling Wang,Yifei Zhang,Shangshu Yu,Xianbing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出训练免训练的 R^3Sieve 框架，在高分辨率 VQA 的推理三阶段分别引入反事实参照，抑制无关信息干扰，从而更有效地利用小目标视觉证据，显著提升多种基础模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率 VQA 要求模型在复杂图像中定位与问题相关的小对象并据此选择答案。现有方法主要关注获得更好的视觉证据（如图像裁剪区域），但仅获得证据并不足以解决问题，关键在于推理过程中如何有效利用证据。作者认为，小目标证据常被“无条件信息”遮蔽：即使原本应依赖的条件被移除，这些信息仍保留在后续表示中，从而干扰细粒度感知与答案生成。

Method: 作者提出无需训练的 R^3Sieve（Residual-Region-Response Sieve），将推理过程划分为定位、裁剪和回答三个阶段，并在每个阶段引入不同的反事实参照来抑制干扰：1）定位阶段使用 Shared-Subspace Canceller（SSC），通过通用图像描述构造反事实参照，得到与问题相关的注意力图；2）裁剪阶段使用 Unique-Contribution Spotter（UCS），从视觉特征和非视觉特征构造反事实参照，识别独特且互补的裁剪区域；3）回答阶段使用 Content-Aligned Voter（CAV），构造无图像分支以校准答案响应。

Result: 在三个高分辨率 VQA 基准上的实验表明，R^3Sieve 在多种设置下均取得对比方法中的最高总体准确率，并可提升基础模型最高达 21.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: R^3Sieve 表明，在高分辨率 VQA 中，除了获取高质量视觉证据外，在推理全过程中通过反事实参照抑制无关信息、提升小目标证据利用效率同样关键。该方法无需训练，可作为通用推理增强框架显著提升多模态大模型在细粒度视觉问答任务上的性能。

Abstract: High-resolution visual question answering (VQA) requires models to identify question-relevant small objects in high-resolution images and select the correct answer based on what they recognize, demanding fine-grained perception. Previous methods focus on obtaining better visual evidence (image crops), but obtaining it alone is insufficient, using it effectively is equally crucial. Visual evidence should therefore be utilized as effectively as possible throughout inference. We argue that small-target evidence is obscured by unconditional information that remains observable in subsequent processing even after the condition on which it should depend is removed. Such information hinders the effective use of small-target evidence. We propose R$^{3}$Sieve (Residual-Region-Response Sieve), a training-free framework. We divide inference into three stages---localization, cropping, and answering---and introduce distinct counterfactual references to suppress interference at each stage. During localization, the Shared-Subspace Canceller (SSC) uses a generic image description to construct a counterfactual reference and obtain question-relevant attention maps. During cropping, the Unique-Contribution Spotter (UCS) constructs counterfactual references from visual and non-visual features to identify distinctive, complementary crop regions. During answering, the Content-Aligned Voter (CAV) constructs an image-free branch to calibrate answer responses. Experiments on three high-resolution VQA benchmarks show that R$^{3}$Sieve achieves the highest overall accuracy among compared methods across diverse settings, improving base models by up to 21.5 percentage points.

</details>


### [75] [AeroRSI: Recursive Self-Improvement for Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24077)
*Shiwei Wu,Yuwen Pan,Zhi Li,Yaowen Li,Yan Zhou,Xuemiao Xu,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AeroRSI，一种用于无人机视觉-语言-动作模型闭环飞行的递归自我改进框架，通过让当前策略在失败后的状态上继续飞行来筛选和验证“修复动作”，从而将自身失败经验转化为可靠监督信号，显著提升语言导航成功率并减少碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型可使无人机根据自然语言指令导航，但在闭环飞行中仍经常失败。修复这些失败需要在策略实际到达的状态上学习避免失败的动作，但人类飞行员难以大规模标注修复动作，基于规则的规划器也无法稳定提供可靠监督。因此需要一种无需额外人工标注、能从失败中自动学习的改进机制。

Method: 提出 AeroRSI 递归自我改进框架。核心思想是：由失败策略自己通过“继续飞行”来判断候选修复动作是否有效，而不是直接模仿策略或规则产生的动作。每一轮中，系统锚定当前策略的真实失败状态，验证候选修复后的延续表现，并将验证有效的修复知识蒸馏回策略中，同时尽量保持其他状态下的已有行为不变。由于 episode 在首个失败处终止，后续失败只有在早期失败被修复后才暴露，因此监督信号在多轮迭代中不断更新。

Result: 在 TravelUAV 基准上，对一个 2B 参数监督预训练策略进行三轮迭代，成功率从 46.4% 提升到 67.5%，碰撞次数降低一半。相比同一初始化的之前最先进方法，成功率提高 10.6 个百分点，且不需要额外人工标注，也不增加推理成本。

Conclusion: AeroRSI 表明，VLA 策略可以在闭环无人机导航中通过自我验证的递归学习从自身失败中有效改进。其“飞行后判断修复”机制使原本不可靠的动作提案变为可靠监督来源，从而在不增加推理成本的情况下显著提升语言条件导航成功率和安全性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to follow natural-language instructions, yet still fail frequently in closed-loop flight. Correcting these failures requires repairs, i.e., actions that avoid them at the states the policy itself reaches, which human pilots cannot label at scale and rule-based planners cannot supply reliably. In this paper, we present AeroRSI, a recursive self-improvement framework in which an aerial navigation policy iteratively learns from its own failures. Our key insight is that a failing policy should judge its own repairs by flying on from them. Judged in this way, its sampled actions and even rule-based proposals that hurt it when imitated directly become reliable supervision. Moreover, an episode terminates at its first failure, concealing subsequent failures until the earlier ones are repaired. AeroRSI thus renews its supervision in every round, anchoring the failures of the current policy, verifying repairs by its continuation, and distilling them while keeping its behavior elsewhere. On TravelUAV, three rounds raise the success rate of a supervised 2B-parameter policy from 46.4% to 67.5% and reduce collisions by half, surpassing the previous state of the art by 10.6 points from the same initialization without extra human labels or inference cost. Code and models are available at this https URL.

</details>


### [76] [APS: Adaptive Partitioning-Based Frame Selection for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24079)
*Junxiang Wen,Yinxi Zhao,Jiawei Chen,Jingying Hu,Lijun Wang,Zhen Qin,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 APS：一种免训练的自适应时序划分帧选择框架，通过复杂度正则化拟合与动态规划确定分段，并据此进行查询相关性感知的帧预算分配，以提升长视频理解效率与效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在长视频理解中面临视觉输入计算开销过大的问题。帧选择是高效方案，但许多现有基于区域的方法依赖固定或预定义的时序划分，容易产生次优的时序区域，进而影响后续预算分配和帧选择质量。

Method: 提出 Adaptive Partitioning-Based Frame Selection（APS）。APS 首先将时序划分建模为复杂度正则化的拟合问题，联合优化划分边界和局部模型，并使用动态规划求解；随后利用自适应划分得到的局部时序区域，根据查询相关性和区域时间跨度指导帧预算分配，从而在有限视觉预算下选择信息量更高的帧。

Result: 在 Video-MME、MLVU 和 LongVideoBench 上进行大量实验。APS 在多种 LVLM 主干上均稳定提升性能，并在与竞争方法相比时取得了相对均匀采样最高的平均增益。进一步分析验证了自适应划分的有效性及其跨模型规模、相似度编码器和更先进 LVLM 的泛化能力。

Conclusion: APS 表明，相较于固定时序划分，根据查询-帧相关性的局部变化自适应地划分视频时序，并据此进行预算化帧选择，能够在不训练模型的前提下显著提升长视频理解性能与效率。

Abstract: Recent advances in large vision-language models (LVLMs) have significantly improved video understanding capabilities, yet processing long videos remains challenging due to the substantial computational cost of extensive visual inputs. Frame selection offers an efficient alternative, but many existing region-based methods rely on fixed or predefined temporal partitions, which may result in suboptimal temporal regions, compromising subsequent budget allocation and frame selection. In this work, we propose Adaptive Partitioning-Based Frame Selection (APS), a training-free framework that adapts temporal partitions to local variations in query-frame relevance. Specifically, APS formulates temporal partitioning as a complexity-regularized fitting problem, jointly optimizing partition boundaries and local models through dynamic programming. The resulting adaptive temporal partitions guide frame budget allocation based on query relevance and temporal extent, enabling informative frame selection under limited visual budgets. Extensive experiments on Video-MME, MLVU, and LongVideoBench demonstrate that APS consistently improves performance across diverse LVLM backbones, achieving the highest average gains over uniform sampling among competing methods. Further analyses validate the effectiveness of adaptive partitioning and demonstrate its generalizability across model scales, similarity encoders, and advanced LVLMs.

</details>


### [77] [CausalDrive: Causal Decomposition for Data-Efficient End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24080)
*Xiaobao Wei,Jingxian Wu,Lirong Yang,Qing Han,Yuan Gao,Ziwei Yang,Shuai Yang,Weiwei Liu,Chang Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出CausalDrive，一种面向端到端自动驾驶策略学习的数据高效因果分解框架，通过将“名义意图”与“驾驶风格”解耦，并利用反事实名义轨迹和扩散风格执行策略分别学习，从而提升专家演示数据的利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在端到端自动驾驶中，直接模仿专家轨迹会将“车辆应该去哪里”的名义意图与“如何执行该意图”的多模态驾驶风格耦合在一起。不同意图和风格的组合可能产生相似的专家轨迹，导致因果贡献被混淆，策略需要从单一纠缠目标中同时学习两者，限制了其对专家演示数据的利用效率。

Method: CausalDrive将策略学习分解为两部分：首先，基于导航命令、地图拓扑和自车状态构造反事实名义轨迹，去除驾驶风格影响，为Nominal Intention Planner提供独立于专家轨迹的监督；其次，在给定预测名义意图和周围交通参与者信息后，采用基于扩散模型的Style Execution Policy，对名义轨迹与专家轨迹之间的残差进行建模，以表达多模态驾驶风格。通过这种因果结构化的监督方式，减少常规场景中重复学习共同名义行为，将专家监督集中于更有信息量的执行差异。

Result: CausalDrive在NAVSIM v1上取得91.2 PDMS，在NAVSIM v2上取得89.5 EPDMS，达到或超越当前最先进性能。进一步实验表明，该方法在有限专家数据下具有较强表现，并在复杂交互场景中展现明显优势。

Conclusion: 将自动驾驶策略中的名义意图与驾驶风格进行因果解耦，可以提高专家演示数据的利用效率。CausalDrive通过反事实名义轨迹监督和扩散式风格执行建模，实现了更高效、更鲁棒的端到端驾驶策略学习，为数据受限场景和复杂交互场景提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient use of expert demonstrations is essential for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, directly imitating expert trajectories couples nominal intention, which specifies where the vehicle should go, with multimodal driving styles, which determine how that intention is executed. Different combinations of these factors can yield similar expert trajectories, obscuring their respective causal contributions and forcing the policy to learn both from a single entangled target. This ambiguity limits how effectively the policy can exploit expert demonstrations. We propose CausalDrive, a causal decomposition framework for data-efficient driving policy learning that separates deterministic nominal intention prediction from probabilistic driving style modeling. Specifically, we construct counterfactual nominal trajectories from navigation command, map topology, and ego state by removing style dependent effects, providing a Nominal Intention Planner with supervision independent of expert trajectories. Conditioned on the predicted nominal intention and surrounding agents, a diffusion-based Style Execution Policy models diverse driving styles as a multimodal distribution over residuals between nominal and expert trajectories. This causally structured supervision reduces redundant learning of shared nominal behavior in routine scenarios, directing expert supervision toward informative execution variations and improving data utilization. CausalDrive achieves 91.2 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 89.5 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, matching or surpassing state-of-the-art performance. Further experiments demonstrate strong performance with limited expert data and clear advantages in challenging interactive scenarios.

</details>


### [78] [From Restoration to Redrawing: Revitalizing Ancient Chinese Characters by SVG Code Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24084)
*Weiguang Zhang,Linhua Dong,Bin Dong,Shanshan Jiang,Maizhen Ning,Xiaoqiang Kang,Kun Zhang,Hao Xu,Xiaowei Huang,Wei Wang,Kaizhu Huang,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReDraw，一种两阶段视觉语言模型，将汉代简牍残损文字的修复重构为SVG代码生成与编辑，先忠实保留可见笔画，再通过骨架引导补全严重缺失笔画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到图像修复方法可能改变已观测到的笔画，且对笔画几何形状控制有限；作者受专家手工描红实践启发，希望实现更忠实、可控、可编辑的古文字修复。

Method: ReDraw包含两个阶段：第一阶段对可观测笔画进行忠实向量化，或对轻度退化笔画进行保守描摹；第二阶段在骨架链式思维推理和结构感知强化学习奖励引导下，先预测骨架再生成轮廓，以追加路径的方式补全严重不完整笔画，同时保持已有SVG路径不变。为支持训练，作者构建了分离墨迹、材质和退化因素的多层数据合成管线，生成120万样本，并从320个标注真实字形中导出最多6400对适配样本。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在合成数据上展现出良好的修复效果，并能迁移适配到真实汉代简牍字形；代码和演示将在论文接收后开放。

Conclusion: ReDraw通过SVG代码化生成与编辑，为残损简牍文字修复提供了一种更可控、更保真且符合专家描红流程的新范式，具有文化遗产数字化与古文字研究应用价值。

Abstract: Hanjian manuscripts written on Han Dynasty bamboo and wooden slips are invaluable heritage of ancient Chinese writing but suffer from damaged ink and incomplete strokes. Existing image-to-image restoration methods may alter observed strokes and offer limited control over stroke geometry. Inspired by experts' manual redrawing practices, we address these issues by introducing \textbf{ReDraw}, a two-stage vision-language model(VLM) that reformulates character restoration as Scalable Vector Graphics(SVG) code generation and editing. Stage one targets faithful vectorization of observed strokes geometry or conservative tracing of mildly degraded strokes. Guided by skeleton chain-of-thought reasoning and reinforcement learning with structure-aware rewards, stage two predicts skeletons before contours to redraw severely incomplete strokes by appending paths while keeping existing SVG paths unchanged. To support training, we develop a multi-layer data synthesis pipeline that disentangles ink, material, and degradation to generate 1.2M samples, and derive up to 6,400 adaptation pairs from 320 annotated real glyphs. Experiments demonstrate synthetic restoration performance and adaptability to real Hanjian glyphs. Code and demo will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [79] [Object Pose: The Link between Weak Perspective, Paraperspective, and Full Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24095)
*Radu Horaud,Fadi Dornaika,Bart Lamiroy,Stephane Christy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文从代数角度推导了 DeMenthon 和 Davis 提出的基于 3D–2D 点对应的位姿估计方法，指出其可归入以弱透视（零阶）或准透视（一阶）近似透视相机的更广泛方法族，并重点提出一种迭代准透视位姿计算方法，分析其收敛性、正交约束处理、标定误差敏感性，并与非线性优化方法进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DeMenthon 和 Davis 方法通过迭代改进弱透视模型得到的位姿来逼近透视模型位姿，但缺乏清晰的代数解释。作者希望揭示该方法所属的近似框架，并比较零阶弱透视与一阶准透视近似的收敛性和标定鲁棒性，以获得更稳健、可分析的物体位姿估计方法。

Method: 论文首先对 DeMenthon 和 Davis 的迭代方法给出代数推导，证明其属于透视相机近似的更广义方法类：一类用零阶近似即弱透视，另一类用一阶近似即准透视。随后提出迭代准透视位姿计算方法，分别适用于非共面和共面物点；同时分析迭代方法的收敛性。作者还引入简单方式纳入旋转矩阵的正交约束，并考察相机标定误差对结果的影响，据此定义对标定不精确情形最优的实验设置。最后将该方法与直接非线性优化方法进行比较。

Result: 迭代准透视方法在收敛性方面优于迭代弱透视方法；论文给出适用于非共面和共面对象点的详细迭代准透视计算方法，并提出处理旋转矩阵正交约束的简化方式。对标定误差的分析给出了针对不精确相机标定的最优实验设置；与直接非线性优化方法的比较显示迭代准透视方法具有相应有效性和实用价值。

Conclusion: DeMenthon 和 Davis 方法可被理解为透视相机近似框架中的迭代方法，而一阶准透视近似比零阶弱透视近似具有更好的收敛性质。迭代准透视位姿估计方法结合正交约束处理和标定敏感性分析，为从 3D–2D 点对应估计物体位姿提供了一种收敛更稳健、实现较简单且可与非线性优化相媲美或互补的方法。

Abstract: Recently, DeMenthon and Davis [4,5] proposed a method for determining the pose of a 3-D object with respect to a camera from 3-D to 2-D point correspondences. The method consists of iteratively improving the pose computed with a weak perspective camera model to converge, at the limit, to a pose estimation computed with a perspective camera model. In this paper we give an algebraic derivation of DeMenthon and Davis' method and we show that it belongs to a larger class of methods where the perspective camera model is approximated either at zero order (weak perspective) or first order (paraperspective). We describe in detail an iterative paraperspective pose computation method for both non coplanar and coplanar object points. We analyse the convergence of these methods and we conclude that the iterative paraperspective method (proposed in this paper) has better convergence properties than the iterative weak perspective method. We introduce a simple way of taking into account the orthogonality constraint associated with the rotation matrix. We analyse the sensitivity to camera calibration errors and we define the optimal experimental setup with respect to imprecise camera calibration. We compare the results obtained with this method and with a non-linear optimization method.

</details>


### [80] [RoughCut-Bench: A Benchmark for Automatic Video Rough-Cut with Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24098)
*Ruisi Zhao,You Li,Sihan Chen,Aosong Cheng,Chixiang Ma,Sen Pei,Zhen Yang,Hehe Fan,Dongliang He,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 RoughCut-Bench 视频粗剪评测基准，覆盖视觉驱动与语音驱动两类实用场景，并设计 agent 工作流引导大型视觉语言模型生成结构化剪辑方案。实验表明当前 LVLM 仍难以稳定产出与专家粗剪高度一致的可执行剪辑计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频粗剪是媒体生产流程中的关键环节，需要把原始素材整理成初步成片。尽管大型视觉语言模型在视频理解任务上取得进展，但自动粗剪仍缺乏系统评测基准、可执行剪辑方案生成流程和成熟工作流，限制了相关研究与实际应用。

Method: 作者构建 RoughCut-Bench，包含两类任务：一是视频案例驱动的视觉设置，涵盖精彩片段检测与选择、源视频排序以及在有用户需求时的要求遵循；二是 ASR 转录文本驱动的语音设置，涵盖口头语删除、表达去重和低价值内容修剪。基准包含 101 个视觉驱动案例、831 段原始视频和 1,476 条精彩片段标注，以及 84 个语音驱动案例，每个案例为带清理片段标注的 ASR 转录。评估通过将模型生成剪辑方案与人工标注比较，并引入与人类偏好对齐的 agent evaluator 评估渲染成片质量。此外提出 agent 工作流，引导 LVLM 从输入素材生成结构化剪辑方案。

Result: 实验显示，现有 LVLM 在自动粗剪任务上仍能力有限，难以生成与专家判断高度一致且可直接执行的剪辑方案，说明自动粗剪仍存在显著挑战。

Conclusion: RoughCut-Bench 为视频粗剪提供了面向实际工作流的系统评测基准，揭示了当前 LVLM 在自动粗剪中的不足，并为后续基于视觉、语音和用户需求的 agent 化剪辑研究提供了基础资源。

Abstract: Video rough-cut is a core stage in media production workflows, organizing raw media materials into an initial video cut. Despite recent advances in large vision-language models (LVLMs) for video-related tasks, automatic rough-cut remains underexplored, without a comprehensive benchmark for evaluation or mature workflows for producing executable cut plans. To address the evaluation gap, we introduce RoughCut-Bench, which covers two practical settings: (1) a visually driven setting over video cases, covering highlight detection and selection, source-video ordering, and adherence to user requirements when provided; and (2) a speech-driven setting over automatic speech recognition (ASR) transcripts, covering filler removal, expression deduplication, and low-value content trimming. Across these settings, the benchmark contains 101 visually driven cases, including 831 raw videos with 1,476 highlight annotations. It also includes 84 speech-driven cases, each consisting of an ASR transcript with annotated cleanup spans. The evaluation compares cut plans with these annotations in both settings and additionally uses a human-aligned agentic evaluator to assess rendered-cut quality in the visual setting. To apply LVLMs to automatic rough-cut, we further propose agentic workflows that guide them to produce structured cut plans from input materials. Experiments show that current LVLMs remain limited in producing expert-aligned cut plans. Our benchmark, code, and prompts are released at this https URL.

</details>


### [81] [IDEA: Intent-Dominance Evidence Audit for Inference-Time Backdoor Detection in GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24102)
*Zhizhuo Kou,Sizai Hou,Jian Yang,Ge Sun,Junyu Luo,Wenkai Zhang,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出IDEA，一种面向GUI智能体逐步动作决策的推理时后门检测框架，通过意图反事实探测和区域因果审计识别屏幕触发器导致的行为劫持，并借助门控保形评分在控制误报率的同时定位可疑区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下载式开放权重GUI智能体面临供应链后门风险：模型在干净数据测试中正常，仅在特定视觉触发出现时异常行动。现有样本级后门检测主要面向图像分类器或CLIP式编码器，难以检测GUI智能体中逐步、依赖视觉区域和指令交互的step-level后门行为。

Method: 作者将后门激活特征定义为“意图—证据支配反转”：正常情况下用户指令主导动作决策，而后门触发时未被指令引用的屏幕区域劫持决策状态。IDEA在推理时逐步骤进行审计，不需要知道触发器或攻击数据。其核心包括：Intent Counterfactual Probing，测量改写或重定向指令后决策状态的偏移，以判断指令是否仍控制决策；Region Causal Audit，量化移除候选屏幕区域后决策状态的偏移，以识别支配性视觉证据；门控保形评分，将两类偏移与相同干净流形距离下的正常步骤比较，从而提供有限样本下的误报率保证并输出告警。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-3B上针对五类攻击实验表明，IDEA能够在受控误报率下稳定检测后门激活，并优于现有最先进检测方法；同时可在每次告警中定位触发区域，且仅带来较小的推理开销。

Conclusion: IDEA为开放权重GUI智能体提供了一种无需攻击先验的推理时step-level后门检测与定位方法，有助于降低未经验证模型权重带来的供应链安全风险。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly deploy downloaded open-weight GUI agents. A backdoored agent passes acceptance tests on clean data and misbehaves only when a visual trigger appears on screen, turning an unvetted checkpoint into a supply-chain risk. However, existing sample-level detectors target image classifiers and CLIP-style encoders, leaving step-level backdoor detection for GUI agents unaddressed. On clean steps, the instruction drives the action; when an unreferenced screen region hijacks the step, the instruction loses control over the decision state or that region dictates it instead. We term this signature intent--evidence dominance inversion and propose IDEA (Intent-Dominance Evidence Audit) to detect backdoor activation at inference time for each step without trigger knowledge or attack data. Specifically, Intent Counterfactual Probing (ICP) measures how far redirected instructions move the decision state, and Region Causal Audit (RCA) quantifies how far ablating a candidate region moves it. A gated conformal score compares both displacements against clean steps at the same distance from the clean manifold, providing a finite-sample false-positive guarantee. Extensive experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-3B across five attack families show that IDEA consistently detects backdoor activation under a controlled false-positive rate and outperforms state-of-the-art detection methods, while localizing the trigger region for every alarm and adding only modest inference overhead.

</details>


### [82] [PatchAdaNet: Unsupervised class incremental learning using local patches and global adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24137)
*Kamel Aouaidjia,Yanchao Duan,Zihui Zhen,Xin Li,Yongfei Lv,Chongsheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 PatchAdaNet，一种无需保存历史数据的无监督类增量学习方法，通过冻结的 patch 自编码器全局特征、可学习适配器、全局特征质心和 EWC 来缓解灾难性遗忘并稳定增量特征表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物学习系统能够利用早期形成且相对稳定的通用表征进行增量学习，而现有回放方法需要随任务增长的内存缓冲，并可能保留任务特定噪声，从而损害泛化。因此，需要一种不依赖大量历史样本存储的类增量学习框架，以在持续更新知识的同时减少表征漂移和灾难性遗忘。

Method: 作者首先利用图像共享的结构特征，将每张图像划分为 patch，并在外部数据上独立训练各 patch 的自编码器。随后，将所有 patch 编码器的各层参数聚合为全局冻结层，作为跨任务稳定的通用特征提取器。在冻结特征之上，引入可学习适配器来构建全局图像表示并重建完整图像。为在不存储历史数据的情况下防止特征漂移，提出全局特征质心 GFC，即适配器输出特征的固定大小滑动平均，并在每个任务后更新，用于惩罚表征漂移。同时，对可学习层应用弹性权重巩固 EWC，以约束重要权重并保留已学习特征。最终，在冻结潜表示和 GFC 上训练线性分类器进行类增量评估。

Result: 论文在 CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、miniImageNet 和 5-Datasets 上评估了 PatchAdaNet，并在 CNN 和 Vision Transformer 两种架构下进行类增量学习设置比较。摘要未报告具体数值结果，但表明该方法用于验证不同架构下无数据重放的类增量特征学习性能，并提供了可复现代码。

Conclusion: PatchAdaNet 通过外部预训练的冻结 patch 编码器、可学习适配器、GFC 和 EWC 构建了一条不依赖历史样本存储的无监督类增量学习路径，可在全局稳定特征上持续整合新类别信息，并抑制表征漂移和灾难性遗忘。该研究为 CNN 与 ViT 架构下的类增量图像分类提供了统一比较框架，并开源了代码。

Abstract: Biological learning systems acquire knowledge incrementally through broad, reusable representations established early and kept largely stable, while integrating new experience with minimal interference with previously learned knowledge. Replay-based methods approximate this by storing samples or features from previous tasks and mixing them into the current task. However, memory buffers grow with the number of tasks and may retain task-specific noise that reduces generalization. To address these limitations, we propose PatchAdaNet (Patch Adapter Network), an unsupervised class-incremental learning method for image classification that continually updates feature representations using autoencoders. Since images share structural features such as edges, corners, and shapes, we first divide each image into patches and train independent autoencoders on external data to reconstruct each patch. The layers of all patch encoders are aggregated into global frozen layers that generalize across tasks and remain fixed throughout learning. Learnable adapters then build global image representations from these features to reconstruct the entire image. To prevent feature drift without storing data, we introduce the Global Feature Centroid (GFC), a fixed-size running average of adapter outputs updated after each task to penalizes representation drift. Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) is applied to the learnable layers to preserve previously learned features by constraining important weights. Finally, a linear classifier is trained on the frozen latent representation and GFC for class-incremental evaluation. We evaluate PatchAdaNet in both CNN and Vision Transformer settings on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, miniImageNet and 5-Datasets, providing a comparative study of the two architectures under identical class-incremental settings. Code is available.

</details>


### [83] [Inherited Adapter Experts for Replay-Free Domain-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24145)
*Gyutae Oh,Hyunmin Kong,Jitae Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Lamarck，一种免回放的域增量学习方法：冻结预训练 ViT，为每个新域训练小型专家和分类头，并用训练无关的 Mahalanobis 路由器选择专家；其核心贡献是认识到性能瓶颈在专家而非路由器，并通过深层适配器与前序专家初始化来增强专家，从而在 CDDB-Hard 等基准上取得更高平均精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在免回放域增量学习中，模型连续学习相同类别在不同域中的分布，但不能保留旧域图像，且测试时没有域标签，因此需要通过路由器从多个专家中选择一个专家进行预测。已有工作尝试训练路由器，但作者发现一种训练无关的 Mahalanobis 路由器相对于“始终选择图像所属真实域专家”的 oracle 选择仅损失不到 2.7 个最终准确率点，说明路由器并非主要瓶颈。真正限制性能的是每个域专家本身的表示和判别能力，以及在无域标签条件下误路由到后续专家所带来的影响。因此需要强化专家并降低误路由损失。

Method: Lamarck 采用“冻结骨干 + 每域专家 + 训练无关路由器”的框架。首先保留预训练 ViT 作为冻结主干，每个新域只训练少量参数和该域的分类头，形成一个域专家。测试阶段使用 Mahalanobis 路由器根据特征分布为每个样本选择专家。为提升专家质量，该方法在每个深层 ViT 块之后引入小型可训练适配器，对所有图像 token 进行修正，从而增强跨域特征适应能力。此外，由于不同域共享相同类别，新专家直接从其前一个专家初始化，不增加额外训练成本，同时使样本即使被误路由到后续专家，也能降低性能损失。整个过程不存储旧域图像，因此属于免回放域增量学习。

Result: 实验表明，训练无关的 Mahalanobis 路由器相对于 oracle 域专家选择仅造成小于 2.7 点的最终准确率损失。Lamarck 的深层适配器在深伪检测基准 CDDB-Hard 上带来 4.4 点的最终准确率提升。与十二个冻结骨干基线在同一协议下比较，Lamarck 在 CDDB-Hard 上以 5.2 点优势排名第一，在 DomainNet 和 OfficeHome 上排名第二，并取得最高平均准确率。

Conclusion: 免回放域增量学习中的性能瓶颈不一定来自路由器本身，而更可能来自域专家的能力和误路由鲁棒性。Lamarck 通过低成本的 Mahalanobis 路由、逐 ViT 块适配器修正和从前一专家初始化的策略，在保持预训练 ViT 冻结、不保存历史图像的条件下显著增强专家，提升了多个域增量基准上的表现，尤其在 CDDB-Hard 深伪检测任务上优势明显。

Abstract: Replay-free domain-incremental learning trains a model on the same classes in successive domains without keeping earlier images. Per-domain learners freeze a pre-trained ViT and train an additional expert for each new domain: the small set of parameters and the classification head learned on that domain. Test images carry no domain label, so a router selects one expert for each image. Recent work trains this router. However, a training-free Mahalanobis router costs the analyzed learners under 2.7 points of final accuracy relative to always selecting each image's own-domain expert. The experts, not this router, limit accuracy. Our learner, Lamarck, therefore uses this router and strengthens the experts. Its adapters, small trainable layers that correct every image token after each deep ViT block, add 4.4 points of final accuracy on the deepfake benchmark CDDB-Hard. In addition, each expert is initialized from its predecessor at no extra cost: the domains share their classes, so an image misrouted to a later expert loses less. Against twelve frozen-backbone baselines under one protocol, Lamarck is first on CDDB-Hard by 5.2 points, second on DomainNet and OfficeHome, and highest in mean accuracy.

</details>


### [84] [SEER: Semantic Evidence for Executable Region Propagation in Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24152)
*Junyang Chen,Xiao Li,Yiguo Qiao,Wenbin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEER是一种免训练的测试时修复层，用于在OVSS预测后基于视觉证据、词表合法性和分数可执行性，对局部错误进行修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练开放词汇语义分割方法仍常出现局部、结构化且与候选标签空间相关的错误，需要一种无需更新模型权重、同时兼顾视觉合理性、词表合法性和分割分数可执行性的修复机制。

Method: SEER将修复建模为证据条件标签传播：由OVSS分数空间生成紧凑候选标签，冻结VLM验证区域视觉证据，执行门控判断该证据是否合法且可执行；接受标签后，根据置信区域或边界风险采用不同传播策略。

Result: 在八个标准OVSS基准上，SEER在不更新OVSS权重情况下达到58.0平均mIoU；匹配输入对照实验验证了修复机制的独立贡献，并报告了完整冻结系统的资源开销。

Conclusion: SEER提供了一种训练时OVSS后处理修复框架，可基于冻结视觉语言模型验证和可执行区域传播提升分割精度，同时明确评估其额外推理成本。

Abstract: Training-free open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) has made strong progress by adapting CLIP-like representations to dense prediction, but its remaining errors are often local, structured, and tied to the active label space. A successful correction must be visually plausible, legal under the target vocabulary, and executable in the segmentation score field. We propose SEER, a training-free test-time repair layer for Semantic Evidence and Executable Region propagation. SEER casts post-prediction OVSS repair as evidence-conditioned label propagation. The OVSS score space proposes compact candidate labels, a frozen VLM verifies closed-set regional evidence, and an execution gate certifies whether the evidence is legally and semantically actionable. The accepted label is then propagated by an OVSS-side policy: coherent high-confidence regions allow broad propagation, while object- or boundary-risk cases use conservative score-grounded propagation. Across eight standard OVSS benchmarks, SEER reaches 58.0 average mIoU without updating OVSS weights. Matched-input controls isolate the repair mechanism, while resource measurements report the cost of the complete frozen system.

</details>


### [85] [Game2Player: Scaling Gameplay Experience for Visual Agents through Environment-Player Co-Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24154)
*Xirui Jiang,Yuxi Chen,Rui Yang,Feng Luo,Pengcheng Wang,Bingxuan Li,Zihao Zhao,Yushan Li,Han Wang,Manling Li,Yang Zhang,Shiyu Chang,Tong Zhang,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出Game2Player：通过让Agent自动改造Web游戏、定义可验证任务，并用Coding/VLM Player试玩收集经验，形成游戏环境与玩家共同发展的数据闭环；基于307小时、15.7K轨迹训练出Game2Player-9B，显著提升游戏任务进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏视觉智能体研究依赖人工采集游戏操作或仅从视频推断缺失动作，成本高、规模有限；同时数据集游戏覆盖窄，常缺少显式任务定义和可验证结果，难以为长程规划、精确控制和大规模训练提供可靠经验数据。

Method: 提出Game2Player框架：Builder Agent改造现有Web游戏并暴露内部状态，定义基于状态进展度量和成功检查的任务；Coding Player与VLM Player执行任务并向Builder反馈问题以修复环境，使试玩同时用于环境验证和数据采集；进一步采用progress-weighted SFT训练VLM Agent，对推进任务进展的步骤赋予更高权重。

Result: 构建568个经自动验证的Web游戏、3,436个任务，收集15.7K条轨迹、约307小时游戏经验，并留出50个游戏作为PlayerBench基准。训练的Game2Player-9B在PlayerBench上将任务进展从14.3%提升至31.2%，在外部GameWorld基准上从30.0%提升至53.1%。

Conclusion: Game2Player展示了一条可扩展视觉智能体游戏经验学习的可行路径：通过自动构建、验证和利用已有游戏世界，生成多样化、可验证、带任务约束的大规模交互数据，并证明这些数据能有效提升VLM Agent在真实游戏任务中的规划与控制能力。

Abstract: Games are a rich testbed for visual agents, demanding perception, long-horizon planning, and precise control. However, collecting human gameplay is costly, and learning from gameplay videos requires inferring missing actions. Existing datasets also cover a limited range of games, often without explicit tasks or verifiable outcomes. To address these gaps, we introduce Game2Player, a framework that co-develops game environments and players to collect diverse and verifiable gameplay experience at scale. In the Game2Player loop, a Builder agent adapts existing web games, exposes their internal state, and defines tasks with state-based progress measures and success checks. A Coding Player and a VLM Player then attempt the tasks and report problems to the Builder for repair, so that play serves as both environment validation and training data collection. With Game2Player, we build 568 verified web games with 3,436 tasks, collect 15.7K trajectories (307 hours of gameplay), and hold out 50 games as the benchmark PlayerBench. To evaluate the collected experience as training data, we train VLM agents with progress-weighted SFT, assigning greater weight to steps that advance task progress. Game2Player-9B improves progress from 14.3% to 31.2% on PlayerBench and from 30.0% to 53.1% on the external GameWorld benchmark. More broadly, our work suggests a path for visual agents to scale their gameplay experience by building, verifying, and learning from existing game worlds.

</details>


### [86] [GenRF: Generative Registration-free Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24157)
*Linfeng Tang,Zhijun Fan,Yang Chen,Zizhuo Li,Hao Zhang,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenRF利用预训练生成基础模型FLUX和LoRA微调，实现无需显式配准的退化/错位多模态图像融合，能联合修复退化、补全缺失区域并聚合跨模态互补信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实多模态图像常因传感器原理和配置差异出现几何错位、视场不一致以及噪声/模糊等退化，导致配准和融合困难。传统方法依赖显式配准，易受大视差和缺失对应区域影响；独立修复又难以利用跨模态互补信息。

Method: 提出GenRF，在生成基础模型时代重新审视错位退化融合问题：通过LoRA适配预训练FLUX，利用cross-attention进行位置无关的跨模态信息聚合，无需估计形变场即可联合完成退化修复、缺失区域推断和互补信息融合。

Result: 在真实红外与可见光图像实验中，GenRF可直接处理未配准、退化的输入并生成高质量融合结果，提升下游任务在错位情况下的性能；同时无需特定任务微调即可泛化到错位的多曝光和多焦点图像融合。

Conclusion: 生成基础模型提供的先验与cross-attention机制可显著降低对显式配准和传统修复流程的依赖，为真实场景下复杂错位、退化和视场缺失的多模态融合提供统一且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-modal image fusion combines complementary observations to improve scene visibility and perception. However, in real-world acquisition, differences in sensing principles and configurations introduce geometric discrepancies, while degradations further compromise source quality. Existing methods typically rely on explicit registration, yet large parallax complicates correspondence estimation, while mismatched fields of view leave some regions without cross-modal counterparts. Meanwhile, independent restoration underuses cross-modal information, whereas restoration-fusion methods assuming alignment remain vulnerable to misregistration. This study revisits misaligned and degraded image fusion in the era of generative foundation models, in which cross-attention enables position-agnostic complementary information aggregation, while generative priors support degradation recovery and inference in missing regions. Building on this perspective, we propose GenRF, which adapts pretrained FLUX through LoRA to jointly enhance degraded observations, infer missing cross-modal context, and fuse complementary information without explicitly estimating deformation fields. Extensive experiments on real-world infrared and visible captures show that GenRF delivers high-quality fusion directly from unregistered, degraded images without preprocessing, while improving downstream task performance under misalignment. Additional experiments demonstrate that GenRF can generalize to misaligned multi-exposure and multi-focus image fusion without task-specific fine-tuning. Project: this https URL.

</details>


### [87] [AVTR: Adaptive Multimodal Reasoning with Visual Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24161)
*Xiaoqian Shen,Yunyang Xiong,Changsheng Zhao,Saksham Suri,Chong Zhou,Zechun Liu,Zhipeng Cai,Zhuang Liu,Qi Qian,Wei Wen,Hongyu Xu,Hanxian Huang,Ernie Chang,Yanbei Chen,Vikas Chandra,Raghuraman Krishnamoorthi,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Lemeng Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应视觉-文本推理方法AVTR，通过引入“视觉想象/视觉反思”作为补充推理模态，并用可学习路由机制根据问题动态选择视觉或文本推理，在多模态推理中同时提升感知准确性与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型主要依赖文本推理解决复杂多模态任务，但存在两类局限：一是在以视觉为中心的问题中，仅靠文本推理可能限制对关键视觉证据的感知和解释能力；二是对所有输入统一施加文本推理会忽略问题在推理模态和推理深度上的差异，导致在无需复杂逻辑推断的问题中产生冗长、低效的推理链。

Method: 提出Adaptive Visual-Textual Reasoning（AVTR）框架。首先，将视觉想象作为与文本思考互补的推理模态，使模型能够重新审视视觉观察，并通过生成式视觉反思提取显著视觉证据。其次，将推理模态选择建模为可学习的路由问题，使单一策略能够根据输入任务的感知需求和推理需求，自适应地调用视觉反思或文本推理，避免对所有样本使用同等深度或同一种模态的推理。

Result: 在16个基准测试上，AVTR相比Qwen3.5基线平均提升4.5分，同时将推理延迟降低33%。此外，该方法在小规模骨干模型上也能有效扩展，并优于评测的2B规模基线模型。

Conclusion: AVTR证明多模态推理不应局限于统一的文本思维链，而应根据问题性质自适应选择推理模态和深度。通过视觉反思补充文本推理，并用学习式路由协调不同推理模态，该方法能够在提升视觉中心任务准确率的同时显著降低推理成本，为高效、自适应的多模态推理提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models increasingly rely on textual reasoning to solve advanced multimodal tasks. Despite its effectiveness, this paradigm faces two fundamental limitations. First, relying on textual reasoning alone can limit perceptual ability in vision-centric questions, where accurate answers depend on interpreting task-relevant visual evidence. Second, applying textual reasoning uniformly across inputs overlooks question-dependent variation in both reasoning modality and depth, often producing verbose and unnecessary traces when little logical inference is required. To this end, we propose Adaptive Visual-Textual Reasoning (AVTR), which introduces visual imagination as a complementary reasoning modality to textual thinking, enabling the model to revisit its visual observations and distill the salient evidence through generative visual reflection. Furthermore, we formulate reasoning-modality selection as a learned routing problem, allowing a single policy to adaptively harness the complementary strengths of visual reflection and textual reasoning in response to the perceptual and inferential demands of the input. Across 16 benchmarks, AVTR improves the average score by 4.5 points over Qwen3.5 baseline, while reducing inference latency by 33\%. It also scales effectively into a smaller size backbone, outperforming the evaluated 2B-scale baselines.

</details>


### [88] [Gaussian Primitive Fusion for Any-Resolution Multimodal Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24164)
*Baoshuo Cai,Xinrui Jiang,Bing Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出Gaussian Primitive Fusion（GPF），将多模态图像融合从传统“离散公共网格 + 重采样”的范式，重构为“共享连续高斯空间中的融合”。该方法直接在源模态的原生分辨率观测上拟合共享高斯几何结构与模态特定内容表示，并在连续表示中学习模态贡献，最后在最高原生分辨率下渲染融合结果，从而避免重采样伪影，提升红外-可见光等异分辨率图像融合质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态图像融合方法通常在统一的离散网格上估计不同模态的贡献。当红外、可见光等源模态具有不同原生分辨率时，构造公共网格往往需要插值、下采样或超分辨率等重采样操作，容易引入模糊、块状伪影、边缘退化或纹理损失，并进一步影响融合权重判断和最终融合结果。

Method: 论文提出Gaussian Primitive Fusion，将异分辨率融合问题建模在共享连续高斯空间中。其核心步骤包括：1）不先将不同分辨率源图像转换到公共网格，而是在各自原生分辨率观测上直接拟合高斯基元；2）使用共享的高斯几何表示来对齐和统一不同模态的空间结构；3）为不同模态保留各自的内容表示，以建模模态特异性信息；4）在连续表示中学习并融合模态贡献；5）优化完成后，将融合表示直接在最细原生分辨率下渲染输出，避免对源图像进行重采样。

Result: 在异分辨率输入条件下，GPF能够获得更清晰的目标轮廓和更精细的纹理细节，并相比“先重采样再融合”的流程显著减少模糊和块状伪影。在多个红外-可见光融合基准测试中，该方法相较于配备插值或超分辨率前端的先进融合方法取得了最佳定量结果。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将多模态图像融合从离散公共网格扩展到连续高斯表示，可以有效规避异分辨率源图像融合中的重采样误差问题。通过共享高斯几何与模态特定内容表示，GPF在保持多模态互补信息的同时提升了融合图像的边缘、纹理和整体质量，为多模态图像融合提供了一种新的连续表示范式。

Abstract: Multimodal image fusion integrates complementary observations from different modalities into a unified representation. Existing methods typically estimate modality contributions on a common discrete grid. When source modalities have different native resolutions, constructing such a grid generally involves resampling, which may introduce artifacts and affect subsequent fusion decisions. To address this problem, we propose Gaussian Primitive Fusion (GPF), which reformulates heterogeneous-resolution fusion in a shared continuous Gaussian space. Instead of first converting source images to a common resolution, GPF directly fits their native-resolution observations with shared Gaussian geometry and modality-specific content representations, and learns modality contributions within the resulting continuous representation. Once optimized, the fused representation is rendered at the finest native resolution without resampling the sources. With heterogeneous-resolution inputs, GPF produces clearer target contours and finer textures, with less blurring and blocking artifacts than resample-then-fuse pipelines. Quantitatively, it achieves the best results on multiple infrared-visible benchmarks against state-of-the-art fusion methods equipped with interpolation or super-resolution frontends.

</details>


### [89] [ViStep-RL: Stepwise Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Visual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24170)
*Junyang Chen,Yao Qin,Xiao Li,Ningchao Wang,Yiguo Qiao,Wenbin Li,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ViStep-RL，通过依赖关联的证据子目标图、增量式任务状态奖励和主动记忆机制，提升长程视觉智能体在多轮交互中“查看—取证—使用证据”的能力，在 ViInteract 等任务上显著超越基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程视觉智能体不仅需要观察图像，还需要将观察结果转化为在整个任务过程中可持续利用的证据。现有主动感知方法主要优化“看哪里、怎么看”，但依赖最终答案奖励，难以监督哪些观察能满足有依赖关系的子目标、哪些证据应被保留并在后续步骤中使用。

Method: 提出 ViStep-RL：将任务执行建模为依赖关联的、承载证据的子目标图；引入验证器追踪各子目标是否获得有效证据支持，并以图势能变化构造净进度学习信号；设计主动记忆协议，在多轮交互中保存证据，并将检索行为与下游使用显式关联。

Result: 在 ViInteract 上，ViStep-RL 基于 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 达到 60.0% 严格完成率，明显高于基模型的 10.0% 和监督微调后的 46.5%。控制实验表明，增量式任务状态奖励优于累计式奖励 3.2 个百分点；主动证据写入相比自动写入提升严格完成率 7.75 个百分点。在统一交互框架下，ViStep-RL 达到 59.5% 答案准确率，比六个通用参考模型中最强者高 24.0 个百分点，并在留出测试和三个外部视觉推理基准上验证了增益。

Conclusion: 将长程视觉任务显式建模为证据驱动的子目标图，并结合进度式奖励与主动记忆协议，可有效解决多轮视觉推理中观察、证据留存和后续使用之间缺乏结构监督的问题。ViStep-RL 显著提升了视觉智能体的任务完成能力与跨基准泛化性。

Abstract: Long-horizon visual agents must do more than inspect images: they must turn observations into evidence that remains usable as a task unfolds. Existing active-perception methods teach agents where and how to look, while a final-answer reward gives little structure for deciding which observations satisfy dependent subgoals or should be retained for later use. We present ViStep-RL (Visual Stepwise Reinforcement Learning), which models execution as a dependency-linked graph of evidence-bearing subgoals. A verifier tracks which subgoals are supported by valid evidence, and changes in graph potential provide a net-progress learning signal; an active memory protocol preserves evidence across turns and links retrieval to downstream use. On ViInteract, ViStep-RL reaches 60.0% strict completion with Qwen2.5-VL-7B, compared with 10.0% for the base model and 46.5% after supervised fine-tuning. Controlled studies show that incremental task-state rewards outperform cumulative rewards by 3.2 points, while active evidence writing improves strict completion by 7.75 points over automatic writing. Under a common interaction framework, the task-trained ViStep-RL policy achieves 59.5% answer accuracy, 24.0 points above the strongest of six general-purpose reference models. Held-out episodes confirm the gain at both Qwen scales, and gains extend to three external visual reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [90] [ART: Bridging the Embodiment Gap in 3D Human-to-Robot Affordance Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24182)
*Zichen Zhang,Wei Zhai,Xuji Zhang,Yang Cao,Yu Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出“3D 人类到机器人可供性迁移”任务，构建人类—机器人配对可供性数据集 HPAD，并设计 ART 网络，将人类先验可供性迁移为机器人可执行分布，最终在真实机械臂上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多直接把人类可供性先验用于机器人操作，但可供性并非物体固有属性，而与分析个体形态密切相关；人手与机器人末端执行器结构差异会导致人类先验无法直接适配机器人执行。

Method: 论文首先定义 3D Human-to-Robot Affordance Transfer 任务，并构建包含 2,137 个物体、80 个类别、13 种原子动作的 HPAD 数据集。随后提出 ART 网络，通过可靠性门控流过滤人类先验中的几何误判，并利用层次化重建与图扩散精化，将人类可供性重构为机器人可执行分布，以解决人类与机器人可供性之间的拓扑失配。

Result: 在 HPAD 上的实验验证了任务可靠性和 ART 的优越性能；在 6-DoF 机械臂配平行夹爪的 2,400 次执行基准中，ART 取得 86.5% 的成功率，超过其 480 次试验基线，证明迁移范式的实用性。

Conclusion: 可供性应被视为与个体形态耦合的机器人可执行属性，而非简单直接借用的人类先验；HPAD 与 ART 为跨形态可供性迁移提供了数据基础和方法路径，可提升机器人操作中的动作规划与执行可靠性。

Abstract: 3D affordance acts as a critical link between perception and action, providing priors for robotic manipulation. While existing approaches predominantly leverage human priors as direct references, they often overlook that affordance is not an object-intrinsic property but is inherently coupled with the agent's embodiment. Due to the structural and morphological disparities between human hands and robot end-effectors, directly applying human priors causes severe incompatibility for robotic execution. Motivated by this, we introduce a novel task setting, 3D Human-to-Robot Affordance Transfer, which addresses the affordance misalignment arising from distinct embodiment structures. To support this task, we construct the Human-Robot Paired Affordance Dataset (HPAD), the first large-scale 3D affordance benchmark in which every object is annotated with both human-prior and simulation-validated robot labels. HPAD covers 2,137 objects across 80 categories and 13 atomic action primitives, yielding over 27,000 object-action pairs. On top of HPAD, we propose the 3D Affordance tRansfer neTwork (ART). ART first filters geometric false positives in the human prior via a reliability gating stream, then reconstructs the prior into a robot-actionable distribution through hierarchical reconstruction and a graph-diffusion refinement that resolves the topological mismatch between human and robot affordances. Experiments on HPAD demonstrate the reliability of the proposed task and the superiority of ART. We further validate ART on a 6-DoF arm with a parallel-jaw gripper in a 2,400-execution benchmark across 12 objects and 4 action categories; ART attains the best success rate (86.5% over its 480 trials), confirming the practicality of the transfer paradigm. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [91] [VideoACE: Benchmarking Agentic Video Understanding through High-Frame-Rate Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24184)
*Luoyuan Zhang,Xuanyu Zheng,Yancheng Long,Haoyang Xu,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VideoACE，一个面向智能体式视频理解的评测基准，重点考察模型能否通过持续交互、工具调用和用户反馈来主动收集证据并改进判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频理解评测通常假定模型只有一次固定观察机会，但智能体式视频理解要求模型能够主动交互、调整观察策略并利用额外证据形成判断。因此，现有评测不足衡量模型“将交互机会转化为更好理解”的能力，需要新的基准来评估这一能力。

Method: 作者构建了包含 1000 道选择题的 VideoACE 基准，覆盖六类细粒度动作理解任务，并提供时间证据标注和用户提示。评测环境支持模型使用视频观察工具，并可模拟用户反馈；模型能够调整时间覆盖范围、采样密度和空间细节，在交互中反复查看证据并修正判断。实验比较了单次观察、自主工具调用以及工具调用结合用户反馈三种设置，并对五个模型进行评估。

Result: 对五个模型的评测显示，它们在证据获取方式和利用交互机会方面存在明显差异。实验表明，在高速、细微变化丰富的动作视频任务中，智能体化的观察与反馈机制能够揭示模型超越固定观察设置的真实理解潜力。

Conclusion: VideoACE 为评估智能体式视频理解提供了专门测试平台，能够系统衡量模型如何通过主动观察、工具使用和用户交互来改善视频理解能力，而不仅限于固定单次输入下的表现。

Abstract: Agentic video understanding requires models to actively interact with video environments to gather evidence and form judgments. Evaluating this capability therefore requires assessing whether models can translate opportunities for interaction into better understanding. We introduce VideoACE, a benchmark for assessing video understanding under conditions that support sustained interaction. We focus on high-frame-rate action videos, where rapid, subtle changes make targeted observation especially valuable. The benchmark comprises 1,000 multiple-choice questions spanning six categories of fine-grained action understanding, accompanied by temporal evidence annotations and user hints screened for answer leakage. Its evaluation environment combines video inspection tools with a user simulator, allowing models to adjust temporal coverage, sampling density, and spatial detail, and to revisit evidence and revise their judgments under simulated user feedback. We evaluate single-pass observation, autonomous tool use, and tool use combined with user feedback to examine how effectively models exploit these opportunities. Evaluation of five models reveals distinct patterns in how they acquire evidence and respond to opportunities for further interaction. These findings establish VideoACE as a testbed for examining agentic video understanding beyond performance under a fixed observation setting.

</details>


### [92] [Monitoring Unplanned Construction Using Satellite Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24190)
*Harsh Kashyap,Taranum Wasu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究利用预训练神经网络特征提取和传统机器学习分类器，测试了从卫星影像识别建成土地与检测物理变化的简单方法，强调卫星图像可反映地表变化但不能判断建设是否经过规划审批。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度快速城市化导致新增建设可能破坏自然环境、增加废弃物并给公共服务带来压力，且大量城市聚落缺乏总体规划；但“无总体规划”的统计并不等于每栋建筑都违法，因此需要利用卫星影像和AI识别实际发生的物理变化。

Method: 使用两个印度之外的公开数据集，先用预训练神经网络将图像转换为特征向量，再输入标准机器学习分类器预测土地类别和图像对是否发生变化；同时在标准变化检测基准上训练小型神经网络，并对阿格拉48个影像块进行初步描述性检查，其中7个为正例。

Result: 方法在识别建成土地方面达到约95%准确率，在判断小图像对是否发生物理变化方面达到约83%准确率；阿格拉样本的初步检查也显示卫星影像包含物理变化信息，但不能直接识别规划或许可状态。

Conclusion: 卫星影像结合AI可辅助监测城市扩张和土地变化，具有作为物理变化监测工具的价值，但不能替代规划审批数据或法律授权信息；相关方法仍需在印度场景下进一步验证泛化能力和应用可靠性。

Abstract: Cities need land for homes, shops, roads, and public services. New construction changes that land. It can harm natural areas and water bodies, add to waste, and strain public services. NITI Aayog's 2021 report stated that 65% of 7,933 urban settlements in India had no master plan. That number tells us about planning coverage at the time. It does not tell us whether any single building was authorised. Artificial intelligence, which lets computers learn patterns from data, can help find physical changes by comparing satellite images taken on different dates. We test simple methods on two public datasets from outside India. First, we turn each image into a set of numbers using a pretrained neural network, a model already trained to recognise patterns in images. We feed those numbers to standard machine-learning classifiers that predict land categories and physical changes. The methods reach about 95 percent accuracy at spotting built-up land and about 83 percent accuracy at saying whether a small pair of images shows change, where each pair gets one label. We also train a small neural network on a standard change-detection benchmark. Separately, we run a preliminary descriptive check on an Agra sample of 48 patches, 7 of them positive. The results show that satellite images carry information about physical change. They do not show whether construction was planned or permitted.

</details>


### [93] [TrajWAM: Trajectory-Conditioned Future Learning for Vision-Language Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24192)
*Xuerui Fang,Gulimila Kezierbieke,Chen Wangxing,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrajWAM 是一种轨迹条件化的世界行动模型，通过辅助的鸟瞰图（BEV）未来预测学习，将自动驾驶中的未来场景预测与规划动作联系起来，并在 Bench2Drive 上提升了驾驶评分和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要模型不仅规划自身轨迹，还要理解给定动作会导致怎样的未来场景。每条驾驶记录包含已执行的自车轨迹及其观测到的未来，这为学习“动作—未来场景”关系提供了事实监督；作者希望利用这种关系增强视觉语言驾驶规划。

Method: TrajWAM 使用自车轨迹查询来条件化与 DINOv3 对齐的 BEV 未来预测，并共享 planner 表征。训练包括两部分：在已执行轨迹下进行事实性 BEV 未来重建，以及在未标注扰动轨迹下采用单侧特征分离。由此训练共享 planner 生成驾驶航点。推理时可直接输出航点，绕过辅助的未来预测分支。

Result: 在全部 220 条 Bench2Drive 路线上，TrajWAM 达到 91.53 的驾驶评分和 75.45% 的成功率；相比按计划匹配、仅规划微调的基线，驾驶评分提高 5.31 分，并多成功完成 8 条路线。

Conclusion: 将事实未来重建与扰动轨迹下的特征分离引入轨迹条件化世界行动模型，可以有效增强共享规划表征对动作与未来场景关系的理解，从而在不增加推理时未来预测开销的情况下提升自动驾驶规划性能。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) connect future scene prediction with action planning for autonomous driving. Each driving record pairs an executed ego trajectory with its observed future, providing a factual outcome for learning this connection. We present TrajWAM, a trajectory-conditioned WAM that improves vision-language driving through auxiliary bird's-eye-view (BEV) future learning. An ego-trajectory query conditions DINOv3-aligned BEV predictions from shared planner representations. Training combines factual reconstruction under the executed trajectory with one-sided feature separation under an unlabeled perturbed trajectory. Together, these objectives train the shared planner to generate driving waypoints. Native inference directly generates waypoints and bypasses the auxiliary future-prediction branch. On all 220 Bench2Drive routes, TrajWAM achieves 91.53 Driving Score and 75.45% Success Rate, exceeding a schedule-matched planning-only fine-tuning baseline by 5.31 Driving Score points and eight successful routes.

</details>


### [94] [WM^MLLM: Turning MLLMs into World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24193)
*Baiang Li,Enze Xie,Jincheng Yu,Jin Wang,Haozhe Liu,Yitong Li,Juanxi Tian,Felix Heide*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 WM^MLLM，一个基于多模态大语言模型的交互式世界模型，通过将世界状态外部化为带版本历史的全景图，实现可持久化、可编辑的单图世界生成，并保证重访一致性与局部编辑不破坏无关区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型通常把世界状态隐含在生成器的上下文中，因此当视角回到之前位置时，早期场景或编辑结果容易被重新合成、漂移甚至消失，难以实现稳定、可编辑的长期世界生成。

Method: WM^MLLM 将世界状态从生成器中解耦，表示为带有修订历史的全景图。多模态大语言模型负责把用户请求转换为对该世界状态的操作，包括选择局部编辑写入位置、验证候选状态是否合规，再将其推进为下一版本；图像生成器仅负责提议局部视图，视频模型仅用于呈现这些视图。

Result: 在 MBench-A 上对比六个世界模型基线，WM^MLLM 在四项环境恢复指标上均取得最高均值，其中 Epipolar 为 91.5，高于基线的 0.1 至 74.0；相机往返完成率也最高，为 85.3%，而基线最高为 50.9%。在 CompBench 的 15 个指标列上，相对 15 个基线均取得最佳值。盲评 112 个局部编辑显示，生成后再选择写入区域可将完成率从 34.8% 提升至 83.0%。在 258 个 WBench 和 MBench-A 样本中，46 个重访全部恢复先前视角，97 个通过检查的局部编辑均保证区域外全景像素不变。

Conclusion: 通过把世界状态外部化为可版本控制的全景表示，并用多模态大模型对其进行操作和校验，WM^MLLM 能够显著缓解视频世界模型中的重访漂移和编辑丢失问题，实现更稳定、可编辑、可交互的单图世界生成。

Abstract: Video world models keep the world in the context of a generator, so a revisit is synthesized again and earlier edits can drift or disappear. We present WM^MLLM, the first interactive world model built on multimodal large language models (MLLMs), for persistent, editable world generation from a single image. WM^MLLM keeps the world state outside every generator, as a panorama with a revision history, and lets the MLLMs write it: they turn each request into operations on this state, select where a local edit is written, and check every candidate before it becomes the next state. Image generators only propose views, and video only presents them. Users can walk to new places and return, edit objects through text or a box, place subjects, and change lighting, weather or season. By construction, a revisit at the same revision reproduces the earlier view exactly, a local edit leaves every panorama pixel outside its region unchanged, and each request starts from the result of the previous one. Against six world model baselines on MBench-A, WM^MLLM has the highest mean on all four environment-recovery metrics (Epipolar 91.5 versus 0.1 to 74.0) and completes the most camera round trips (85.3% versus at most 50.9%). It has the best value in all 15 CompBench columns against 15 baselines. On 112 local edits judged blind, selecting the region to write after generation raises completion from 34.8% to 83.0%. On 258 WBench and MBench-A items, all 46 revisits recover their earlier views, and all 97 local edits that pass the check leave panorama pixels outside their region unchanged.

</details>


### [95] [SpiderMark: Hybrid Watermarking for Generative AI via Algorithmic Injection and Learning-Based Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24200)
*Chihcheng Hsieh,Yang Song,Flora Salim,Martin Bell,Jacinto C. Nascimento,Catarina Moreira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SpiderMark：一种面向扩散生成图像的水印框架，通过在初始潜噪声频域嵌入“蜘蛛网”结构图案，并结合轻量级学习式验证器实现鲁棒、可感知性较好且无需改模权重的 AI 图像溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型生成图像激增，导致需要可靠方法追踪和验证 AI 生成图像来源。现有扩散模型水印多依赖各向同性频域图案和手工检测指标，在多种图像变换下容易方向性脆弱、检测阈值不稳定，难以兼顾不可感知性、鲁棒性和实际可验证性。

Method: SpiderMark 将算法式频域注入与学习式概率验证结合。首先，将结构化“蜘蛛网”图案——径向辐条和同心多边形环——嵌入初始潜噪声的傅里叶表示中；其次，不修改预训练扩散模型权重；最后，训练轻量级 ResNet-18 验证器，并加入 PSNR 与 L1 辅助相似度线索，使检测器输出连续的水印置信度分数。

Result: 在九种评估条件下，SpiderMark 在八种条件下取得最高固定阈值准确率；在未见过提示的 COCO 数据上，无需重新训练验证器仍保持较强检测能力。CLIP 相似度和 FID 结果显示，检测性能提升伴随的提示对齐和分布图像质量差异较小。

Conclusion: 结构化水印几何与学习式概率验证可统一为实用水印框架，在不修改预训练扩散模型的前提下，改善生成内容归属验证，弥合不可感知性、鲁棒性和真实场景可验证性之间的差距。

Abstract: The proliferation of diffusion-based generative models has intensified the need for reliable methods to trace and verify the provenance of AI-generated images. Existing watermarking approaches for diffusion models typically rely on isotropic frequency patterns and handcrafted detection metrics, which are prone to directional vulnerability and threshold instability across diverse image transformations. We propose SpiderMark, a hybrid watermarking framework that combines algorithmic frequency-domain injection with learning-based probabilistic verification, requiring no modifications to pretrained diffusion model weights. SpiderMark embeds a structured spider-web pattern, comprising radial spokes and concentric polygonal rings, into the Fourier representation of the initial latent noise, and trains a lightweight ResNet-18 verifier augmented with auxiliary similarity cues (PSNR and L1) to produce a continuous watermark confidence score for watermark detection. Across nine evaluation conditions, SpiderMark achieves the highest fixed-threshold Accuracy in eight, while retaining strong detection on unseen COCO prompts without verifier retraining. CLIP similarity and FID measurements show that these detection gains are accompanied by small measured differences in prompt alignment and distributional image quality. SpiderMark establishes that structured watermark geometry and learned probabilistic verification can be unified into a single, practical framework, bridging the gap between imperceptibility, robustness, and real-world verifiability for generative AI content attribution.

</details>


### [96] [DetailScale-SR: Decoupling Restoration and Continuous Detail Control for Real-World Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24212)
*Shengchuan Gao,Zhongjie Duan,Yingda Chen,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DetailScale-SR提出一种偏好条件的图像超分辨率框架，可在不改变场景语义和固定低分辨率输入的前提下，连续调节重建细节强度，实现从保守修复到增强纹理的平滑控制，并在文字富集场景中显著提升可读性与字符保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界图像超分辨率通常需要最细致且合理的重建，但“更多细节”并不总是理想结果。不同用户可能希望得到克制修复、忠实重建或增强细节；现有方法往往依赖后处理或反复修改提示词，难以在一致语义和输入条件下连续控制细节程度，也常常难以恢复文字等语义关键内容。

Method: 论文提出DetailScale-SR，采用偏好条件生成框架，将内容恢复与细节偏好解耦。模型学习同一低分辨率输入对应的连续重建族，通过连续控制信号调节高频细节和纹理生成强度，从而在保守修复与富细节增强之间平滑过渡，同时保持场景内容和语义约束一致。该机制超越传统仅恢复参考高分辨率的目标，还可增强超出参考图像的高频结构和感知纹理。

Result: 实验或方法描述表明，DetailScale-SR能够在固定输入和语义条件下实现连续、平滑的细节强度控制，并可生成比配对高分辨率参考更丰富、更有质感的高频结构。在文字富集场景中，尤其是在中文海报和招牌图像上，相比已有生成式超分辨率方法，在匹配文本提示的条件下，它能恢复更清晰、更忠实于原始字符的文字内容。

Conclusion: DetailScale-SR将连续细节控制、超越参考的纹理增强以及语义关键内容尤其是文字的可靠恢复统一到一个框架中，增强了真实世界图像超分辨率的可控性和实用性，适合需要按用户意图调节细节强度并重视文字可读性的应用场景。

Abstract: Real-world image super-resolution often seeks the most detailed plausible reconstruction, although more detail is not always desirable. The same degraded image may require restrained restoration, faithful reconstruction, or enhanced visual detail according to user intent. We present DetailScale-SR, a preference-conditioned framework that continuously controls reconstructed detail for a fixed low-resolution input without altering the scene being restored. Rather than relying on post-processing or repeatedly rewriting prompts, it learns a continuous family of reconstructions while separating content recovery from detail preference. This enables a smooth transition from conservative restoration to richly textured results under consistent input and semantic constraints. Its controllable range also extends beyond conventional restoration: DetailScale-SR can enhance high-frequency structures and generate perceptually richer textures than the paired high-resolution reference. Text restoration is another distinct strength. For text-rich scenes, especially Chinese posters and signs, it recovers more legible and character-faithful text than prior generative SR methods under matched textual guidance. DetailScale-SR therefore unifies continuous detail control, beyond-reference enhancement, and reliable recovery of semantically important content. Our model is available at this https URL

</details>


### [97] [Target-Consistent Action Guidance via Semi-Unbalanced Optimal Transport for Drifting World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24222)
*Md Abtahi Majeed Chowdhury,Md Rifat Ur Rahman,Md Sakib Hossain Shovon,Jaesik Choi,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Mass-Share Accentuation（MSA），用于修正动作条件世界模型中类似 classifier-free guidance 的增强项带来的目标偏置，使模型在正确预测未来时增强力为零，从而在不增加推理成本的情况下提升动作条件预测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界模型可用于机器人想象动作结果并进行规划和离线策略评估，但多步扩散采样代价高。Drifting models 将迭代移到训练阶段，只需一次网络前向即可生成样本。DriftWorld 通过让预测远离当前帧来增强动作跟随能力，但这种增强存在目标偏置：即使预测已经匹配真实未来，推力仍然存在，可能影响准确性。

Method: 作者提出 Mass-Share Accentuation。该方法把当前帧视为一个与真实未来竞争的反事实目标，在半松弛最优传输框架中处理不同预测的归属，并减去正确未来应得的闭式份额，使得增强项在预测命中目标时消失。作者还证明，在坍缩预测情形下，记录的未来是沿运动方向的唯一固定点，且增强强度会随预测误差增大而增强、随误差减小而减弱。

Result: 在 Push-T、Robomimic 和 BridgeV2 数据集上，相对于潜在空间基线方法，MSA 提升了动作排序、响应斜率和运动区域误差等指标，并且不增加推理成本。实验场景中的已训练模型主要表现为动作反应不足而非过冲，说明 MSA 的偏置修正在这些条件下带来收益。

Conclusion: MSA 从目标和概率归属角度修正了 DriftWorld 类动作条件漂移模型中的增强偏置，使指导信号在正确预测时自然消失。该方法在不增加推理开销的情况下改善动作条件世界模型的预测质量，但现有基准尚不能单独隔离偏置移除这一因素作为性能提升的唯一原因。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models let robots imagine the outcomes of their actions for planning and offline policy evaluation, but these uses need many rollouts, which multi-step diffusion makes expensive. Drifting models move the iteration from inference to training and generate samples in one network pass. DriftWorld brought this paradigm to action-conditioned world models; to strengthen action following, it repels predictions from the current frame, a construction borrowed from classifier-free guidance. We show that this accentuation has a target bias: the push persists even when a prediction matches the recorded future, in kernel, balanced, and unbalanced Wasserstein drifting fields alike. We introduce Mass-Share Accentuation (MSA) to remove this bias. MSA treats the current frame as a counterfactual destination competing with the recorded future in a semi-relaxed optimal transport problem; subtracting the closed-form share of the correct future makes the guidance vanish at the target, and the remainder is shared by all predictions. We prove that the recorded future is the unique fixed point along the motion direction for collapsed predictions, and that the guidance fades with prediction error. On Push-T, Robomimic, and BridgeV2, where trained models under-react rather than overshoot, MSA improves action ranking, response slope, and moving-region error over latent-space baselines at no inference cost, though these benchmarks do not isolate bias removal as the cause.

</details>


### [98] [Training Denoising Models of Any Architecture Without Stored Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24223)
