*Christos Koutlis,Ioannis Kompatsiaris,Symeon Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种无需保存原始图像数据的教师监督训练方法，利用预训练连续时间扩散/流模型的速度场直接训练学生模型，从而替代传统基于原始数据集的扩散模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型和流模型训练通常依赖大规模原始图像数据，带来显著存储成本，并在数据未公开或隐私受限场景下难以复现。本文探讨一个核心问题：一个预训练的去噪模型能否替代其原始训练数据集，用于从头训练新的扩散/流模型。

Method: 该方法采用教师监督训练框架。学生模型从随机初始化开始，不访问任何真实图像样本，而是对预训练教师模型输出的速度场进行回归。具体地，维护一个滚动噪声样本池，沿教师模型轨迹以随机步数推进，以生成分布较独立、覆盖较密集且近似连续的噪声水平，从而为学生模型提供连续时间训练监督信号。

Result: 实验覆盖 6 个数据集上的 12 种不同教师模型。结果显示，对于比教师小 4 倍的学生模型，该方法在 CIFAR-10 上优于同网络的常规数据训练；当学生模型达到教师大小时，其 FID 与标准训练相差仅 0.75 到 1.92。同时，该方法优于仅使用教师生成图像训练，以及不依赖教师初始化的步数缩减方法。

Conclusion: 预训练去噪模型可以作为原始数据集的有效替代，用于训练任意架构和大小的连续时间扩散模型及流模型。该方法能够大幅减少数据存储需求，并适用于私有或不可公开数据场景，同时保持较强生成质量。

Abstract: Can a pretrained denoising model replace the dataset used to train it? We investigate this question through teacher-supervised training, showing for the first time that continuous-time diffusion and flow models of any architecture and size can be trained from random initialization without stored images. Our students regress the teacher's velocity on a rolling pool of noisy samples, advanced along trajectories of random step counts to obtain independent, dense and approximately continuous noise levels. This saves considerable storage for large datasets and applies even when the data are unreleased or private. Our experiments include 12 teachers of 6 families and 6 datasets. Our method outperforms standard training of the same network on CIFAR-10 for students 4x smaller than the teacher, comes within 0.75 to 1.92 FID of it at the teacher's size and surpasses training on teacher-generated images and step reduction methods without teacher-based initialization. Our code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [99] [Psychometrics-Inspired Coreset Selection for Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24233)
*Susu Jiang,Xuelin Shen,Baoliang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRAM受心理测量学中的分级响应模型启发，从潜在质量、内容相关响应敏感度和失真相关等级阈值三个因素学习质量感知表示，用于更有效的图像质量评价核心集选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像质量评价模型依赖大规模主观标注，标注成本高。现有核心集选择方法常在通用视觉特征空间中追求样本多样性，但这种视觉多样性不一定对应感知质量多样性，导致所选样本未必能充分覆盖与质量判别相关的关键信息。

Method: 提出质量感知表示学习框架GRAM，其思想来自分级响应模型：个体潜在能力决定其在不同评价等级上的响应概率，其中响应敏感度和等级阈值控制评分边界。作者认为该机制与感知质量评价相符，因为图像退化的影响取决于内容特征和失真类型。GRAM将质量判断分解为潜在质量、内容依赖的响应敏感性和失真依赖的等级阈值，三者共同决定不同质量等级的概率及期望质量分数，并由此得到结构化质量感知表示，与通用视觉特征结合用于核心集选择。

Result: 在九个数据集上的实验表明，GRAM在多种核心集选择策略、下游图像质量评价模型和不同标注预算下均带来性能提升，说明所学习的质量感知表示有助于减少标注需求并提高IQA模型效果。

Conclusion: GRAM通过引入心理测量学分级响应机制，使表示学习更贴合感知质量评价结构，为低成本主观标注和核心集选择提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Collecting large-scale subjective annotations remains a critical bottleneck in training accurate image quality assessment (IQA) models. Coreset selection reduces this burden by selecting representative images before annotation. However, diversity in a visual feature space does not necessarily reflect diversity in perceptual quality, raising a fundamental question about which quality-related factors the representation should encode. We propose GRAM, a quality-aware representation learning framework inspired by the graded response model (GRM) from psychometrics. GRM describes how an individual's latent ability determines the probabilities of reaching successive assessment grades, with response sensitivity and grade thresholds governing this relationship. In this paper, we find that this principle naturally aligns with perceptual quality assessment, where the impact of image degradation depends on image content and distortion characteristics. Building on this connection, GRAM models quality judgments through three complementary factors: latent quality, content-dependent response sensitivity, and distortion-dependent grade thresholds. These factors jointly determine the probabilities of attaining different quality grades and the resulting expected quality score. The learned parameters provide a structured quality-aware representation that complements general visual features for coreset selection. Experiments on nine datasets demonstrate improvements across different selection strategies, downstream IQA models, and annotation budgets. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [100] [ViLFlow: Parallel Vision-Language Generation with Continuous Latent Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24240)
*Kevin Zhai,Bian Sun,Zhenyi Wang,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ViLFlow，一种面向视觉-语言生成的连续流模型，通过图像引导去噪、教师监督、并行解码与预测感知 token decoder，实现视觉问答与图像描述生成，并在 VQAv2、GQA 和 COCO 字幕任务上超越自回归与掩码扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言生成主要依赖自回归或离散扩散模型：自回归需串行生成 token，效率与全局建模受限；离散扩散通常在各去噪步独立预测 token，难以充分利用连续表示与输出间的联合依赖。因此，需要在连续潜空间中结合图像条件、并行生成和高质量 token 解码，以提升视觉-语言生成的准确性与效率。

Method: ViLFlow 将预训练连续语言生成模型适配到视觉问答和图像描述任务。模型联合建模输出嵌入空间并并行解码 token。训练中使用经过视觉适配的教师模型，为去噪过程提供图像条件预测作为额外监督，使去噪器学习直接复现这些预测，每一步保留一个图像条件预测。Token decoder 以去噪器预测与参考文本为训练目标，提升连续输出到文字的转换能力，并可独立适配而不重训去噪器。图像描述任务中，decoder 在后期去噪阶段提供文本反馈，使已解码词参与后续精炼。

Result: ViLFlow 在 VQAv2 上相对自回归和掩码扩散基线最高提升 8.46 个百分点，在无需 GQA 训练数据的迁移设置下于 GQA 上提升 4.50 分。在 COCO Karpathy test 上，相比掩码扩散提升 16.25 CIDEr，相比采样自回归提升 17.81 CIDEr。

Conclusion: ViLFlow 证明连续流建模与并行 token 解码可用于视觉-语言生成，并在保持高效并行生成能力的同时提升问答与描述质量。其解耦的去噪器与解码器设计便于任务适配，图像引导和教师监督则增强了视觉条件生成能力。

Abstract: Vision-language generation has predominantly relied on autoregressive and discrete diffusion models. Autoregressive models generate tokens sequentially, while discrete diffusion models typically predict tokens independently within each denoising step. We propose ViLFlow (Vision-Language Flow), a continuous-flow vision-language model that combines joint modeling of output embeddings with parallel token decoding. ViLFlow adapts pretrained continuous language generation to visual question answering and image captioning through learned image guidance and prediction-aware token decoding. A visually adapted teacher provides image-guided predictions as additional denoising supervision. The denoiser learns to reproduce these predictions directly, retaining one image-conditioned prediction per generation step. We train the token decoder on denoiser predictions and paired reference text to improve the conversion of continuous outputs into words. The decoder can be adapted independently, improving answering and captioning without further denoiser training. For captioning, it also supplies text feedback during late denoising, allowing decoded words to inform subsequent refinement. ViLFlow outperforms autoregressive and masked-diffusion baselines by up to 8.46 percentage points on VQAv2 and 4.50 points on GQA without GQA training. On COCO Karpathy test, it improves CIDEr by 16.25 over masked diffusion and 17.81 over sampled autoregression.

</details>


### [101] [End-to-End Landsat Land Surface Temperature Reconstruction with AlphaEarth Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24243)
*Shengjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出端到端 U-Net 模型 A^2E^2，融合年度温度周期、地球系统模型粗分辨率 LST 与 AlphaEarth 嵌入，用于重建受云遮挡影响的 Landsat 30 m 地表温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Landsat 30 m 高分辨率地表温度数据应用价值高，但云污染会破坏其空间连续性，需要利用多源先验信息实现缺失像元的高精度重建。

Method: 构建端到端 U-Net 框架 A^2E^2：以年度温度周期作为物理引导的热先验，以地球系统模型粗分辨率 LST 提供时间背景，并利用 AlphaEarth 嵌入表示地物特征，由 U-Net 将嵌入场转换为依赖观测像元的日尺度精细校正量，从而重建 Landsat 级 LST。

Result: 在 2022 年纽约市四个季节分布的 Landsat 8/9 场景实验中，A^2E^2 达到 0.78 K 平均绝对误差和 1.01 K 均方根误差，较无空间校正基线降低 60.1%，较以年均光谱反射作为引导的相同 U-Net 降低 3.7%；敏感性分析显示模型对到清晰像元的距离和遮挡像元比例较为稳定；独立使用 ECOSTRESS LST 验证表明地球基础模型嵌入优于年均光谱波段。

Conclusion: A^2E^2 能有效利用物理热先验、粗尺度温度背景与地球基础模型嵌入，实现高精度、鲁棒的 Landsat 地表温度空间重建，说明基础模型嵌入在高分辨率 LST 重建中具有优于传统年均光谱特征的潜力。

Abstract: Land surface temperature (LST) data at Landsat's 30 m resolution is important for many applications, but cloud contamination prevents its spatially continuous observations. Here, we propose A$^2$E$^2$, an end-to-end U-Net that integrates annual temperature cycle (ATC), coarse LST from Earth system modeling, and AlphaEarth embeddings for Landsat LST reconstruction. The ATC supplies a physics-guided thermal prior, the coarse LST provides a temporal background, and the U-Net converts the embedding field into day-specific fine-scale correction using observed pixels. Experimental results on four seasonally distributed Landsat 8/9 scenes over New York City in 2022 show that A$^2$E$^2$ achieves 0.78 K mean absolute error and 1.01 K root mean squared error, reducing error by 60.1% relative to baseline method without spatial correction and by 3.7% relative to the same U-Net guided by annual mean spectral reflectance. Sensitivity analysis shows stable performance regardless of distance to clear pixels and percentage of blocked pixels. Independent validation with ECOSTRESS LST shows that Earth foundation model embeddings have better performance than annual mean spectral bands for LST reconstruction.

</details>


### [102] [Allocentric Memory, Egocentric Acting: A Dual-Frame VLA for Urban Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24247)
*Haotian Xu,Yue Hu,Zhengqiu Zhu,Tuo Feng,Wenguan Wang,Quanjun Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CityNav-VLA 提出一种面向语言目标空中导航的双帧视觉-语言-动作模型，通过固定北向地图构建持久第三人称空间记忆，并结合动态第一人称感知、检索式地图目标预测、确定性控制器和早目标承诺机制，显著提升长程 UAV 导航的准确性、稳定性与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言目标 UAV 导航需要仅依靠飞行过程中的视觉观测到达自然语言描述的目的地。核心难点在于：UAV 的 egocentric 感知视角不断变化，而导航所需的空间证据往往绑定于稳定的地理位置。若缺乏持久且对齐真实世界坐标的空间记忆，长程导航中容易出现证据丢失、视角变化下推理不连续以及预测目标频繁波动等问题。

Method: 该方法提出 CityNav-VLA，将动态 egocentric 上下文与持久 allocentric 空间记忆融合。具体地，UAV 在带有地理先验和地标先验的固定北向地图上增量积累视觉观测，形成跨视角稳定的空间证据表示。模型通过一个由指令、当前观测、UAV 状态和运动历史共同条件化的动态 query，从空间记忆中检索相关证据，并在地图坐标系中预测目标位置。随后，确定性控制器利用 UAV 当前位姿将地图坐标目标转换为机体坐标系控制指令，从而连接全局空间推理与局部执行。为降低长程预测中的目标抖动，作者提出 Early Goal Commitment（EGC）机制，在前 K 次预测后将执行目标固定为坐标分位中位数，无需额外可训练参数。

Result: 在 Test Unseen 设置下，CityNav-VLA 将 NE 降低 21.2%，SR 提升 15.8%，SPL 提升 30.9%。在数据集精化后，相比此前 SOTA，其 NE 进一步降低 6.2%，SR 提升 5.6%，SPL 提升 23.2%。消融实验表明，导航地图特征带来最大性能增益，而引入度量坐标可进一步提升终点定位精度。模型在 4B 规模 backbone 下，每次决策平均耗时 0.68 秒，单张 A100 峰值显存为 8.6 GB，具有较高效率。

Conclusion: CityNav-VLA 证明了在语言目标空中导航中，将不断变化的第一人称观测与稳定、可持久维护的第三人称空间地图记忆相结合是有效的。其检索式目标预测、确定性坐标转换和 EGC 机制能够兼顾长程空间一致性、控制稳定性与计算效率，为实际 UAV 场景中的语言导航提供了一种高效且可扩展的建模路径。

Abstract: Language-goal aerial navigation requires a UAV to reach a destination specified in natural language using observations collected during flight. A key challenge is linking changing egocentric perception with spatial evidence anchored to stable locations for long-range navigation. We propose CityNav-VLA, a dual-frame vision-language-action model for urban navigation integrating dynamic egocentric context with persistent allocentric spatial memory. The UAV incrementally accumulates visual observations on a fixed north-up map initialized with geographic and landmark priors, preserving evidence across viewpoint changes. A dynamic query conditioned on the instruction, current observations, UAV state, and motion history retrieves relevant memory evidence to predict a map-frame target. A deterministic controller converts this target into body-frame commands using the UAV's current pose, connecting global reasoning with egocentric execution. To reduce target fluctuations over long horizons, early goal commitment (EGC) fixes the execution target to the coordinate-wise median of the first $K$ predictions without extra trainable parameters. On Test Unseen, CityNav-VLA reduces NE by 21.2% and improves SR and SPL by 15.8% and 30.9%, respectively. It also reduces NE by 6.2% and improves SR and SPL by 5.6% and 23.2%, respectively, relative to the former SOTA after dataset refinement. Cumulative ablations show that navigation-map features yield the largest gains, while metric coordinates further improve endpoint accuracy. With a 4B-scale backbone, inference averages 0.68 s per decision with 8.6 GB peak memory on a single A100, presenting remarkable model efficiency.

</details>


### [103] [One Shot, Any View: High-Fidelity Video Reshooting with Geometry Guidance and Visual References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24253)
*Kai Huang,Runze Xu,Hui Ren,Taiyi Wu,Xinyuan Li,Xun Gao,Quande Liu,Dong Tang,Tongtong Shen,Xuefeng Su,Alan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 One Shot Any View：以已有视频和用户指定相机轨迹为输入，基于前馈重建得到的 3D 点视频作为显式几何先验，结合预训练的参考到视频生成模型，实现免补拍的多视角视频重生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影视场景若要从新角度呈现通常需要昂贵补拍。现有几何引导方法依赖前馈重建生成的 3D 先验，但人物中心影视素材常具有长焦、浅景深、低照度等特点，导致点云/深度质量下降，进而使生成质量明显退化。

Method: 系统条件包括原始视频和沿指定轨迹渲染的 3D 点视频。为提升退化点视频的可用性，提出两点设计：一是训练时同时使用真实多相机视频，使其点视频由推理相同重建管线生成，从而保持训练与推理一致，并让模型主要从点视频获取人及场景元素的布局，从原视频获取外观；二是几何锚定 RoPE 偏移，使生成视频与源视频在时间上同步，并减少复制点视频中的退化外观。由于生成器是预训练参考到视频模型，还支持可选的人物和场景参考图，以补充原视频未观测区域的外观。

Result: 在客观指标和人工评测中，One Shot Any View 优于现有视频重拍/新视角生成方法，能在退化几何先验条件下生成质量更高、更稳定的重拍视频。

Conclusion: 通过训练推理一致的点视频监督和几何锚定的时间位置编码，系统能够更有效利用低质量 3D 先验，实现面向人类中心影视视频的高质量指定轨迹新视角重生成，降低补拍成本。

Abstract: Filming a scene from an additional angle after production usually requires a costly reshoot. We present One Shot Any View, a system that takes a recorded clip and generates a reshot video of the same performance along a user-specified camera trajectory. As in recent geometry-grounded methods, the system conditions generation on the source video and an explicit 3D prior, namely a point video rendered along the requested trajectory from a feed-forward reconstruction of the source video. We target human-centric cinematic videos, which are often shot with long lenses, shallow depth of field, and low-key lighting, all of which degrade feed-forward reconstruction. Existing geometry-grounded methods tend to inherit the degraded point video, and their output quality drops visibly. Two designs make such point videos usable. First, in addition to rendered videos of 3D assets, we train on real-world multi-camera videos, whose point videos are produced by the same reconstruction pipeline as at inference. This training-inference consistency enables the generator to take only the composition of people and scene elements from the point video, and their appearance from the source video. Second, a geometry-anchored RoPE offset keeps the generated video temporally synchronized with the source while largely preventing the output from copying the degraded appearance of the point video. Meanwhile, since the generator is a pretrained reference-to-video (R2V) model, the system also accepts optional character and scene reference images, which supply the appearance of regions that the source never shows. In both objective and human evaluations, One Shot Any View outperforms existing reshooting methods.

</details>


### [104] [Spark-H3: Adaptive Block Sparse Attention for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24256)
*Zecheng Tang,Jiaye Fu,Youtian Lin,Xinyao Rao,Liuxuan Sun,Jian Zhang,Yao Yao,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Spark-Attn，一种免训练的块稀疏注意力方法，通过重新分组和重加权校准提升视频扩散模型中长序列注意力的效率与质量平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型中的长视频序列导致注意力计算成本高昂。块稀疏注意力虽可降低开销，但固定token分组容易将注意力偏好差异大的token放入同一块，而均匀key平均则会低估被压缩块的注意力质量。

Method: Spark-Attn包含两个核心组件：Spark-Reblock递归地按注意力偏好对token进行分组，同时保持规则块形状以适配GPU高效计算；Spark-Reweight以全局平均query为锚点，构造注意力加权的键值表示和logit bias，用于校准压缩块的注意力质量。该方法为免训练方案，并在MiniMax-H3上实例化为Spark-H3。

Result: 在10%精确块密度下，Spark-H3相比对应稠密输出达到2.49倍DiT加速和24.81 dB PSNR（20个去噪步、345帧、1344×768分辨率）。将密度提高到20%后，PSNR进一步提升到26.76 dB，同时仍保持2.26倍DiT加速。

Conclusion: Spark-Attn通过注意力感知的重新分组和压缩块质量校准，在视频扩散模型的免训练块稀疏注意力中实现了领先的质量与效率权衡。

Abstract: Long video sequences make attention a substantial cost in video diffusion models. Block sparse attention (BSA) reduces this cost by computing selected blocks exactly while compressing the remaining blocks. However, fixed token grouping can place tokens with dissimilar attention preferences in the same group, while uniform key averaging can underestimate the attention mass of compressed blocks. We introduce Spark-Attn, a training-free BSA method comprising Spark-Reblock and Spark-Reweight. Spark-Reblock recursively groups tokens by attention preference while preserving regular block shapes for efficient GPU execution. Spark-Reweight uses the global mean query as an anchor to construct attention-weighted key-value representations and logit biases for the compressed blocks, calibrating their attention mass. We instantiate Spark-Attn in MiniMax-H3 as Spark-H3. At 10% exact-block density, Spark-H3 delivers a 2.49$\times$ DiT speedup and reaches 24.81 dB PSNR against the corresponding dense outputs on MiniMax-H3 (20 denoising steps, 345 frames, 1344$\times$768). Increasing the density to 20% further improves PSNR to 26.76 dB while still achieving a 2.26$\times$ DiT speedup. These results demonstrate that Spark-H3 achieves a state-of-the-art quality-efficiency trade-off for training-free BSA on video diffusion models.

</details>


### [105] [Do Thinking VLMs Need to Keep Looking at Long Videos? Write It Down, Then Look Away](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24259)
*Pengyu Li,Zhitao Gao,Lingling Zhang,Heng Wang,Chengqian Ma,WenHao Wang,Wenxuan Huang,Chen Zheng,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LookAway 让视频-语言模型在推理时先读取视频并写出与问题相关的带时间戳笔记，随后隐藏视频 KV cache，仅基于笔记继续生成答案，从而大幅降低长视频推理中的重复视频读取成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频-语言模型在处理长视频时，每个解码步都需要反复读取整个视频的 key-value cache，导致推理成本很高。论文提出关键问题：模型在推理过程中是否必须一直“看”视频？观察显示，模型对视频信息的依赖主要集中在响应早期，因此可能不需要全程保留视频缓存访问。

Method: LookAway 将视频推理过程分为两阶段：第一阶段，模型读取视频，生成针对当前问题的带时间戳笔记；第二阶段，一旦笔记写入完成，推理引擎隐藏视频对应的 KV cache，模型只基于笔记和已生成文本继续推理/回答。作者在混合注意力和纯注意力 VLM 上评估该方法，并比较自由推理、使用其他视频笔记、保留视频可见等设置。为缩短早期视频读取长度，作者进一步用模型自身正确的 LookAway 输出进行微调，并用强化学习同时奖励答案正确性、惩罚 KV 读取。

Result: 在不增加额外训练的情况下，LookAway 的准确率接近完整视频推理，但建模解码成本仅约为其 0.12–0.13 倍。在 Qwen3.6-27B 上，书写笔记比在同一撤出步骤进行自由推理更能保持准确率；替换成另一个视频的笔记会显著损害准确率，而即使保留视频可见，只要已有模型自己的笔记，也没有显著收益。经过微调和强化学习后，训练模型通常在中位 66–68 个 token 后隐藏视频。在三个保留基准上，其生成 token 数多于同样训练的全视频模型，但在匹配准确率时仅读取 27%–35% 的 KV 位置。

Conclusion: 长视频 VLM 不需要在整个推理过程中持续读取完整视频 KV cache；通过早期写入结构化、与问题相关的视觉笔记，并在后续推理中屏蔽视频缓存，可以在保持准确率的同时显著降低视频读取和解码成本。该方法为高效视频推理提供了训练无关和训练增强两种路径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) reason about long videos over hundreds to thousands of tokens. Every decoding step re-reads the key-value (KV) cache of the entire video. Must the model keep looking while it reasons? In LookAway, the model first writes timestamped notes on what the question needs and then puts the video away. Once the notes close, the inference engine hides the video's KV cache for the rest of the response. Across both hybrid and pure-attention VLMs, dependence on the video is concentrated early in the response. On Qwen3.6-27B, writing notes preserves accuracy better than free reasoning at the same withdrawal step. Replacing them with another video's notes harms accuracy, whereas keeping the video visible alongside the model's own notes adds no significant benefit. Without additional training, LookAway nearly matches full-video thinking accuracy at 0.12-0.13 times its modeled decode cost. To shorten this initial look, we fine-tune the model on its own correct LookAway responses. Reinforcement learning then rewards correct answers while penalizing KV reads. The trained model puts the video away after a median of 66-68 tokens. Across three held-out benchmarks, it writes more tokens than an identically trained full-video model yet reads only 27-35% as many KV positions at matched accuracy.

</details>


### [106] [Look Closer, Look Back, or Wait for Agentic Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24267)
*Pengyu Li,Zhitao Gao,Lingling Zhang,Wenxuan Huang,YiCheng Xiao,Chen Zheng,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ActiveForager，一个面向流式视频问答的智能体框架：在极低内存和延迟约束下，模型只保留简短的当前视频窗口，并在需要时选择直接回答、放大高分辨率近期帧、检索历史记忆或等待未来关键帧，从而提升实时感知、历史回忆和主动响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频模型需要在在线场景下实时回答视频问题，但可用上下文受显存和延迟限制。仅使用最新几帧会导致历史信息缺失，而加入过多过去帧又会稀释当前注意力并诱发幻觉。已有级联式检索方法虽能在不确定时调用历史证据，但在当前画面模糊、决定性线索尚未出现于未来时仍无法可靠应对。因此需要一种能按需、主动、隔离地获取证据的流式视频智能体。

Method: ActiveForager 维护一个“干净”的短工作窗口，并在每个查询时将回答过程建模为工具选择问题：可直接回答、可放大高分辨率近期帧以改善当前感知、可召回历史事件、或可等待后续帧。对于历史召回，论文设计共享权重的子智能体，用于隔离验证候选片段，并返回紧凑证据摘要。同时引入 stream-decoupled attention mask，使历史证据不会干扰当前视觉 token 的表示与推理。训练采用 counterfactual information gain：只有当工具调用所收集的证据能够提高真实答案概率时才给予奖励，从而抑制无效工具调用和工具坍缩。

Result: 在 OVO-Bench 上，该方法同时改善实时感知、历史回忆和主动响应。基于 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 和 Qwen3-VL-8B 的实验显示，ActiveForager 将 Qwen3-VL-8B 的总体准确率从 47.6% 提升到 65.9%。在多跳推理基准 Video-Holmes 上，其 zero-shot 准确率提升约一倍，且每个查询仅增加极少量的视觉 token。

Conclusion: ActiveForager 的核心贡献在于将流式视频问答重构为可学习的证据获取过程，而非一次性向模型输入大量历史帧。通过按需检索、高分辨率局部放大、未来等待以及注意力隔离机制，该框架能够在严格延迟和内存限制下更有效地利用有限视频证据，显著降低幻觉与工具滥用的风险，并提升实时视频理解的整体性能。

Abstract: Streaming video models must answer questions about a live video under strict memory and latency limits. Feeding only the latest few frames keeps present perception sharp, whereas adding past frames dilutes attention on the current scene and triggers hallucinations. Recent cascade methods retrieve past frames when uncertain, but they still fail when current frames are blurry or when decisive future clues have not appeared yet. We introduce ActiveForager, a streaming video agent that maintains a clean short working window and gathers extra evidence only on demand. For each query, the agent either answers directly, zooms into high-resolution recent frames, recalls past events from memory, or waits for upcoming frames. When recalling history, a shared-weight sub-agent verifies candidate clips in isolation and returns a compact evidence summary. A stream-decoupled attention mask then keeps historical evidence from interfering with present visual tokens. We train this policy using counterfactual information gain, which rewards a tool call only when the gathered evidence makes the true answer more likely and prevents tool collapse across all actions. Across Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B on OVO-Bench, ActiveForager improves real-time perception, historical recall, and proactive response at once, lifting overall accuracy on Qwen3-VL-8B from 47.6% to 65.9%. It also doubles zero-shot accuracy on the multi-hop Video-Holmes benchmark while adding minimal visual tokens per query.

</details>


### [107] [Semantic Guidance for Joint Infrared-Visible Video Fusion and Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24277)
*Yushen Xu,Xiaosong Li,Huaming Wu,Dongwei Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAFI，通过目标时刻的语义信息联合引导视频插帧与红外-可见光融合，以缓解高帧率融合中因帧率不一致、缺失观测和运动遮挡导致的误差累积与亮度闪烁问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在红外与可见光视频高帧率融合中，当相机帧率不一致或输入帧率低于目标输出帧率时，需要恢复缺失观测。简单级联“先插帧再融合”会累积误差并引起亮度波动；运动与遮挡进一步增加内容选择难度，而可见光帧缺失还会削弱直接的语义引导。

Method: SAFI利用目标时刻的语义信息联合指导插帧与融合。当可见光帧率高于红外帧率时，直接利用可见光帧提供语义上下文；在缺失可见光观测的时刻，通过运动引导的局部匹配与传播恢复语义。语义上下文与对齐后的图像特征互补，匹配置信度用于筛选可靠的红外和可见光信息。重构与融合在每个尺度共享源选择机制，使缺失帧上的监督能够指导融合。同时，通过限制热红外增强幅度并惩罚跨帧亮度不一致，同时考虑热对比度变化，来保持可见光外观并减少闪烁。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，SAFI相比级联基线取得了更好的视觉质量，同时计算量更低；在输入帧率不一致的情况下仍能获得稳定的时序性能提升，并且在缺失帧条件下保持较强的下游目标跟踪性能。

Conclusion: SAFI证明了利用目标时刻语义信息联合建模插帧与红外-可见光融合的有效性。该方法能够同时改善融合质量、时序一致性和计算效率，尤其适用于帧率不一致或缺失可见光观测的高帧率视频融合场景。

Abstract: Infrared and visible video fusion at high frame rates requires recovering missing observations when camera rates differ or fall below the desired output rate. Cascading interpolation and fusion can accumulate errors and cause brightness fluctuations. Motion and occlusion further complicate content selection, particularly when missing visible frames also remove direct semantic guidance. We propose SAFI, which uses semantics at the target time to guide joint interpolation and fusion. When the visible frame rate exceeds the infrared rate, available visible frames provide semantic context directly. At times without visible observations, motion guides local matching, and propagation recovers semantics from neighboring frames. Semantic context complements aligned image features at the target time, while matching confidence helps select reliable infrared and visible information. Reconstruction and fusion share source selection within each scale, allowing supervision on missing frames to guide fusion. To preserve visible appearance and reduce flicker, we bound thermal enhancement and penalize brightness inconsistencies across frames while accounting for changes in thermal contrast. Experiments on three datasets show improved visual quality with less computation than cascaded baselines and consistent temporal gains when input rates differ. SAFI maintains strong downstream tracking performance despite missing frames. The code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [108] [CROMPT: Adapting CLIP via Cross-Modal Prompting for Robust Zero-Shot Generalization under Adverse Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24284)
*Aniruddh Sikdar,Arya Kishor,Snehashis Dhibar,Suresh Sundaram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CROMPT，一种面向零样本语义分割域适应的提示学习框架，通过跨模态特征增强、混合文本提示、视觉语言模型生成条件提示以及由易到难的渐进式适应策略，在无需目标域图像的情况下提升分割泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当目标域图像在训练阶段不可用时，语义分割的域适应极具挑战。现有提示学习方法难以适配基于ResNet的CLIP视觉骨干网络，也难以实现像素级的跨模态特征对齐，因此需要一种能在零样本域适应场景下联合优化视觉与文本表示、并保持推理效率的方法。

Method: 作者提出CROMPT框架，核心是跨模态特征增强模块CROMA，其结合随机视觉提示和混合文本提示，以同时调整视觉表征与文本表征；进一步提出CROMA-VL，利用视觉语言模型生成与目标域条件相关的提示，并采用由易到难的渐进式域适应策略，以增强模型对多域变化的不变性。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比强基线和现有先进方法取得一致提升，mIoU提高1.63%至2.63%，同时保持与原有设置相近的推理复杂度。

Conclusion: CROMPT能够有效利用文本描述完成零样本、多域语义分割适应，在显著提升跨域分割性能的同时维持推理效率，为缺乏目标域图像条件下的语义分割域适应提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Domain adaptation for semantic segmentation is challenging when target-domain images are unavailable during training. Zero-shot domain adaptation addresses this by adapting models using only textual descriptions of the target domain. However, existing prompt-learning methods are not well suited for ResNet-based CLIP vision backbones or pixel-level cross-modal feature alignment. We propose CROMPT, a prompt-learning framework for zero-shot domain adaptation in semantic segmentation. CROMPT introduces Cross-Modal Feature Augmentation (CROMA), combining Random Visual Prompting with Hybrid Textual Prompting (HTP) to jointly adapt visual and textual representations. We further propose CROMA-VL, which leverages vision-language models to generate domain-conditioned prompts, and a progressive easy-to-hard adaptation strategy for improved domain invariance. Experiments on two benchmark datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines and state-of-the-art methods, achieving 1.63-2.63% mIoU gains while maintaining inference-time complexity. These results establish CROMPT as an effective and efficient framework for zero-shot multi-domain semantic segmentation. OpenReview: this https URL

</details>


### [109] [AnchorUp: Anchoring Feature Upsampling on a Classical Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24296)
*Kotaro Horikawa,Keisuke Toida,Junya Komori,Kazuhiro Hotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 AnchorUp：一种轻量级特征上采样器，不再从头预测高分辨率特征，而是以双线性插值为锚点，只学习对插值结果的边缘/细节修正，通过一个可学习标量门融合，显著降低参数量和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型输出的特征通常是粗粒度 patch grid，而密集预测任务需要像素级分辨率特征。现有学习式上采样器通常直接回归完整的高分辨率特征图，但其中平滑区域大部分可由传统插值重建，导致模型浪费容量重复计算已有内容。作者认为学习式上采样器应只聚焦于传统插值难以恢复的边缘和细粒度结构。

Method: AnchorUp 将上采样重新定义为“锚点 + 修正”：保留无参数的经典插值上采样器作为 anchor，提供平滑内容；另用小型 guidance network 学习残差式修正，主要恢复边缘和细节；再通过单个可学习标量 gate 融合 anchor 与修正结果。整体参数极少，仅使用 167,425 个参数，约为最强基线 NAF 的 1/4，推理延迟也更低。

Result: 分析表明学习到的修正主要集中在边缘和精细结构上，验证了 anchor 负责平滑内容、guidance 负责局部修正的设计。在 Pascal VOC 和 Cityscapes 上，仅使用 NAF 第一训练阶段训练的 AnchorUp 已能与完整训练两阶段的 NAF 持平；在 DAVIS-2017 视频目标分割上超过 NAF，并在所有 backbone 上一致提升，尤其轮廓准确度 F 提升更明显。

Conclusion: AnchorUp 说明在视觉特征上采样任务中，传统插值已足以承担大部分低难度平滑区域重建，学习模型更适合专注于边缘和细节修正。这种“参数化锚点 + 轻量修正 + 标量门控”的范式能以更少参数、更低延迟达到甚至超过强基线，是密集预测任务中高效特征分辨率恢复的有效方案。

Abstract: Vision foundation models produce semantically strong features on a coarse patch grid, and dense prediction needs them at pixel resolution. Existing learned upsamplers close that gap by regressing the high-resolution feature map from the low-resolution one, typically through cross-attention driven by the input image. We observe that most of what they regress needs no learning: plain bilinear interpolation already reproduces the smooth regions of the target, and recent benchmarks even report bilinear and bicubic on par with several learned upsamplers. The rest is mostly edges and fine details, which suggests that these learned upsamplers spend much of their capacity re-computing the smooth content that classical interpolation provides. We therefore introduce AnchorUp, which stops predicting the high-resolution feature map and predicts only its correction from the interpolation. It keeps a parameter-free classical upsampler as an anchor, obtains a second prediction from a small guidance network, and blends the two through a single learnable scalar gate. Because the anchor supplies the smooth content, the guidance network only has to correct what remains. An analysis of the learned correction confirms that it concentrates on edges and fine details, and so the network can be small: AnchorUp uses 167,425 parameters, a quarter of the budget of the strongest baseline, NAF, and runs at 64% of its latency. Trained once on DINOv3 with only the first of NAF's two training stages, and evaluated on that backbone plus six held-out ones, it is already on par with NAF trained through both stages on Pascal VOC and Cityscapes. Our method surpasses it on DAVIS-2017 video object segmentation; the gain holds on every backbone and is larger in contour accuracy F. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [110] [Fuse to Recognize the Unseen: Class-Relative Infrared-Visible Fusion for Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24316)
*Zehao Gao,Jie Feng,Wenda Zhao,Junpeng Zhang,Ronghua Shang,Weisheng Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EmergeFuse，一种面向开放词汇语义分割的可见光-红外图像融合框架，通过类相对热线索筛选与文本对比修正，将红外互补信息转化为未见类别语义校正，提升无监督开放词汇分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光-红外融合虽能增强视觉感知，但CLIP型开放词汇模型主要基于RGB预训练，直接注入红外信息可能破坏RGB-语言对齐，造成语义干扰，尤其影响未见类别识别。

Method: 基于像素级RGB-IR语义分析，EmergeFuse采用类相对线索选择，评估RGB-IR语义响应与校准后的热证据相对RGB预测的贡献，并通过竞争选择生成类相对修正请求；随后利用文本对比将修正请求转化为语义修正方向，进行证据门控特征更新。

Result: 在三个基准实验中，未见类别mIoU最高提升4.755%，严格互补转换率最高提升15.730%，同时保持有竞争力的融合质量。

Conclusion: 互补红外信息可通过类相对筛选和文本引导修正安全转化为开放词汇语义增益，验证了RGB锚定、选择性热证据转换对改善OVSS未见类别识别的有效性。

Abstract: Visible-infrared image fusion leverages complementary thermal cues to enhance visual perception, particularly under challenging illumination. However, such perceptual complementarity does not readily translate into semantic gains in open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS), where CLIP-based models are predominantly pretrained on RGB imagery. Directly introducing IR information may instead disrupt RGB-language alignment, turning promising thermal cues into semantic interference that undermines unseen-class recognition. Our pixel-level RGB-IR semantic analysis reveals that class-relevant thermal cues can correct certain RGB misclassifications while preserving reliable RGB predictions, motivating RGB-anchored, class-relative thermal cue selection. Based on this insight, we propose EmergeFuse, an infrared-visible fusion framework for OVSS that selectively converts complementary thermal evidence into unseen-class semantic corrections. Specifically, class-relative cue selection evaluates RGB-IR semantic responses and calibrated thermal evidence relative to RGB predictions, generating class-relative correction requests through competitive selection. Text-contrast image rectification then translates these selected requests into semantic rectification directions through text contrasts, guiding evidence-gated feature updates to realize complementary thermal evidence for unseen-class recognition. Experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate gains of up to 4.755% in unseen-class mIoU and 15.730% in strict complementary conversion rate over existing fusion methods, while maintaining competitive fusion quality.

</details>


### [111] [Pixel2Perspective: A Benchmark for Multi-Modal and Multi-View Generalization in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24327)
*Aditya Gandhamal,Aniruddh Sikdar,Arya Kishor,Suresh Sundaram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该摘要提出面向具身智能的开放词汇语义分割基准 Pixel2Perspective，用于跨空中与地面视角、多传感器和复杂环境条件下的鲁棒感知评估，并引入 RGB-IR 多源不确定性量化方法 MSRIQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇语义分割模型虽具备潜在应用价值，但在户外、恶劣天气、视角变化、跨域迁移和不同传感器条件下鲁棒性不足，难以直接用于无人机和自动驾驶等具身智能系统。现有视觉语言模型在真实世界应用中的评估仍不充分。

Method: 作者构建 Pixel2Perspective 基准，支持多角度目标定位与开放词汇分割，覆盖空中和地面视角、多种户外条件、仿真到真实场景以及包括红外在内的多传感器数据。该工作评估 CLIP 等基础模型和先进开放词汇分割模型，并提出 Multi-Source RGB-IR Uncertainty Quantification（MSRIQ）框架，联合建模 RGB 与红外图像中的偶然不确定性和认知不确定性。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数值结果，但强调该基准和框架能够更全面地评估开放词汇分割模型在不同视角、传感器和环境条件下的鲁棒性与泛化能力，并为开放世界具身智能感知研究提供基础性支撑和关键洞见。

Conclusion: 该研究以鲁棒性为导向，推动了开放词汇语义分割在具身智能感知中的实际应用评估。其贡献包括多视角、多传感器基准、仿真到真实与复杂环境测试设定，以及面向 RGB-IR 的不确定性量化方法。

Abstract: Embodied AI systems, such as drones and autonomous vehicles, improve navigation by enhancing perception in environments containing unseen objects. While Visual Language Models (VLMs) offer strong image understanding, their real-world application is underexplored. For effective deployment, open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) models, leveraging models like CLIP to assign pixel-level labels from text, must be robust to outdoor conditions, adverse weather, and varying viewpoints. Despite their potential, these models encounter significant challenges in cross-domain generalization, hindering their practical use in embodied AI applications. In this work, we present Pixel2Perspective, a benchmark for multi-angle object localization and open-vocabulary segmentation across both aerial and ground perspectives. This benchmark supports thorough evaluation across different viewpoints and sensor modalities. We assess foundational models like CLIP and advanced OVSS models using a curated dataset that spans varied outdoor conditions, sim-to-real scenarios, and multiple sensors, including infrared, to support the development of robust and generalizable perception systems. We also present a novel framework, Multi-Source RGB-IR Uncertainty Quantification (MSRIQ), which jointly models aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties across RGB and infrared imagery. This approach supports robust evaluation of open-vocabulary segmentation models across diverse environmental conditions. Our work sets a robustness-driven benchmark, providing key insights and laying the foundation for future research in open-world embodied AI perception. The benchmark and project page are available at this https URL

</details>


### [112] [Can Weight-Space Programming Alter Learned Ethical Priorities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24330)
*Kai Yi Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过权重编辑改变模型在“遵守承诺”与“救助更多人数”之间的权衡机制，并在文本模型和YOLO目标检测中初步验证了选择性调整学习到的优先级的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型常隐含价值权衡，例如在守约义务与更大功利收益之间如何选择。研究希望检验是否可以通过修改模型权重，有选择地改变这种权衡规则，尤其让正式承诺比一般承诺更难被功利收益推翻。

Method: 在概念监督文本模型中修改拟合的选择权重，使推翻正式承诺所需的收益高于普通承诺；在六个独立训练文本模型上测试编辑效果，并用分类器验证“正式承诺”与无条件选择测试的差异；同时在YOLO11n/COCO128上进行偏置编辑试点，并对比等价分数校准。

Result: 编辑后，模型在目标普通冲突中两种顺序均以100%选择更大群体，在目标正式冲突中为83.3%；在小规模冲突和事实准确性上保持原模型表现。正式冲突失败与受益人数读取错误相关。YOLO11n中偏置编辑将小目标匹配数从3/43提升至9/43，但等价分数校准也能复现被保留的检测结果。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种在具有显式权衡机制的模型中测试选择性优先级修改的方法，结果支持通过权重编辑实现特定价值规则调整。但该方法能否扩展到结构较弱的大型语言模型仍是开放问题。

Abstract: We investigate whether a weight edit can alter how a model trades off honoring a commitment against benefiting more people. In a concept-supervised text model, we alter fitted choice weights so that the benefit required to override a formal pledge is larger than for an ordinary promise. Across six independently trained text readers, the edit selects the larger group in both orders on 100% of targeted ordinary conflicts and 83.3% of targeted formal conflicts. It preserves the source model's success rates on smaller conflicts and its factual accuracy. The remaining formal-conflict failures coincide with errors reading a beneficiary number. A separate classifier demonstrates why this distinction matters: reusing a factual answer passes an unconditional choice test but fails to preserve small-gap promises. In a YOLO11n pilot on COCO128, a bias edit increased small-target matches from 3/43 to 9/43, and an equivalent score calibration reproduced the retained detections. These exploratory results show a way to test selective changes to learned priorities in a model with an exposed trade-off mechanism. Whether the method can discover such changes in less structured language models remains open.

</details>


### [113] [OCTR2A: Real-Time RGB-to-BEV Trajectory Projection and Forecasting at Urban Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24352)
*Emanuele Iacobelli,Simone Teglia,Francesco Pro,Claudia Melis Tonti,Irene Amerini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出OCTR2A模型，仅利用城市道路相对角落的RGB相机视频，完成交通参与者轨迹的BEV重建与未来预测，并发布R3ST++合成基准支持训练与部署微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统路口轨迹预测通常假设已有可靠的鸟瞰图运动历史，但这类数据往往依赖LiDAR、航拍、高精地图或标定基础设施，成本高且部署困难。论文希望解决从真实低成本路口视觉感知到未来轨迹预测的上游问题。

Method: 论文构建OCTR2A模块级联流程：首先在单视角RGB图像中进行目标检测与类别优化；随后利用几何约束进行跨相机轨迹匹配；再通过Transformer完成BEV轨迹投影；最后使用自中心GRU模型进行轨迹预测。训练主要采用软多尺度空间重叠损失。由于缺少公开数据支持该感知配置，作者提出R3ST++，基于SIND轨迹在Blender中生成同步相对角落视图、跨视角对应关系和BEV真值，也可作为现场微调仿真器。

Result: 在10 Hz、输入12帧并预测12帧的设置下，OCTR2A的投影到预测端到端结果为ADE/FDE 0.144/0.140至0.154/0.241米，可在单张消费级GPU上实时运行。在给定真值BEV轨迹的条件下，预测模块相比SIND最强SOTA方法在ADE/FDE上分别提升66.7%和41.7%。在TUMTraf-V2X上的迁移实验表明，R3ST++预训练有助于适应不同感知配置的真实路侧场景。

Conclusion: 论文表明仅依靠现有路口对侧RGB相机即可构建实用的BEV轨迹重建与预测管线，降低了高精度传感器和基础设施成本。OCTR2A和R3ST++为低成本路侧智能交通轨迹预测提供了可行方案与数据基准。

Abstract: Trajectory forecasting at urban intersections is often studied assuming that reliable bird's-eye-view (BEV) motion histories are available, which in practice requires costly resources such as LiDAR, aerial views, HD maps, or calibrated infrastructure. We address this upstream perception-to-forecasting problem by reconstructing and predicting agents' BEV trajectories only from paired opposite-corner RGB cameras mounted on existing urban infrastructure at variable heights at inference time. We introduce the Opposite-Corner Trajectory Reconstruction and Anticipation Architecture (OCTR2A), a modular pipeline integrating per-view detection with class refinement, geometric cross-camera matching, transformer-based BEV projection, and egocentric GRU forecasting, trained in cascade and mainly supervised with a soft multi-scale spatial-overlap loss. Since no public benchmark provides this sensing configuration, we introduce R3ST++, a Blender-based synthetic benchmark built from SIND trajectories that provides synchronized opposite-corner views, cross-view correspondences, and BEV ground truth, while also serving as a deployment-support simulator for site-specific fine-tuning. Experiments on these datasets show that, with 12 observed and 12 predicted frames at 10\,Hz, OCTR2A achieves projection~-~forecasting ADE/FDE of 0.144/0.140~-~0.154/0.241~meters in real time on a single consumer GPU. Evaluated on ground-truth BEV histories, the forecasting module improves over the strongest state-of-the-art methods on SIND by 66.7\%/41.7\% ADE/FDE. Transfer experiments on TUMTraf-V2X show that R3ST++ pretraining supports adaptation to real-world roadside domains with different sensing configurations.

</details>


### [114] [One Affinity, Two Axes: Bidirectional Slot Attention for Iterative Aggregation across the Structure--Detail Spectrum in Visual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24371)
*Zihang Wang,Wenkai Zhu,Lingdong Kong,Teng Yan,Alan Liang,Shuyuan Li,Xieyuanli Chen,Dong Kong,Guanyu Zong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出双向Slot Attention（BiSA），通过竞争轴与选择轴的跨轴共识和渐进式令牌过滤，将稠密patch tokens迭代聚合为兼具结构语义与细粒度判别性的全局描述子，在多种视觉检索任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉检索需要将大量局部patch tokens压缩为紧凑且具有判别力的全局表示。现有聚合模块多依赖单次映射，缺乏显式协调全局布局、结构分解与局部细节线索的机制，难以同时保证全局语义完整性、非冗余分解和细粒度特征选择。

Method: BiSA将token聚合重构为迭代耦合系统。其核心Cross-Axis Consensus基于共享的token-slot亲和度，沿两个互补轴同时更新可学习slot：一是竞争轴，通过跨slot共享的温度决定token归属，实现非冗余的scene decomposition；二是选择轴，通过slot专属温度控制每个slot的响应范围，使slot覆盖从宽泛布局到稀疏细粒度的多尺度信息。两个轴的共识将分解与多粒度特化耦合起来。进一步利用竞争强度与选择显著性作为内在token可靠性线索，由Progressive Token Filtering计算保留概率，在下一轮CAC前降权不可靠token，无需额外分割分支或外部先验，形成竞争分解、选择性聚焦和反馈净化的自增强循环。

Result: 实验表明BiSA在多个视觉检索基准上表现突出：在六个地点识别基准中的五个上领先，并在Baidu Mall上取得R@1提升4.2；在无人机与卫星图地理定位任务的全部16个指标上最优，150米范围R@1提升8.4；在ROxford-Hard实例检索上mAP提升4.5。选择轴仅增加20个可学习标量，却带来平均R@1提升2.8，说明细粒度响应范围调控具有显著效率收益。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了视觉检索聚合问题中“token分配竞争”与“slot多粒度选择”可共享同一亲和度矩阵并通过跨轴共识协同优化的机制。BiSA以极少额外参数引入slot特化与token可靠性反馈，在不依赖外部先验的情况下提升全局描述子的结构一致性、判别性和鲁棒性，为基于基础模型局部特征的紧凑全局聚合提供了新范式。

Abstract: Aggregating dense patch tokens into compact and discriminative global descriptors is central to visual retrieval. Although foundation models provide rich local features, existing aggregation heads still rely on single-pass mappings and lack an explicit mechanism to coordinate structural and fine-grained cues. Therefore, we propose Bidirectional Slot Attention (BiSA), which reformulates aggregation as an iterative coupled system. Cross-Axis Consensus (CAC) progressively refines learnable slots by interpreting a single shared token--slot affinity along two complementary axes. Along the competition axis, slots vie for each token under a temperature shared across all of them, determining token ownership and inducing a non-redundant scene decomposition. Along the selection axis, a slot-specific temperature governs the breadth of each slot's response, spanning a spectrum from broad layouts to sparse fine-grained details. Their consensus couples non-redundant decomposition with multi-granular slot specialization. Furthermore, the two axes expose intrinsic token-reliability cues at no extra cost: a token's claim strength under competition and its salience under selection. Progressive Token Filtering (PTF) converts these cues into a retention probability that down-weights unreliable tokens before the next CAC iteration, without any segmentation branch or external prior, forming a self-reinforcing loop of competitive decomposition, selective focusing, and feedback purification. Across all three retrieval tasks, BiSA leads on five of six place recognition benchmarks (+4.2 R@1 on Baidu Mall), tops all 16 metrics of drone--satellite geo-localization (+8.4 R@1 at 150 m), and improves instance-level retrieval on ROxford-Hard by 4.5 mAP points. Notably, the selection axis adds only 20 learnable scalars, yet improves average R@1 by 2.8 points.

</details>


### [115] [CanopyKV: Remember beyond the Crop for Fast Active Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24373)
*Lei Yang,Dayan Wu,Yiliang Zhu,Xinze Liu,Hengjie Zhu,Zihao Zhang,Ding Wang,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出CanopyKV，一种面向主动视觉推理的免训练KV缓存压缩框架，利用工具调用产生的父子裁剪区域关系，区分被局部替代的证据与仍需保留的互补信息，从而在显著压缩KV缓存、降低显存和推理成本的同时提升准确率与端到端效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动视觉推理依赖多模态模型通过反复裁剪图像、调用工具和生成推理token来收集细粒度视觉证据。但每轮图像检查和推理都会扩展key-value缓存，导致显存占用和推理成本增加。现有KV压缩方法多基于注意力、时间顺序或冗余度选择历史token，难以识别主动搜索中两类不同信息：一是当前crop已刷新覆盖的局部区域，二是早期视图仍可能为后续决策提供互补证据。若错误丢弃后者，可能损害准确率或引发额外搜索；同时，复杂评分和缓存重组又可能抵消压缩带来的计算收益。

Method: 作者提出CanopyKV框架，核心思想是利用视觉工具调用中父子crop之间的空间层级关系作为KV缓存保留信号。该框架免训练运行，不依赖额外注意力打分：通过工具元数据判断哪些历史视觉token已被当前局部crop替代，哪些属于跨区域互补证据并应继续保留。CanopyKV同时保持已保留历史缓存稳定，避免频繁重排或重写带来的开销，并保护当前推理过程所需的关键状态。最终通过紧凑KV存储降低后续注意力计算和内存访问成本。

Result: 在VisualProbe、V*、HR-Bench和MME-Lite等评测集上，CanopyKV在已评估的KV压缩方法中取得了最优的答题准确率和端到端推理效率。以MME-Lite为例，CanopyKV仅保留Dense方法约四分之一的总KV缓存，同时将准确率提升10.5个百分点，并使完整推理过程加速1.64倍，表明其在压缩率、性能保持和推理效率之间取得了较好平衡。

Conclusion: CanopyKV表明，在主动视觉推理场景中，单纯依赖注意力、近期性或冗余度的通用KV压缩策略并不足以应对工具化搜索带来的证据结构问题。通过利用父子crop关系这一训练无关、低成本且任务相关的结构信号，可以在压缩KV缓存的同时保留未来推理所需的互补视觉证据，从而实现更准确、更高效的长程视觉推理。

Abstract: Active visual reasoning enables multimodal models to answer challenging visual questions by gathering fine-grained evidence through iterative image inspection and tool use. However, accumulating crops and reasoning tokens expands the key-value (KV) cache, making compression important for reducing memory and inference costs. Existing KV compression methods typically select historical tokens based on attention, recency, or redundancy. These criteria do not explicitly distinguish locally replaced evidence from complementary information needed for future exploration: each crop refreshes a local region, while earlier views may still support subsequent decisions. Discarding such evidence can impair accuracy or require additional search, while scoring and cache reorganization can offset the computational savings from compression. We introduce CanopyKV, a training-free KV compression framework that uses parent-child crop relationships to retain complementary evidence, keeps retained history stable, and protects the state needed for ongoing reasoning. Tool metadata provides retention signals without additional attention scoring, while compact KV storage reduces subsequent attention costs. Across VisualProbe, V*, HR-Bench, and MME-Lite, CanopyKV achieves state-of-the-art answer accuracy and end-to-end efficiency among the evaluated KV compression methods. On MME-Lite, it retains approximately one-quarter of Dense's total KV while improving accuracy by 10.5 percentage points and accelerating complete reasoning by $1.64\times$.

</details>


### [116] [Where Should a Diffusion Transformer Meet Its Teacher?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24380)
*Zhuoqi Hu,Shiwei Li,Haozhao Wang,Xiandi Luo,Yihao Ouyang,Wenting Shen,Ang Wang,Jiamang Wang,Minyi Guo,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AdaREPA，一种在线自适应层路由框架，用于在扩散 Transformer 训练中动态选择最合适的特征对齐层，从而提升训练效率与生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表示对齐方法（REPA）通过预训练视觉教师监督扩散 Transformer 的中间特征，可显著加速训练；但现有方法通常固定使用某一层进行监督。随着训练推进，学生模型表示不断变化，不同层对最终收敛和生成质量的适配程度会改变，固定层监督难以持续匹配学生当前的学习需求。

Method: 作者测量流匹配目标与对齐目标之间的梯度兼容性，以刻画不同层在训练阶段对监督的有效程度。基于平滑后的层兼容性分数，AdaREPA 构造 softmax 分布，并在每个训练步按概率采样一个层作为监督层，实现在线、自适应的教师监督路由。该方法训练开销较小，且不增加推理成本。

Result: 在 ImageNet-256 上，AdaREPA 在不同模型规模下均能加速扩散 Transformer 收敛并提升生成质量，同时揭示了最适合用于监督的层会随训练过程发生迁移。

Conclusion: AdaREPA 通过在线梯度兼容性评估和概率化层路由，使教师监督能够动态适配学生表示的演化，为扩散模型训练中更高效、更灵活的表示对齐提供了一种轻量方案。

Abstract: Representation Alignment (REPA) significantly accelerates Diffusion Transformer training by guiding intermediate student features with a pretrained visual teacher, yet existing methods typically attach supervision to a single fixed layer. As student representations evolve, however, we find that the most effective layer for alignment varies across training stages. To track this evolving layer suitability online without costly search, we measure gradient compatibility between the flow-matching and alignment objectives, and propose AdaREPA, an online routing framework that adaptively selects the supervision layer as training progresses. AdaREPA uses a softmax distribution over smoothed layer compatibility scores to sample a layer for alignment at each training step. This probabilistic routing adapts teacher supervision to the student's changing learning needs, with minimal training overhead and no additional inference cost. On ImageNet-256, AdaREPA accelerates convergence and improves generation quality across model sizes, while revealing how the most suitable layers for supervision shift over training.

</details>


### [117] [Enhancing Active Learning Performance with Initial Data Selection Based on Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24384)
*Mohammad Mirzaee,Mehrshad Fallah Estalkhzir,Mohammad Reza Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种面向主动目标检测的主动学习框架，重点优化初始标注池选择：初始阶段使用YOLO-World特征聚类与objectness分数筛选，后续阶段采用一致性采样，以在低标注成本下提升目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂目标检测数据集中，标注成本高、预算有限，而初始标注池的选择会显著影响主动学习后续训练效果。传统初始周期常用不确定性采样，但检测模型在训练早期预测不可靠，因此需要更适合早期阶段的样本选择策略。

Method: 方法分为两个阶段：1）初始训练周期利用YOLO-World基础模型进行特征空间聚类，并结合objectness分数筛选图像，优先选择特征多样且基础模型较有把握的样本；2）后续周期采用基于一致性的采样策略，保持训练过程稳定。实验在Aquarium和NWPU VHR-10两个复杂数据集上评估。

Result: 在NWPU数据集上，该方法在基于图像的评估中平均提升mAP50约6.6%；基于图像的筛选方法在基于目标的评估中带来约11%的提升。结果同时表明模型性能和标注效率均有改善。

Conclusion: 将基础模型引入主动目标检测的早期周期，并通过特征聚类与objectness筛选优化初始标注池，可有效降低不确定性采样的早期噪声，提升检测性能并节省标注成本。

Abstract: In active object detection, selecting the initial labeled pool is essential, especially in complex datasets with expensive labeling and low resource budget. This study presents an active learning framework designed to optimize the initial training cycle of active learning for the object detection task. Our approach diverges from traditional methods by utilizing feature-based clustering from the YOLO-World model and refining images based on objectness scores rather than relying on uncertainty sampling in the initial cycle. This approach focuses on finding samples with various features and emphasizes training in areas where the foundation model is most confident. Doing so helps the model better understand different types of objects. For subsequent cycles, we employ a consistency-based sampling technique to maintain the robustness of the training process. To evaluate our method, we use two complex datasets called Aquarium and NWPU VHR-10, which are well known for their diverse object sizes and challenging conditions. In the NWPU dataset, our method increases mAP50 by an average of 6.6% in image-based evaluation. Moreover, the image refinement method shows an improvement of approximately 11% in object-based evaluation. The results show improvements in model performance and labeling time, demonstrating the advantages of using foundation models in early cycles of active learning. An implementation of the algorithm is available at this https URL

</details>


### [118] [RAPID: Reconstruction Accelerated by Polarization-Informed Depth for 2D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24404)
*Daisong Ouyang,Linlin Xia,Liangkun Yan,Song Gu,Shedong Zhao,Xin Dai,Yi Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RAPID 提出一种“几何优先”的高斯泼溅三维表面重建框架，利用多视图偏振线索生成稠密表面先验，从而显著加速优化并维持较高质量的渲染与网格重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯泼溅的多视图三维表面重建方法常依赖稀疏几何初始化和迭代致密化，导致优化开销大，且在反光区域和弱纹理区域难以平衡渲染质量与几何准确性。

Method: 该方法从偏振多视图输入恢复稠密表面先验：将表面法线融合到带有稀疏几何锚点的深度图中，再跨视图融合深度信息；随后通过曲率自适应采样和定向高斯初始化，实现高效的高斯泼溅优化。

Result: 在 PISR 数据集上，RAPID 相比基于高斯的先进方法 PhyGaP 实现约 5 倍加速，平均优化时间从约 21 分钟降至约 4 分钟，同时保持有竞争力的新视角合成质量和网格重建精度。

Conclusion: 偏振多视图几何先验能够有效加速高斯泼溅优化，RAPID 验证了利用偏振信息提升三维表面重建效率与几何一致性的有效性。

Abstract: Efficient and accurate 3D surface reconstruction from multi-view images remains a fundamental challenge in Gaussian splatting. Many existing methods rely on sparse geometric initialization and iterative densification, resulting in substantial optimization overhead and difficulties in balancing rendering quality and geometric accuracy, particularly in reflective and weakly textured regions. To address these limitations, we propose RAPID, a geometry-first framework that uses multiview polarization cues to recover dense surface priors. We integrate surface normals into depth maps using sparse geometric anchors. These depth maps are then fused across views. Curvature-adaptive sampling and oriented Gaussian initialization then support efficient optimization. Experiments on the PISR dataset demonstrate an approximately \(5\times\) speedup over PhyGaP, which is a state-of-the-art Gaussian-based reconstruction method. RAPID reduces the average optimization time from approximately 21 minutes (PhyGaP) to 4 minutes while maintaining competitive novel-view synthesis quality and mesh reconstruction accuracy. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of RAPID in accelerating Gaussian optimization from polarimetric multi-view inputs.

</details>


### [119] [StrideWorld: Benchmarking Scene Rigidity and Action Consistency in Interactive Video World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24408)
*Chuang Wang,Rui Nie,Jie Song,Junyuan Gao,Jiahe Song,Haitao Zhou,Boyu Zhu,Xing Li,Jiang Wu,Conghui He,Qian Yu,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 StrideWorld，一个用于评估交互式视频世界模型的基准，重点检测场景在相机运动过程中的3D结构刚性与用户动作对相机位移的一致性；其关键设计是使用在测试前固定、不随被测视频拟合的参考标准，从而避免传统基准吸收渐进式场景形变、节奏漂移和路径长度误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频世界模型基准通常依赖与被测视频本身对齐的参考，例如从被测视频帧重建的场景，或按指令路径进行尺度对齐的相机轨迹。这样的参考容易被被测视频“适应”，导致模型缓慢改变场景几何、动作速度或路径长度等错误被掩盖。因此需要更客观、固定的评估标准，以真实揭示场景结构不稳定和动作执行不一致的问题。

Method: 作者提出 StrideWorld 基准，从两个维度评估模型：第一，场景刚性，以起始视图的真实几何作为固定参考，比较相机移动后场景是否保持稳定的3D结构；第二，动作一致性，以每个指令动作对应的“stride”（一次命令产生的相机位移）为参考，先在被测模型的第一步中测量 stride，再用该 stride 检验后续步骤，使模型只与自身先前动作行为比较，避免外部尺度对齐造成偏差。作者还在已知相机运动的渲染视频上验证指标有效性，并与人工判断进行对比。实验覆盖多个开源模型、多个起始视图和大量生成视频；原文中视频数量与模型数量存在 LaTeX 占位符，未明确给出具体数值。作者还尝试用少量渲染片段微调两个模型。

Result: 实验显示，除一个模型外，其余模型在相机移动时都会发生场景形变，其形变偏移最高可达沿同一相机路径刚性渲染结果的7.3倍。在单个动作被持续执行时，多数模型能保持较稳定节奏；但在组合路径中，多个模型常出现停滞，且大多数完成路径的终点偏离由各模型自身 stride 预测的位置。只有两个根据估计场景深度缩放动作的模型能将大多数路径终点拉近到预期位置。对两个模型进行少量渲染片段微调后，可显著减少路径中途停滞问题。

Conclusion: StrideWorld 通过固定且预先设定的参考标准，更严格、更可靠地评估交互式视频世界模型的3D结构稳定性和动作一致性，揭示了现有模型在长时间相机运动和组合指令下容易出现的渐进形变、停滞和位移预测偏差。该基准具有与人工判断一致的评估价值，同时表明场景深度估计与轻微微调可改善动作执行稳定性；相关代码、数据和排行榜已公开。

Abstract: Interactive video world models turn user commands into the exploration of a generated scene, which requires the scene to keep its 3D structure as the camera moves and each action to keep a stable effect. Existing benchmarks mostly evaluate these properties against references fitted to the video under test, either a scene reconstructed from its own frames or a camera trajectory aligned to the command path with a free scale. Such references can absorb gradual scene deformation and changes in pace and route length, so these errors can pass undetected. We therefore introduce StrideWorld, a benchmark whose references are fixed before the motion they judge. It evaluates scene rigidity against the ground-truth geometry of the start view, and action consistency against the stride of each action, the camera motion of one command. The stride is measured on the model's first steps and then used to test the steps that follow, so each model is judged only against its own steps. Its measurements are validated on rendered videos with known camera motion, and its scores agree with human judgments. Across \nvideos{} videos generated by \nmodels{} open models from $140$ start views, all models but one deform their scenes as the camera moves, with offsets up to $7.3$ times those of a rigid rendering along the same camera path. Most models keep a steady pace when a single action is held. Along composed routes, several models often stall, and most completed routes end away from the positions predicted by each model's own strides. Only the two models that scale commands by the estimated scene depth bring most routes close to these positions. Fine-tuning two models on a few rendered clips removes most of their mid-route stalls. Code, data, and a leaderboard are publicly available at this https URL.

</details>


### [120] [Why Multi-Source Audio Fails to Compose: A Geometric Diagnosis and Its Remedy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24410)
*Anurag Singh,Prajwal Singh,Shanmuganathan Raman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出“几何位移”框架，解释多源音频到图像生成中的组合性失败：音频标记在CLIP文本标记空间中位置偏移、语义锚定不足，导致扩散模型难以有效关注并定位不同声源。作者通过分析发现CLAP相比BEATs在投影后空间更具聚类紧凑性、语义对齐性和标记间分离性，并据此提出无需重训扩散主干的多标记方法与推理期目标，以提升多源声图对齐与生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源音频驱动图像生成常出现声源混淆、定位错误和组合语义失效。作者认为关键问题不在生成模型本身，而在跨模态投影后的表示几何：不同音频源的标记可能被映射到CLIP文本空间中彼此靠近但偏离自然语言文本流形的区域，使U-Net缺乏先验去关注它们或将它们绑定到不同图像区域。因此需要从表示空间几何角度诊断并修正多源音频条件建模。

Method: 作者首先通过两级聚类分析比较BEATs与CLAP在原始编码层和投影到CLIP文本标记层后的几何性质，重点考察类内紧凑性、与CLIP文本流形的角度对齐以及标记间空间分离。随后提出多标记扩展：将每个音频源分配到CLIP tokenizer中的不同占位符标记，且无需重新训练扩散主干。推理阶段设计混合目标，结合Attend-and-Excite机制与基于A*的空间分离损失，以缓解残余的跨标记干扰。实验基于VGGSound构建VGG2、VGG3和VGGN多音频组合基准，并扩展六项标准音频-图像指标到多源设置。

Result: CLAP在所有三项几何性质上显著优于BEATs，silhouette score提升超过3倍，同时Audio-Image Content score也得到改善。在五个基准和四个强基线的比较中，该方法在与组合忠实度最相关的指标上表现突出：相比最强基线，AIC最高提升2.25倍；在两声源生成任务中，FID降低接近2倍。结果表明改进音频条件标记的几何结构能够显著提升多源生成一致性与图像质量。

Conclusion: 几何位移现象可作为理解并诊断跨模态生成系统失败的重要框架，尤其适用于将学习到的音频或视觉嵌入投影到预训练对比空间如CLIP的场景。该工作表明，通过优化投影后标记的空间紧凑性、语义对齐性和分离性，并在推理期加强多源标记的注意力与空间解耦，可在不重训主干模型的情况下提升多源音频到图像生成的组合能力。

Abstract: Multi-source audio-to-image generation exhibits consistent compositional failures when audio tokens occupy geometrically displaced, semantically unanchored positions in the CLIP text-token space, where the diffusion U-Net lacks the prior to reliably attend to them or ground them in distinct spatial regions. We formalize this as the geometric displacement phenomenon and expose it through a two-level clustering analysis comparing BEATs and CLAP embeddings at the raw encoder and projected CLIP token levels. Three geometric properties emerge as important: intra-class compactness, angular alignment with the CLIP text manifold, and inter-token spatial separation. We show that CLAP, whose contrastive language-audio pretraining aligns embeddings with natural language semantics, substantially outperforms BEATs on all three properties, improving the silhouette score by more than 3x and the Audio-Image Content score as well. Building on this analysis, we introduce a multi-token extension that requires no diffusion-backbone retraining and assigns each audio source to a distinct placeholder token in the CLIP tokenizer, complemented by a mixed inference-time objective that combines Attend-and-Excite with an A* spatial separation loss to resolve residual cross-token interference. For evaluation, we construct multi-audio compositional benchmarks from VGGSound (VGG2, VGG3, and VGGN) and extend six standard audio-image metrics to the multi-source setting. Across five benchmarks and four strong baselines, our method achieves strong results on the metrics most directly tied to compositional faithfulness, with AIC improvements of up to 2.25x over the strongest baseline and FID reduced by nearly 2x on two-source generation. We propose geometric displacement as a diagnostic framework for cross-modal generation systems that project learned embeddings into a pretrained contrastive space.

</details>


### [121] [PhysResponse: Probing the Physics of Video Generation with Controlled Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24419)
*Yuhan Li,Shuran Zhang,Haixin Tian,Baonuo Rao,Weipeng Li,Yujia Hou,Zhenyu Wang,Zijun Wang,Luoyang Lin,Lijing Luo,Jiehui Huang,Kaiyuan Yang,Jingbin Tan,Panwen Hu,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出PhysResponse基准，用于评估视频生成模型对指定物理参数的响应能力，而不仅是生成外观逼真的运动；通过参数反演和临界事件评测发现现有模型缺陷，并构造带受控物理参数的模拟视频数据用于微调提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型被视为世界模拟器，需要运动不仅看起来合理，还应正确响应重力、摩擦、初速度等指定物理参数。然而现有模型、数据集和评测主要关注物理合理性，缺少对物理参数响应性的系统评估与训练信号。

Method: 提出PhysResponse两级评测：1）场景条件模拟器反演，从生成视频中恢复有效重力、摩擦和初始速度，度量参数误差；2）临界事件评估，测试物理状态接近或跨越临界转变时的结果一致性。进一步构建72,000个具有20种多物体交互类型的受控参数模拟视频，对图像到视频模型进行微调，并尝试加入数值编码器进行继续训练。

Result: 对10个图像到视频模型的评测显示，即使最低参数误差仍比物理正确视频高一个数量级以上；模型很少同时复现临界转变对的两类结果；两级评测暴露出PSNR等视觉指标难以捕捉的互补性失败。在模拟数据上微调可降低参数误差，并泛化到保留交互类型、真实视频和外部物理基准；使用数值编码器继续训练进一步降低三类误差。

Conclusion: PhysResponse为评估视频生成模型是否真正遵循物理条件提供了基础。结果显示当前模型仍主要依赖外观与统计相关性，而非参数化物理规律；受控模拟数据和数值化训练可作为提升世界模拟器物理可控性的重要路径。

Abstract: Video generation models are increasingly viewed as world simulators, which requires not only plausible motion but dynamics that respond correctly to specified physical parameters. Existing models, datasets and benchmarks, however, largely target physical plausibility, leaving this physical parameter responsiveness insufficiently explored. We introduce PhysResponse, a benchmark that measures it at two levels: scene-conditioned simulator inversion recovers the effective gravity, friction and initial speed of generated motion, and event evaluation tests outcomes near and across critical transitions. Evaluating ten image-to-video models, we find that even the lowest parameter errors exceed those of physically correct videos by over an order of magnitude, that models rarely reproduce both outcomes of a transition pair, and that the two levels expose complementary failures that visual metrics such as PSNR miss. To supply the missing training signal, we construct 72,000 simulated videos of 20 multi-object interaction types with controlled parameters; fine-tuning on them lowers parameter errors, even on held-out interaction types, and transfers to real videos and an external physics benchmark. Continued training with a numerical encoder further lowers all three errors on held-out types. PhysResponse provides a foundation for world simulators that follow physical conditions, not just appearances.

</details>


### [122] [Let a Small Model Read First: Faster Prefill for Long Interleaved Image-Text Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24431)
*Xiaolin Lin,Kai-Chiang Wu,Bing Li,Grace Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SkimPrefill，利用一个小型混合模型中的单个早期注意力头快速检索长图文输入中的关键证据单元，从而显著加速视觉语言模型处理长文档或多图输入时的 prefill 阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在回答关于长交错输入的问题时，如多页文档或大量图片集合，pre 处理长输入本身会带来显著延迟，尤其在约 10 万 token 的输入规模下，等待第一个输出 token 的时间主要被 prefill 占据。现有通过目标模型或同族小模型对 token 进行评分并剪枝的方法虽然能减少输入长度，但评分过程本身耗时过长，抵消了剪枝带来的收益。

Method: 作者发现，问题的证据通常集中在少数完整语义单元中，例如单个图片或段落，而不是分散在所有 token 中。基于此，提出 SkimPrefill：首先让小型混合模型 Qwen3.5-0.8B 以图像半分辨率读取完整输入，仅计算到模型早期某一层；由于该小模型的大部分层具有线性时间复杂度，这一快速阅读过程开销较低。随后，使用早期层中的一个特定注意力头对每个图文单元进行评分和排序，并将排名靠前的单元作为普通较短 prompt 输入给目标大模型。该选择器无需针对每个目标模型训练，可跨不同模型族使用。

Result: 在 MMLongBench 的 128K token 长输入评测上，覆盖 19 个数据集和 5 类任务。实验表明，当仅保留 10% 输入 token 时，SkimPrefill 能在基本保持目标模型平均准确率的同时显著加速 prefill：使 Qwen3.5-9B、Gemma-4-12B 和 Qwen3-VL-32B 的 prefill 分别加速 3.7 倍、11.6 倍和 8.2 倍。

Conclusion: SkimPrefill 通过单元级证据检索替代昂贵的 token 级评分剪枝，实现了长视觉语言输入下高效、可扩展的 prefill 加速。该方法无需训练选择器，并可跨模型族使用，为缓解 VLM 处理长文档或多图输入时的首 token 延迟提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can answer questions about long interleaved inputs, such as documents of many pages and collections of hundreds of images, but they are slow to start: on inputs of about 100K tokens, the prefill takes most of the wait for the first output token. A common approach prunes the input by scoring its tokens with the target model itself or with a small model of the same family that first writes an answer. However, this scoring takes much of the time that pruning saves. We find that the evidence for a question lies in a few whole units (images or paragraphs), and that a small hybrid model finds them with one attention head of an early layer, even at half image resolution. Based on these findings, we propose SkimPrefill, which lets the small hybrid model read the whole input first, up to that early layer, with images at half resolution. Most layers of the small hybrid model (Qwen3.5-0.8B) run in linear time, so this pass is short. SkimPrefill then ranks every unit with that head and gives the target the top units as an ordinary shorter prompt. One untrained selector serves targets of different model families. Extensive experiments on MMLongBench at 128K tokens, with 19 datasets of five task types, show the effectiveness of SkimPrefill. With 10% of the input tokens kept, SkimPrefill speeds up the prefill of Qwen3.5-9B, Gemma-4-12B and Qwen3-VL-32B by 3.7 times, 11.6 time and 8.2 times while maintaining their average accuracy.

</details>


### [123] [MLIE-CoT: Chain-of-Thought Reasoning via Multi-Level Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24433)
*Baichuan Yang,Dongyuan Xu,Zhao Yang,Yong Liu,Bing Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 MLIE-CoT，一种基于多层级图像编辑的视觉思维链框架，通过选择合适粒度的图像修复策略，在文生图推理中保留正确语义、减少错误图像累积和幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向文生图的 CoT 方法主要依赖文本推理，难以充分利用图像中已存在的正确语义；将整幅图像作为上下文持续传递时，早期错误图像会干扰模型，且反复整体重生成会引入并累积新的幻觉。

Method: MLIE-CoT 让模型在多三个修复层级中选择合适的图像编辑粒度，尽可能保留正确内容并局部修正错误；同时构建覆盖不同错误情形的图像修复数据集，用于训练修复层级路由模型，使不同严重程度的错误被分配到相应的修复层级。

Result: 大量实验表明，MLIE-CoT 在多个基准上显著优于基础模型以及采用固定修复策略的方法。

Conclusion: 通过可学习的多层级图像编辑与错误路由机制，MLIE-CoT 能在视觉思维链生成中更好地保留有效语义、抑制错误传播和幻觉累积，从而提升复杂视觉推理生成能力。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has proven effective in improving the reasoning abilities of large language models by breaking complex tasks into step-by-step solutions. In recent years, researchers have attempted to extend CoT reasoning to visual generative models to improve their ability to handle complex tasks. However, existing CoT methods for text-to-image generation still rely mainly on textual reasoning and therefore cannot fully use and preserve the correct semantic information already present in an image, as even the most detailed language cannot capture all visual information. With advances in image generation, models such as FLUX and BAGEL can generate images conditioned on visual inputs. Recent work has attempted to carry images forward as context in the chain of thought, but this introduces another limitation: unsuitable earlier images can strongly interfere with the model, while repeated whole-image regeneration introduces new hallucinations at each round and causes them to accumulate. To address these issues, we propose MLIE-CoT, a visual chain-of-thought framework based on multi-level image editing. MLIE-CoT learns to select the repair granularity from three levels, preserving correct semantics whenever possible during reasoning while reducing the effect of incorrect content. To improve the ability of multimodal models to route errors of different severity to appropriate repair levels, we construct an image-repair dataset covering different error cases and use it to train a repair-level router. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MLIE-CoT significantly outperforms both its base models and methods using fixed repair strategies, producing clear improvements across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [124] [Retroactive Concept Bottleneck Models for Exemplar-Free Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24445)
*Yang Ji,Ying Sun,Yuanxin Zhuang,Yuting Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RetroCBM，用于无示例类增量可解释概念瓶颈模型：通过保存少量与词汇无关的特征统计量，使新概念能够反向支持旧类预测，并抑制概念误差造成的错误分类边界偏移，在六个数据集上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无示例类增量学习要求模型在不保留旧任务训练样本的情况下持续学习新类别。连续概念瓶颈模型能通过识别图像中的视觉概念并对类别打分实现可解释预测，但现有方法通常侧重于保留已学概念或用生成特征近似旧类，导致新学到的概念难以有效贡献于旧类预测，限制增量学习性能。

Method: RetroCBM保留一小部分与词汇无关的统计信息，用于连接冻结的视觉特征与预训练视觉语言模型的嵌入空间。即使早期训练图像被丢弃，这些统计量仍可监督新概念。方法提出一种回溯式回归规则，使每个新概念得到的预测器等价于在所有已见图像上进行类平衡岭回归的结果。随后利用重新投影的统计量恢复全历史平方分类损失，学习新概念对旧类预测的贡献。进一步，由于分类器可能利用系统性概念误差区分相邻类别，引入图加权惩罚项来约束这些误差造成的边距偏移。

Result: 在六个数据集的类增量设置中，RetroCBM均优于现有最先进的可解释方法，相比最强无示例基线，在平均增量准确率上取得18.1%的相对提升。

Conclusion: 在可解释类增量学习中，仅保存紧凑的历史统计信息即可恢复旧数据的监督价值，使新概念参与旧类预测，并降低概念误差对分类边界的负面影响。RetroCBM兼顾可解释性、无示例存储要求和增量识别性能，是连续概念瓶颈学习的一种有效框架。

Abstract: Exemplar-free class-incremental learning (EFCIL) requires models to learn new classes sequentially without retaining training samples from previous tasks. Continual concept bottleneck models (CBMs) make their predictions interpretable by identifying visual concepts in an image and using them to score each class. However, these methods typically focus on preserving learned concepts or approximating past classes with generated features, which can leave new concepts underused in old-class predictions. We propose RetroCBM, which retains a small set of vocabulary-independent statistics relating frozen visual features to the embedding space of a pretrained vision-language model. Even after earlier training images have been discarded, we can use these statistics to supervise new concepts. To do so, we develop a retroactive regression rule that yields the same predictor for each new concept as class-balanced ridge regression on all images seen so far. We then reproject the stored statistics to recover the full-history squared classification loss and thus learn how much each new concept should contribute to old-class predictions. Since the classifier may use systematic concept errors to distinguish nearby classes, we further introduce a graph-weighted penalty on the margin shifts caused by those errors. Across six datasets, RetroCBM outperforms state-of-the-art interpretable methods in all class-incremental settings, with an 18.1\% relative gain in mean average incremental accuracy over the strongest exemplar-free baseline.

</details>


### [125] [EviScope: Evidence-Conditioned Sampling for Long-Range 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24451)
*Mingyang Lyu,Yinqian Sun,Gongce Cao,Chujie Tang,Yuhao Zhang,Lu Jia,Qian Zhang,Bo Wang,Pin Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该摘要提出并评估了一个极简模块 EviScope：在基于查询的相机-雷达检测器中，利用当前帧雷达回波构建非学习式的鸟瞰证据场，让每个目标查询在其解码中心读取该证据，并据此调整径向采样范围和跨帧权重。模块仅增加 5,450 个参数且未增加推理延迟。实验表明，查询读取到的证据与其目标雷达回波数量正相关，但相对自身基线的端到端性能增益较小且不稳定；相较参数匹配的几何条件控制器则有较一致的优势。作者强调，只有严格匹配的端到端比较才能判断该机制的真实贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长距离（如 MAN TruckScenes 的 150 米范围）自动驾驶感知中，相机-雷达检测器的召回率会随目标级别雷达回波数量在不同距离段发生显著变化。传统基于查询的相机-雷达检测器通常根据查询位置或特征决定“在哪里采样”以及“在哪些帧上采样”。作者试图检验：除位置和特征外，查询周围雷达证据本身是否也应参与决定采样策略，即雷达可见性/回波强度能否作为自适应采样半径和多帧聚合权重的信号。

Method: 作者提出 EviScope，一种最小化测试框架。其核心流程为：1）使用当前帧的雷达回波构造一个非学习式的鸟瞰图证据场；2）每个目标查询根据解码中心位置读取该证据值；3）利用读取到的证据缩放查询的径向采样范围，并对八帧时序信息进行加权。该方法被集成到 RaCFormer 中，仅增加 5,450 个参数，占模型参数约 0.009%，且实验中未增加推理延迟。由于作者提出的无模块基线配方在 TruckScenes 上的 mAP 已超过已发表结果（25.9 vs. 24.1，七次运行），因此模块贡献只与同种子条件下的自身基线进行比较，而非与外部已发表基线比较。实验还使用参数数量相同的几何-only 条件模块作为对照，并开展多轮配对训练和预指定的保留测试。

Result: 实验结果显示，查询读取到的雷达证据量与其对应目标真实雷达回波数量存在正相关，Spearman 相关系数为 0.51，说明模块确实能部分感知目标雷达可见性；但该证据场只能部分捕捉零回波目标。学习得到的采样半径与读取证据之间呈强负相关，四次运行中 ρ ≈ -0.91，表明证据越强的查询倾向于采用更小的径向采样范围。然而，在七组四 epoch 配对实验中，相对自身基线，NDS 仅提升 +0.31±0.36，mAP 下降 -0.27±0.22，整体增益不稳定。与参数匹配的几何-only 对照相比，EviScope 在三组匹配种子下平均高 +1.25±0.72 NDS 和 +0.87±0.21 mAP，且每组 mAP 均更高；而对照的平均 NDS 接近基线。探索性的远距增益未通过预先指定的保留集检验；推理时代替性替换也会产生较大性能惩罚，但这并非仅由查询局部证据导致。

Conclusion: EviScope 证明了查询周围雷达证据确实能被检测器学习并利用：证据与目标回波数量相关，且学习到的采样半径会随证据调整。同时，相对于参数匹配的纯几何条件控制器，该模块表现出更一致的相对优势，说明雷达证据信息具有一定价值。但相对于作者自身强基线，端到端性能提升较小且不稳定，不能仅凭模块相关性或推理时依赖性宣称其有效。该工作强调，在此类复杂感知模型中，评估自适应采样机制必须依赖严格匹配的训练条件和端到端对比，而非单独分析模块行为或外部基线数字。

Abstract: Query-based camera-radar detectors choose where and over which frames each object query samples from the query's position or feature. We test whether radar evidence around the query should also set these choices at the 150 m range of MAN TruckScenes, where recall differs across object-level radar-return counts within every range band. EviScope is a minimal test: the current frame's returns form a non-learned bird's-eye evidence field, which each query reads at its decoded centre to scale its radial sampling extent and to weight eight frames. It adds 5,450 parameters (0.009%) to RaCFormer, with no latency increase in our timing. Our module-free recipe exceeds the published TruckScenes mAP (25.9 over seven runs against 24.1), so we measure the module only against its own baseline at matched seeds. The evidence a query reads rises with its object's return count (Spearman 0.51) but captures zero-return objects only in part, and the learned radius tracks it ($\rho\approx-0.91$ in four runs). Over seven four-epoch pairs the module changes NDS by $+0.31\pm0.36$ and mAP by $-0.27\pm0.22$ (mean $\pm$ SE). Against a geometry-only conditioner with the same parameter count, trained at three matched seeds, it is higher by $+1.25\pm0.72$ NDS and $+0.87\pm0.21$ mAP, on mAP in every pair, and the control's mean NDS is near the baseline's. An exploratory far-range gain failed an internally pre-specified held-out test, and large inference-time substitution penalties also occur without query-local evidence: neither learned allocation nor inference-time dependence substitutes for matched end-to-end comparisons.

</details>


### [126] [ShoWorld: Shape-Faithful Multi-Hypothesis Occupancy World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24457)
*Zhiqiang Yan,Guangfa Wang,Yuan Wu,Jian Yang,Gim Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该摘要提出ShoWorld，通过将占用世界模型中的运动预测与形状预测解耦，以缓解移动物体预测中的运动不确定性和形状失真问题，并在Occ3D-nuScenes上取得当前最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占用世界模型在预测移动物体时面临两大挑战：一是运动不确定性导致单一轨迹可能把多个合理未来平均成一条不合理的轨迹；二是重复重新体素化会即使运动正确也会使物体形状被侵蚀或膨胀。

Method: ShoWorld将预测分为运动分量和形状分量。运动预测器根据场景布局、附近智能体和未来自车轨迹预测概率性轨迹；每个轨迹假设被渲染为占用体素，并按概率融合以保留多种可能未来。形状预测器基于局部几何预测子体素点，包括占用概率和几何偏移；这些点随物体刚性移动，体素占用由其中是否存在被占据点决定，从而降低运动过程中的形状失真。

Result: 在Occ3D-nuScenes数据集上，ShoWorld达到state-of-the-art结果。在给定真实占用和真实自车轨迹的条件下，整体mIoU提升6.56个点，IoU提升9.16个点；在动态类别上，mIoU提升6.06个点，IoU提升5.53个点。

Conclusion: 将运动预测与形状表示分开建模，有助于更准确地预测移动物体的未来状态，并在保持形状完整性的同时提升占用预测性能。

Abstract: Occupancy world models struggle to forecast moving objects due to motion uncertainty and shape distortion. A single trajectory can average plausible futures into an implausible path, and repeated re-voxelization can erode or inflate object shape even under correct motion. We introduce ShoWorld to address these challenges through separate motion and shape representations. The motion forecaster predicts probabilistic trajectories conditioned on scene layout, nearby agents and the future ego trajectory. Each hypothesis is rendered into occupancy and fused according to its probability to retain alternative futures. The shape component predicts sub-voxel points with occupancy probabilities and offsets from local geometry. These points move rigidly with the object, and voxel occupancy follows the probability that at least one occupied point falls within it. This representation reduces shape distortion under motion. ShoWorld achieves state-of-the-art results on Occ3D-nuScenes. With ground-truth occupancy and ego trajectory, it improves mIoU and IoU by 6.56 and 9.16 points overall and by 6.06 and 5.53 points on dynamic classes.

</details>


### [127] [Every Query Routes Itself: Accelerating Video Diffusion Transformers with Per-Query Routed Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24479)
*Zhen Huang,Shuwei Li,Ruizhe Yao,Xinrui Chen,Danyi Liu,Haoyuan Liang,Zhiyu Ye,Qiuyan Sun,Bingwei Chen,Yiyuan Li,Xu Zhou,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 PQRS：一种免训练的逐查询路由稀疏注意力方法，仅对键进行聚类并让每个查询独立选择键簇，从而在视频扩散 Transformer 中兼顾长序列推理速度与接近稠密注意力的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散 Transformer 中的自注意力在长 token 序列上开销巨大。现有基于聚类的稀疏注意力会同时对查询和键聚类，并让同一查询簇共享键簇选择，但作者发现这种共享会限制精度；当键簇和稀疏密度固定时，查询簇越细，注意力越准确，最准确的情况是每个查询独立选择键簇。

Method: 提出 Per-Query Routed Sparse Attention（PQRS）。该方法免训练，只对键进行聚类，让每个查询独立选择自己的键簇，并用未选中键簇的质心进行补偿。为解决逐查询选择导致无法按查询瓦片共享键列表的问题，PQRS 改为遍历键簇而非查询，将具有相同选择结果的查询归入密集计算任务，并利用上一步去噪中继承的固定 softmax 偏移，在一次遍历中合并部分结果。

Result: 在 Wan 和 HunyuanVideo 家族的四个文生视频与图生视频模型上评估，PQRS 是所有被评测稀疏注意力方法中速度最快的，并且输出最接近稠密注意力。具体地，在 Wan2.1-T2V 上达到 28.9 dB PSNR，在 HunyuanVideo 上达到 35.4 dB PSNR，端到端加速比为 1.66–2.10 倍。

Conclusion: PQRS 通过“只聚类键、逐查询路由、质心补偿和相同路由查询批量合并”的设计，有效缓解了共享查询聚类带来的稀疏注意力精度损失，同时显著降低视频扩散模型的推理成本，为高质量、低开销的视频生成注意力加速提供了一种实用的免训练方案。

Abstract: Self-attention over long token sequences dominates the inference cost of video diffusion transformers. Clustering-based sparse attention clusters both queries and keys and lets all queries in a cluster share one selection of key clusters. We show that this sharing limits accuracy: with key clusters and density fixed, attention grows more accurate as the query clusters are refined, and is most accurate when each query selects on its own, which needs no query clustering at all. We therefore propose Per-Query Routed Sparse Attention (PQRS), a training-free method that clusters only keys, lets each query select its own key clusters, and compensates for the unselected ones with their centroids. Per-query selections leave no query tile with a shared key list, so PQRS traverses key clusters instead of queries, buckets queries with identical selections into dense tasks, and merges their partial results in one pass under a fixed softmax offset carried from the previous denoising step. On four text-to-video and image-to-video models from the Wan and HunyuanVideo families, PQRS is the fastest of all the sparse attention methods we evaluate and the closest to the dense output, reaching $28.9$ dB PSNR on Wan2.1-T2V and $35.4$ dB on HunyuanVideo at $1.66$--$2.10\times$ end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [128] [Comparing Each Generation with Independent Siblings Gives Video World Model Post-Training a Proper Objective for Outcome Frequencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24512)
*Xin Li,Yiwen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 SibPO（Sibling-paired Policy Optimization），用于让视频世界模型在物理后训练后不仅生成单条“更像录像”的未来，还能在重复采样时匹配真实物理结果的概率分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型若被用作模拟器，当同一初始帧对应多个可能物理结果时，多次生成应按结果在数据中的真实频率出现。传统物理后训练通常把每次生成与唯一记录的未来比较，这虽然提高单条视频保真度，但期望目标关于模型分布是线性的，最终会偏向只生成最常见结果，造成分布失真或模式坍塌。

Method: 作者从记录结果的 log-likelihood 出发，其梯度可按模型生成某结果的频率的倒数加权；同一历史条件下独立生成的“兄弟”样本可用于估计该频率。SibPO 利用兄弟样本估计截断的逆频率权重，一方面鼓励与真实记录一致，另一方面减去同一权重下与兄弟样本的一致性，使期望目标在任意组大小下对应于结果分格上的严格真散度，从而训练模型输出正确的分布。

Result: 在 PisaBench 上，SibPO 在四个视频模型中的三个上，将预测落点时间的 KS 距离相对其奖励家族外最强方法降低 9.1%-10.2%，且没有损害单条视频质量；同时将估计的机器人策略成功率误差降低 13%。

Conclusion: SibPO 通过兄弟样本估计逆频率权重并构造严格真散度目标，使视频世界模型在保持单视频物理真实性的同时，能够匹配多结果概率分布，更适合作为模拟器和下游策略评估使用。

Abstract: Video world models are used as simulators, so when a first frame leaves several physical outcomes open, repeated generations should produce each outcome as often as it occurs in the data. Physics post-training scores every generation against the single recorded future of its history, which makes single videos more faithful, but the expected score is linear in the model distribution and is maximized by always producing the most likely outcome. The log-likelihood of the recorded outcome is maximized by the data distribution, and its gradient weights agreement with the record by the inverse of how often the model produces the generated outcome, a frequency that other generations of the same history with independent noise, its siblings, can estimate. To train with this weight from a finite group, we propose SibPO, which performs Sibling-paired Policy Optimization: siblings estimate a truncated inverse-frequency weight for agreement with the record, and a second term subtracts the same weight times the agreement with the siblings, which makes the expected objective a strictly proper divergence over outcome cells at every group size. On PisaBench, SibPO lowers the KS distance to simulated landing times by 9.1-10.2% relative to the strongest method outside its reward family on three of four video models without degrading single videos, and it lowers the error of estimated robot policy success rates by 13%.

</details>


### [129] [RAID: Representation Autoencoder for Intrinsic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24521)
*Claudia Cuttano,Tamy Boubekeur,Elena Garces,Giuseppe Vecchio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 RAID，一种基于 DINOv3 预训练表征的单图像内禀分解方法，通过将“重建”与“分解”解耦，在紧凑潜空间中一次性预测多种内禀属性，避免了扩散模型所需的大规模迭代生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像内禀分解是强病态问题，需要从单张 RGB 图像中解耦相互耦合的外观因素，如反射率、光照、阴影等。现有方法依赖扩散先验来缓解歧义，但模型大、推理迭代、成本高，且常具有随机性。论文试图证明：高质量内禀分解并不必然依赖大型生成模型。

Method: 方法核心是在强预训练表征基础上解耦重建与分解。首先训练一个 teacher 模型，将各内禀图经 DINOv3 特征通过共享 decoder 重构回该内禀图本身，即 X→X 重建，而不需要成对的 RGB-to-intrinsic 监督。然后训练基于 DINOv3 的 student 模型，从 RGB 图像预测对应 teacher 的潜空间表征，并通过轻量级、模态特定投影实现分解。该设计允许使用部分标注数据训练，并在单次前向传播中预测所有内禀属性。

Result: 所得 RAID 模型仅约 1.65 亿参数，推理速度比扩散方法快约 100 倍，同时取得有竞争力甚至更好的内禀分解质量，并对不同输入分辨率表现出更强鲁棒性。学习到的投影还形成可解释的模态特定特征空间。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，利用强预训练视觉表征并将重建与分解解耦，可以在不依赖大型扩散生成模型的情况下实现高效、鲁棒的单图像内禀分解。这为紧凑型内在图像分解提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Single-image intrinsic decomposition is an inherently ill-posed problem, requiring the disentanglement of strongly coupled appearance factors from a single RGB observation. Recent methods address this ambiguity with diffusion priors, at the cost of large, iterative, and often stochastic models. We show that accurate decomposition does not require a large generative model. Our key idea is to decouple reconstruction from decomposition on top of a strong pretrained representation. First, an $X \rightarrow X$ teacher learns to reconstruct each intrinsic map from DINOv3 features using a shared decoder, without paired RGB-to-intrinsic supervision. Then, a DINOv3-based student predicts the corresponding teacher representations from RGB through lightweight, modality-specific projections, enabling training on partially annotated datasets and predicting all intrinsics in a single forward pass. \textbf{RAID}, predicts intrinsic properties in the structured latent space, enabling a compact 165M-parameter model, 100x faster than diffusion-based methods. Despite its compact size, it achieves competitive or superior decomposition quality, with improved robustness to input resolution. Additionally, our learned projections yield interpretable, modality-specific feature spaces.

</details>


### [130] [SCRiD: Spatial and Contextual References for Generalizable AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24524)
*Hao Tan,Zekai Zuo,Boyang Chen,Jinghui Qin*
