Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 SCRiD，通过空间残差线索与真实视频条件化去噪线索的综合建模，提升 AI 生成视频检测在跨生成器、跨任务和后处理扰动下的泛化鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可泛化 AI 生成视频检测面临两个核心难题：一是自然视频中的运动和内容变化可能与合成伪影高度相似，导致单一视频内的局部异常不足以可靠判断真伪；二是真实伪造边界往往依赖上下文参照，而不同生成器、任务和后处理会使伪影表现剧烈变化，因此检测器容易过拟合特定源域特征，难以跨域泛化。作者希望利用互补的“空间参照”和“上下文参照”来更稳定地解释视频变化，从而提升检测泛化能力。

Method: 论文提出 Spatial and Contextual Reference-informed Detection（SCRiD）框架，将视频变化置于两类参照系中进行分析。其一是 Matched-Coordinate Residual Response（MCR），通过匹配坐标和亚像素采样条件下的运动与坐标参照，刻画时间残差之间的关联，从而抑制自然运动变化带来的伪差异干扰。其二是 Real-Conditioned Score Adaptation（RCSA），从真实视频中学习内容条件化的去噪参照，并在多噪声尺度和多质量视角下探测其响应，用于引导特征自适应，使模型更接近真实视频的正常分布。其三是 Reversible Evidence Exchange（REE），采用可逆辛式更新机制融合空间残差证据与真实条件上下文证据，实现互补信息交换同时保持可逆性和数值稳定性。实验上，模型仅使用 4 个生成器产生的合成视频训练，并在涵盖 6 个基准、88 个生成器或任务子集上评估。

Result: SCRiD 在跨生成器和跨任务检测中取得平均 ACC 72.72%、AP 74.23%，相比最强基线分别提升约 3.24 和 0.81 个百分点。消融实验与鲁棒性实验进一步验证 MCR、RCSA 和 REE 等组件的有效性，并表明该框架在常见后处理操作下仍具有较好稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作说明，AI 生成视频检测不应仅依赖局部合成伪影，而应结合空间残差参照与真实视频上下文参照进行综合判断。SCRiD 通过 MCR 建模运动与坐标一致的时间残差，通过 RCSA 学习真实内容条件化去噪参照，再通过 REE 进行可逆证据融合，从而显著提升了有限源域训练下对未知生成器、任务和后处理的泛化检测能力。

Abstract: Generalizable AI-generated video detection remains challenging because natural motion and content variations can resemble synthesis artifacts, making isolated video discrepancies unreliable indicators of authenticity. To address this challenge, we propose Spatial and Contextual Reference-informed Detection (SCRiD), a framework that interprets video variations through complementary spatial and contextual references. Specifically, we introduce Matched-Coordinate Residual Response (MCR) to characterize temporal residual associations under motion and coordinate references with matched subpixel sampling conditions. We further develop Real-Conditioned Score Adaptation (RCSA), which learns a content-conditioned denoising reference from authentic videos and probes its responses across multiple noise scales and quality views to guide feature adaptation. To integrate these complementary sources of evidence, we design Reversible Evidence Exchange (REE) to enable information exchange through reversible symplectic updates. We train SCRiD using synthetic videos from only 4 generators and evaluate it across 88 generator or task subsets spanning 6 benchmarks. Our method achieves average ACC and AP of 72.72% and 74.23%, respectively, outperforming the strongest baseline by 3.24 and 0.81 percentage this http URL and robustness experiments further validate the effectiveness of the proposed components and the stability of SCRiD under common post-processing operations.

</details>


### [131] [SCR: Detecting Backdoor-Poisoned Samples through Clean-Reference Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24558)
*Yi Yang,Jinyang Huang,Feng-Qi Cui,Yu-Tong Guo,Sirui Zhao,Haiming Jin,Weihua Li,Quan Bai,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语义反事实残差化（SCR）方法，利用少量干净参考样本建模良性语义变化，通过检测样本特征相对于干净重建基线的一致偏离，识别深度神经网络中的后门投毒样本，无需预知攻击类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络容易受到后门攻击，攻击者通过向训练数据注入投毒样本来操控模型预测。然而，现有检测方法多依赖类级统计特征，难以区分投毒信号与正常样本本身的语义变化，导致误报良性偏离，同时漏检融入类分布的投毒样本。

Method: SCR 使用一小部分干净参考样本，通过样本特异性的特征重建对良性语义变化进行建模，并为每个待检样本构造干净基线。方法通过比较实际特征与该基线之间的一致性偏差来识别投毒样本，即语义反事实残差化。整个过程不需要知道攻击的具体类型、触发器模式或先验攻击信息。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100 和 Tiny ImageNet 上，针对 12 种 dirty-label 和 clean-label 后门攻击进行评估。使用小规模干净参考集时，SCR 在各数据集上的攻击平均真阳性率超过 99.5%，假阳性率低于 2%，并在宏平均真阳性率上优于 8 种代表性检测基线。

Conclusion: SCR 通过样本级干净基线重建有效缓解类级统计方法对良性语义变化的误判问题，能够高灵敏、低误报地检测多种后门投毒样本，说明面向样本特异性的语义残差分析是训练数据投毒检测的一种有效范式。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, in which adversaries manipulate model predictions by injecting poisoned samples into training data. Detecting these samples remains challenging because legitimate semantic variation can obscure poisoning-related signals in learned representations. Consequently, class-level statistics used by existing detectors may flag benign feature deviations while overlooking poisoned samples that blend into the class distribution. In this paper, we propose Semantic Counterfactual Residualization (SCR), which uses a small set of clean reference samples to account for benign variation through sample-specific feature reconstruction. SCR identifies poisoned samples by detecting coherent deviations from these clean baselines, without requiring prior knowledge of the attacks. We evaluate SCR through experiments covering twelve dirty-label and clean-label attacks across CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny ImageNet. With a small clean-reference set, SCR achieves attack-averaged true positive rates above 99.5\% and false positive rates below 2\% on each dataset, outperforming eight representative detection baselines in macro-TPR.

</details>


### [132] [Beyond Appearance: Evidence-Guided Programmatic 3D Scene Reconstruction with Agentic Lighting Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24565)
*Zeting Luo,Yifan Li,Shuai Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 LightHarness，一个基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的 agentic 图像生成代码式三维场景重建框架。它显式引入光照推理，并利用几何、内在属性和光照互补线索迭代修正场景结构与外观，同时通过 Lighting Reasoning Memory 记录光源假设、支持证据和修改历史，从而在没有额外训练或可微渲染的情况下提升复杂阴影和光照条件下的三维场景重建质量，并支持场景编辑、物体插入和重光照等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 image-to-code 三维场景重建方法主要依赖外观相似性作为优化目标，但这种信号难以区分几何、材质和光照方面的错误。光照通过阴影、反射、着色等现象与几何和材质相互作用，显著影响图像外观。因此，仅靠外观相似度会导致模型对错误的光照、遮挡或材质估计缺乏可靠判断。作者认为需要显式建模和推理光照信息，以提高三维场景重建的准确性和可解释性。

Method: 论文提出 LightHarness，其核心是将显式光照推理融入基于 VLM 的迭代式 scene-to-code 重建流程。首先，方法联合利用互补的几何线索、内在属性线索和光照线索，对三维场景结构和外观进行迭代优化。其次，提出 Lighting Reasoning Memory，用于维护光源假设、支持证据和修改历史，使智能体能够在多轮迭代中追踪和修正光照推理。最后，框架通过 evidence-guided verification 判断当前假设是否得到视觉证据支持，并据此引导后续对几何、材质或光照的修正。整个框架无需额外训练或可微渲染，输出显式、可编辑的三维场景代码。

Result: 大量实验表明，LightHarness 在复杂阴影和困难光照条件下的图像到代码三维场景重建中取得了更高的准确性。相比主要依赖外观相似性的现有方法，该框架在场景结构、材质表现和光照一致性方面更稳健，并进一步支持场景编辑、物体插入和多样化重光照等应用，说明其重建结果具有较好的显式表示能力和实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在 image-to-code 的三维场景重建中，显式光照推理和光源假设记忆能够显著缓解仅依赖外观相似性造成的几何、材质和光照错误难区分问题。LightHarness 通过 VLM agent、多线索联合优化和 Lighting Reasoning Memory，实现无需额外训练和可微渲染的可解释、可编辑三维场景重建，为复杂光照下的场景生成和下游编辑提供了更可靠的框架。

Abstract: Image-to-code methods for 3D scene reconstruction produce executable code that generates editable digital content and supports visual simulation. However, existing methods primarily rely on appearance similarity, which provides limited guidance for distinguishing errors in geometry, materials, and illumination. Light shapes scene appearance through its interactions with geometry and materials, influencing shading, cast shadows, and reflections. We therefore present LightHarness, a VLM-based agentic framework that incorporates explicit lighting reasoning into iterative scene reconstruction. To be specific, we leverage complementary geometric, intrinsic, and illumination cues to jointly refine scene structure and appearance, and propose Lighting Reasoning Memory to maintain light-source hypotheses, which supports evidence, and revision history throughout iterations. Together, they support reasoning about partially visible and unseen sources, with evidence-guided verification directing subsequent revisions. Our framework reconstructs explicit scenes through coherent reasoning about geometry, materials, and illumination without additional training or differentiable rendering. Extensive experiments demonstrate superior image-to-code reconstruction accuracy under challenging shadows and complex illumination, further supporting downstream scene editing, object insertion, and versatile relighting.

</details>


### [133] [BaseRSI: Recursive Self-Improvement of a Base Vision-Language Model via Training-Free Harness Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24578)
*Mingda Zhang,Yuanhao Dong,Qiang Huang,Rui Qian,Yanjin Li,Zhenyu Liu,Xiao Huang,Zihao Yang,Jian Wang,Pinjia He,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出BaseRSI，一种让基础模型递归地将外部harness能力内化到自身权重中的新方法；通过ReGap监督每个Transformer block的激活差异，并用ReKey将改进信号写入少量key-value neurons，最终在多个视觉推理基准上提升基础视觉语言模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有递归自我改进（RSI）仍主要依赖提示、工具等外部harness，收益停留在模型外部，且监督信号通常是黑盒式的输出反馈，修改位置和强度也往往预先固定，难以真正、精细地把能力提升写入基础模型本身。

Method: 提出“递归harness内化”范式：基础模型先改进其外部harness，再通过无需训练的参数编辑，将harness带来的增益写入自身权重，并在模型自身响应上反复迭代。实现上引入ReGap机制，用harness在每个Transformer block处造成的局部高维激活差异作为监督信号，并在低维子空间中更新对应block权重。进一步提出ReKey算法，通过回归将该信号写入每个block的少量key-value神经元，并在模型自身响应上确定每次编辑的位置与强度。整个过程仅依赖前向传播，且不同位置写入的编辑可在同一checkpoint中组合，无需重新拟合。

Result: 在十个视觉推理基准上的实验表明，BaseRSI能够一致地提升基础视觉语言模型的表现，既改善分布内性能，也提升保留基准上的泛化能力。

Conclusion: BaseRSI通过ReGap和ReKey实现了从外部harness增益到基础模型权重内部精细编辑的递归转化，为不依赖传统训练的模型自我改进提供了一种新路径，并显示出对视觉语言模型性能的有效提升。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) is moving from the harness of prompts and tools around a base model toward the model itself. However, existing RSI still faces key challenges, including harness-bound gains that stay outside the base model, black-box supervision that reads only the model's outputs, and imprecise edits whose site and strength are fixed in advance. To address these challenges, we propose BaseRSI, a new paradigm of Recursive Harness Internalization: the base model improves its harness, then writes what the harness adds into its own weights through training-free edits, round after round on its own responses. To support this paradigm, we introduce ReGap, a mechanism that supervises every transformer block with the high-dimensional activation difference that the harness makes at that block alone, and updates the block's weights along this signal in a low-dimensional subspace. Building on this mechanism, we further propose ReKey, an algorithm that writes this signal by regression into a few key-value neurons of every block, and measures the site and strength of each edit on the model's own responses. Moreover, the base model fills every role of this recursion with forward passes alone, and its edits, written at different sites, compose in one checkpoint without refitting. Experimental results on ten visual reasoning benchmarks show that BaseRSI consistently improves a base vision-language model both in distribution and on held-out benchmarks. Our code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [134] [EgoJev: Finding Evidence in Egocentric Video by Reading VLM Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24616)
*Chunggi Lee,Hanspeter Pfister*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 EgoJev：一种无需训练的长第一人称视频关键帧选择方法，利用同一个 VLM 从带标签的候选帧中选出证据帧，再围绕这些帧二次选择最终回答帧，以更好定位视频中短暂关键片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长第一人称视频问答常依赖极短的关键时刻，例如手放下物体的瞬间。均匀采样容易漏掉该帧；已有免训练方法要么依赖额外图文模型对帧排序，要么让 VLM 直接写出时间，效果不够稳定。作者希望只使用一个冻结的 VLM 就能更准确地定位证据时刻。

Method: 核心思路是让 VLM 在“从若干带编号/带标签的候选缩略图中选择最可能包含证据的帧”这一任务上表现更好，并从其输出分布中读出证据位置。EgoJev 先读取模型对带标签缩略图的选择结果，再在其指向的时间窗口内继续筛选；最终将少量回答帧放在所得证据时间分布的分位数上，从而覆盖关键证据。整个流程无需训练、无需第二个模型，由同一冻结 VLM 完成选择、回答和证据时间返回。设备端可先编码粗略缩略图以支持可穿戴助手。

Result: 在受控测试中，相比图文模型打分或让 VLM 写时间跨度，候选帧选择能更准确命中证据，且一次比较所有候选。以 Qwen3.5-4B 和 8 个回答帧为例，EgoJev 将 QAEgo4D 准确率从均匀采样的 51.2% 提升到 62.0%，并在 QAEgo4D 和 MyEgo 上优于近期免训练帧选择方法。单级版本可在 Mac 和 iPhone 上运行。

Conclusion: 该论文的关键发现是：对于长第一人称视频，让 VLM 直接“选择带标签帧”比“写时间”或依赖外部图文排序更有效；EgojEV 基于此构建了实用、训练无关、可部署于可穿戴设备的帧选择框架，显著改善长时间视频中的关键时刻定位和问答性能。

Abstract: Questions about long egocentric video often depend on one brief moment, such as the second a hand puts an object down. A vision-language model (VLM) that sees a few evenly spaced frames usually misses it. Training-free methods rank frames with a separate image-text model or ask the VLM to write a time. In a controlled test, the VLM itself locates the moment better when it chooses among labeled frames, and we read this choice from its output distribution. With the same candidates, this choice lands on the evidence more often than image-text scores or a written time span, and it compares all candidates in one decision. Based on this finding, we propose EgoJev, a training-free frame selector for wearable assistants. EgoJev reads where the evidence is from labeled thumbnails and looks again at the windows it points to. It places its few answer frames at the quantiles of the resulting distribution. One frozen VLM selects, answers and returns the evidence time, with no second model. A wearable device can encode its coarse thumbnails while it records. With Qwen3.5-4B and 8 answer frames, EgoJev raises QAEgo4D accuracy from 51.2% with uniform sampling to 62.0%, and it outperforms recent training-free frame selectors on QAEgo4D and MyEgo. A single-level version runs on a Mac and an iPhone.

</details>


### [135] [H3Remover: Reinforcing Video Object Removal without Paired Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24633)
*Kangjie Chen,Xiangyu Li,Jinhao Deng,Dongbin Zhang,Chaoda Zheng,Shijia Chen,Yuqi Zhang,Choo Sin Wai,Boyi Sun,Puyi Wang,Yu Zhang,Xianming Liu,Boyang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: H3Remover 通过奖励建模与 LoRA 适配，将 MiniMax-H3 Base Ref2VA 强化为视频目标移除模型，在去除目标的同时保持场景几何与背景外观一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频编辑器具有强大的修复先验，但在视频目标移除时可能仅做表面编辑，保留目标、改变无关内容，或破坏背景几何与外观一致性。因此需要一种无需配对无目标训练视频、同时约束目标移除、背景保护和视觉质量的方法。

Method: 该方法以 MiniMax-H3 Base Ref2VA 为骨干，在冻结骨干的前提下使用 DiffusionART 优化 LoRA 适配器。引入多种奖励：目标移除奖励鼓励与暴露更远内容一致的校准深度变化，抑制单纯重着色等表面编辑；背景几何奖励保持相对深度顺序，限制无关场景变化；外观奖励在源视频派生的保护区域内约束 RGB 变化，但豁免目标区域及阴影、反射等估计效应；同时使用冻结的 HPSv3 提供合成内容的感知质量反馈。所有候选的评估区域保持固定。

Result: 实验显示，相比原始 H3，H3Remover 在重建质量上取得显著提升；定性比较表明其能更有效地移除视频目标，并更好地保持周围场景内容和背景外观。

Conclusion: H3Remover 证明了多目标奖励约束结合 LoRA 参数高效微调，可在没有配对无目标视频监督的情况下提升预训练视频编辑模型的目标移除能力，同时缓解表面编辑、无关场景改变和背景外观失真问题。

Abstract: Pretrained video editors possess rich removal and completion priors, yet may retain targets or alter unrelated content. We present H3Remover, which reinforces MiniMax-H3 Base Ref2VA for video object removal without paired object-free training targets. To discourage superficial edits such as recoloring, a target-removal reward favors calibrated depth changes consistent with revealing farther content. To limit unintended scene changes, a background-geometry reward encourages preservation of relative depth ordering. To preserve background appearance without retaining unwanted shadows or reflections, an appearance reward constrains RGB changes in source-derived protected regions while exempting targets and estimated effects. Frozen HPSv3 complements these constraints with perceptual-quality feedback for synthesized content. Evaluation regions remain fixed across candidates, and DiffusionART optimizes LoRA adapters with the backbone frozen. Experiments show substantial reconstruction gains over original H3, with qualitative comparisons demonstrating more effective object removal and better preservation of surrounding scene content.

</details>


### [136] [AggCLIP: Aggregating CLIP for Zero-Shot Multimodal Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24643)
*Zhi Li,Hanyu Wang,Fei Shen,Zhengtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AggCLIP针对零样本多模态异常检测中3D几何信息丢失、2D特征噪声大和跨模态语义错位问题，提出几何感知与上下文感知空间聚合模块，并引入双流提示对比损失，在MVTec 3D-AD与Eyecandies上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本多模态异常检测需要在没有目标类别训练数据的情况下识别新类别的外观缺陷和几何缺陷，适用于数据稀缺、隐私限制或标注成本高的真实场景。现有基于CLIP等视觉-语言模型的方法虽具备开放词表泛化能力，但仍存在三类关键问题：第一，3D点云渲染为2D图像时会造成严重的几何信息损失；第二，原始CLIP patch embedding生成的2D特征图具有空间噪声，且多模态融合往往停留在较浅的后期阶段；第三，点云与RGB分支被解耦处理，容易导致跨模态语义错位，难以形成一致的正常/异常语义表示。

Method: 提出AggCLIP框架，通过显式引入局部几何结构和视觉空间上下文来适配冻结CLIP表示，用于零样本多模态异常检测。具体包括：Geometry-Aware Spatial Aggregation模块利用密度自适应邻域加权，对反投影后的3D点特征进行细化，以恢复和保留精细几何结构；Context-Aware Spatial Aggregation模块通过轻量可训练适配器平滑多尺度2D patch embeddings，从而抑制原始CLIP特征图的空间噪声并增强上下文一致性；Dual-Stream Prompt Contrastive损失在点云分支和RGB分支之间对齐抽象的“正常”和“异常”概念，缓解双流独立处理造成的语义漂移。该框架在保持CLIP预训练表示能力的同时，加强了3D几何信息、2D空间上下文和跨模态语义一致性建模。

Result: 在MVTec 3D-AD和Eyecandies基准上，AggCLIP在one-vs-rest和跨数据集零样本设置下均达到SOTA性能。与最强竞争方法相比，其在像素级AUPRO指标上最高提升约2.9%，表明所提出的几何感知空间聚合、上下文感知空间聚合和双流提示对比损失能够有效改善细粒度缺陷定位和零样本多模态异常检测性能。

Conclusion: AggCLIP表明，在零样本多模态异常检测中，仅依赖渲染后的2D CLIP特征并不充分；显式建模局部3D几何结构、优化多尺度2D空间上下文，并在点云与RGB双流之间进行抽象语义对齐，是提升异常检测精度的关键。该方法为数据受限场景下的开放词表、跨类别、跨数据集异常检测提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Zero-Shot Multimodal Anomaly Detection (ZSMAD) aims to identify both appearance and geometric defects in novel categories without relying on target-specific training data, making it especially valuable in real-world scenarios constrained by data scarcity, privacy concerns, or high annotation costs. While recent approaches successfully adapt vision-language models like CLIP to achieve this open-vocabulary generalization, they still face significant challenges: (i) severe geometric information loss during 3D-to-2D point cloud rendering; (ii) spatially noisy 2D feature maps produced by raw CLIP patch embeddings, compounded by shallow late-stage modality fusion; and (iii) severe cross-modal semantic misalignment due to decoupled processing streams. To address these limitations, we propose AggCLIP, a novel framework that adapts frozen CLIP representations for ZSMAD by explicitly incorporating local geometric structure and visual spatial context. Specifically, to accurately localize fine-grained defects, we design a Geometry-Aware Spatial Aggregation (GSA) module that refines back-projected 3D point features via density-adaptive neighborhood weighting, alongside a Context-Aware Spatial Aggregation (CSA) module that smooths multi-scale 2D patch embeddings using a lightweight trainable adapter. Furthermore, to prevent semantic drift between modalities, we introduce a Dual-Stream Prompt Contrastive (DSPC) loss, which aligns the abstract concepts of normality and abnormality across the point cloud and RGB streams. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed AggCLIP framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on the MVTec 3D-AD and Eyecandies benchmarks under both one-vs-rest and cross-dataset zero-shot settings, notably surpassing the strongest competitive methods by up to 2.9% in pixel-level AUPRO.

</details>


### [137] [Predicting Species Splits: A Challenging Fine-Grained Benchmark for Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24672)
*Christian Lange,Joakim Bruslund Haurum,Bingchen Zhao,Oisin Mac Aodha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PS-SPLIT：一个基于鸟类分类学“拆分”过程的大规模细粒度类别发现基准，并定义“按类别拆分进行发现”的新任务，用于评估和推动真实世界中的细粒度类别发现方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类别发现基准趋于饱和，难以反映真实世界中类别不断被重新划分的发现场景。作者受到分类学中物种“拆分”现象的启发，即原本被视为同一物种的类群后来被发现包含多个不同物种，因此希望构建一个更贴近现实、更具长期评估价值且视觉差异更细微的基准。

Method: 作者从257个经专家验证的真实分类学拆分中构建PS-SPLIT基准，包含79K张全球分布、高质量、社区采集的鸟类图像，覆盖771个物种，并提供地理元数据与完整分类层级。基于此，提出“discovery by category splitting”任务：判断哪些已知类别应被进一步细分为更细粒度的子类。随后将四类广义类别发现方法适配到该任务并进行评测。

Result: 实验表明，PS-SPLIT中的类别差异来自真实分类学拆分，通常非常细微，对现有广义类别发现方法构成显著挑战；已有方法在该任务上仍有很大提升空间。

Conclusion: PS-SPLIT通过真实世界中的类别重新划分机制构建了更具挑战性的细粒度类别发现评测框架，有助于推动类别发现研究向更精细、更现实的场景发展。

Abstract: Widely used category discovery benchmarks have become increasingly saturated and often fail to reflect real-world discovery settings, making long-term evaluation of new methods difficult. We introduce PS-SPLIT, a large-scale fine-grained benchmark for category discovery, motivated by taxonomic splitting: the real-world process by which what was once considered a single species is found to comprise multiple distinct ones. PS-SPLIT contains 79K globally distributed, high-quality, community-collected bird images spanning 771 species, derived from 257 recent real expert-verified taxonomic splits, with associated geographic metadata and full taxonomic hierarchy. The visual differences between categories arising from real taxonomic splits can be very subtle, often requiring significant human expertise to tell apart, making PS-SPLIT a challenging benchmark which we hope will assist in advancing fine-grained category discovery research. Based on taxonomic splitting, we introduce the task of discovery by category splitting, where the goal is to determine which known categories should be subdivided into new finer-grained subcategories. We adapt four generalized category discovery methods to this setting, evaluate existing methods, and show that there is substantial room for future progress.

</details>


### [138] [DrawScore: Evaluating Generated CAD Models against Engineering Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24681)
*Haorui Chen,Shiteng Lu,Zhanyu Guo,Zaixi Li,Yunhe Ni,Yaosong Du,Yichen Xiang,Xiande Huang,Rui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作指出常见CAD生成评价指标（IoU、Chamfer距离、表面F-score）对局部微小几何错误不敏感，并基于统一积分形式分析了错误权重的尺度衰减规律，提出面向三视图的DrawScore指标，使局部错误的惩罚随尺度仅线性衰减，从而更有效地发现可制造性关键缺陷、改进生成模型并作为无需三维真值的测试时验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动CAD生成常用于工业设计、制造和工程图纸理解，但现有基于体积、点云或表面积的度量往往被整体几何形状主导，难以惩罚小尺寸但关键的局部错误，例如孔位置偏移、槽开错面等。这些错误在制造中可能导致零件失效，因此需要更敏感、更符合工程检验逻辑的评价与奖励机制。

Method: 作者首先将IoU、Chamfer距离和表面F-score统一写成积分形式的错误度量，证明局部错误权重随其尺寸以 (k+q) 次方衰减，其中 k 是积分区域维度，q 由惩罚函数决定，说明大尺度指标对细小错误不敏感。随后提出DrawScore：将生成模型与参考模型分别投影为三个正交工程视图，并利用可见线与隐藏线，在投影曲线上施加饱和惩罚，使局部错误权重随尺度仅线性衰减，从而增强对关键小错误的敏感性。

Result: 受控实验验证了理论预测的错误尺度衰减速率。在现有CAD生成代理的输出上，DrawScore能够暴露被IoU认为接近完美的局部错误。此外，将DrawScore作为强化学习奖励可提升生成质量；在由工程图纸生成CAD的任务中，它还可作为测试时验证器，无需三维真值或额外奖励模型。

Conclusion: DrawScore从工程图纸的二维投影视角重新定义CAD生成评价，克服了传统三维/表面指标对局部微小错误不敏感的问题。它不仅是一个更可靠的评估指标，也可作为生成训练中的奖励信号和无三维真值场景下的推理时验证器，对提高CAD生成的可制造性和实用性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Automatic CAD generation is commonly evaluated with volumetric IoU, Chamfer Distance, and surface F-score, which barely penalize local errors that occupy only a small fraction of a part's volume or surface area, such as a misplaced hole or a slot on the wrong face, even though such errors are critical to manufacturing. We formalize these metrics in a unified integral form and show that the weight of a local error decays as the $(k+q)$-th power of its size, where $k$ is the dimension of the integration domain and $q$ is determined by the penalty function. Guided by this analysis, we propose DrawScore, which projects both models into three orthographic views with visible and hidden lines, as in engineering drawings, and applies a saturating penalty along the projected curves, so that the weight of a local error decays only linearly. Controlled experiments confirm the predicted decay orders, and on outputs of existing CAD generation agents, DrawScore exposes errors in outputs that IoU rates as nearly perfect. DrawScore further improves generation as a reinforcement learning reward and, for CAD generation from engineering drawings, serves as a test-time verifier without 3D ground truth or an additional reward model. Our code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [139] [Improving Zero-shot 3D Semantic Mapping in Offroad with Geometric Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24696)
*Eerik Alamikkotervo,Risto Ojala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向自然越野场景的语义3D建图方法：利用高置信度视觉基础模型预测拟合每个语义类别的“球形度-高程”几何先验，再将其与视觉预测融合，以提升3D语义地图在结构化场景之外的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义3D建图有助于移动机器人在复杂非结构化环境中进行平台无关的导航。现有视觉基础模型在室内或结构化场景中可实现准确的零样本语义预测，但在自然环境中的表现会下降；同时，3D点云本身包含有助于区分语义的几何信息。因此需要一种方式将视觉语义能力与点云几何先验结合，提升野外场景下的语义建图鲁棒性。

Method: 作者为每个语义类别拟合一个“球形度-高程”（sphericity-elevation）分布，作为几何锚点/先验。为了避免低质量点云拟合，该方法只使用视觉基础模型中高置信度的预测结果来构建几何先验分布。最终，将估计得到的几何先验与视觉基础模型的语义预测进行融合，生成更具鲁棒性的语义3D地图。

Result: 在 RELLIS-3D 数据集上，所提方法相比 SAM3 baseline 提升 mIoU 6.3 个百分点、准确率 8.9 个百分点；在 WildScenes 数据集上提升 mIoU 1.7 个百分点、准确率 2.1 个百分点，说明几何先验融合能够显著改善自然环境中语义3D建图性能。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将高置信度视觉语义与3D点云中的类别特定几何统计信息相结合，是一种有效的野外语义建图增强策略。所提方法尤其适合改善视觉基础模型在非结构化自然环境中的不足，并有望提升移动机器人语义感知能力。代码将在论文录用后公开。

Abstract: Semantic 3D mapping is a powerful representation that enables platform agnostic mobile robot navigation in challenging offroad conditions. Current vision foundation models can accurately predict semantics zero-shot on structured scenes but performance is compromised in natural environments. On the other hand, 3D point clouds encode valuable information for discriminating semantics in scenarios where vision foundation models fail. In this work, we propose fitting a sphericity-elevation distribution for each semantic class to act as geometric anchor. Only the points with the most confident vision foundation model predictions are considered when fitting to produce high quality geometric prior distributions. The geometric prior is fused with the vision foundation model prediction to produce the final semantic mapping. The proposed prior improves SAM3 baseline by +6.3 in mIoU and +8.9 in accuracy on the RELLIS-3D dataset, and by +1.7 in mIoU and +2.1 in accuracy on the WildScenes dataset. The source code will be made public upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [140] [Can Omni-modal LLMs Understand Spoken Intentions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24706)
*Zijie Xin,Tianyi Wang,Daoze Zhang,Jie Yang,Changli Tang,Qijie Wei,Fengyun Rao,Xirong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 O-IN-Bench，用于评估全模态大语言模型在视频通话式交互中识别用户请求并给出恰当回应的能力；实验显示，模型“识别何时应回应”相对较好，但“回应是否真正满足用户需求”仍明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模态大语言模型可通过语音和视频直接回应用户，但其理解 spoken intentions 的难点在于：同一句话在不同场景下可能表示求助、闲聊，或根本不需要助手回应。现有音视频基准常把问题作为独立文本输入，或将模型回答整体打分，因而难以分别衡量模型是否知道何时应回应、以及回应内容是否满足需求。

Method: 作者提出 O-IN-Bench：模型观看包含原生语音的完整视频，仅输出一段文本回应；若无需回应，则输出 [SILENCE]。基准包含 1000 条中英文样本，覆盖 5 大类别、16 个子类别，并设计对照样本对，使相同请求处于不同场景条件，以检验回应是否随上下文变化。评测采用样本专属 rubric，分别判断语言、意图和内容；同时使用 O-IN-Engine 对每条评分标准与视频进行校验。

Result: 在 30 种全模态模型配置中，最佳整体得分为 59.6，由 Gemini3.7-Flash 取得。该模型在意图判断上通过率为 74.4%，但内容判断通过率仅 42.6%。当语音是用户之间的同伴对话而非对助手的请求时，即使是较强模型也常继续回应；当回应需要同时满足多个对象时，最佳得分仅 25.9。

Conclusion: 结果表明，识别用户请求与满足请求内容是两种相对独立的能力：现有全模态大语言模型在“何时回应”上具有一定进展，但在“回应是否真正符合场景和用户需求”上仍明显薄弱。O-IN-Bench 提供了可分别评测这两方面能力的基准。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (omni-modal LLMs) can respond to spoken requests directly through voice and video calls. Such requests rely on the scene shared by the user and the assistant, so the same utterance may call for different help or for no response. Most audio-visual benchmarks provide questions as separate text input, and interactive benchmarks mostly score a response as a whole. It thus remains unclear whether a model recognizes when the user is asking for help, and whether its reply meets the user's need once the request is recognized. We propose O-IN-Bench to evaluate how well omni-modal LLMs understand spoken intentions. A model watches a complete video with native speech and outputs one text response, or [SILENCE] when no response is needed. O-IN-Bench contains 1,000 Chinese and English samples in five categories and 16 subcategories. Contrastive pairs combine the same request with different scene conditions to test whether the response changes with the context. A sample-specific rubric judges language, intent, and content separately, and O-IN-Engine verifies each rubric against the video. Across 30 omni-modal model configurations, the best overall score is 59.6, achieved by Gemini3.7-Flash. Gemini3.7-Flash passes 74.4% of the intent judgments but only 42.6% of the content judgments. When the speech is a conversation between peers rather than a request to the assistant, even strong models often keep responding. When a reply must satisfy several people, the best score is only 25.9. These results indicate that recognizing a request and meeting its requirements are separate abilities, and O-IN-Bench measures each of them.

</details>


### [141] [DRIFT: Detachable Reasoning for Runtime-Flexible and Efficient End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24719)
*Emad Aghajanzadeh,Bethany Anne Wong,Leonhard Kunze,Moritz Thoma,Maximilian Forstenhäusler,Nael Fasfous,Manoj Rohit Vemparala,Pierpaolo Mori,Christos Anagnostopoulos,Daniel Mueller-Gritschneder,Ulf Schlichtmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DRIFT，一个面向端到端自动驾驶的轻量视觉-语言融合框架，通过小语言模型生成场景描述与动作注释，并引入可替换的代理推理特征，使部署时可动态关闭语言模型而不显著损害驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型能够增强自动驾驶系统的场景理解和推理能力，但通常计算开销大、延迟高，难以在实际车载部署中按需开启或关闭语言推理。论文旨在解决 VLM 在端到端自动驾驶中“强理解、难部署”的问题，探索如何在保持驾驶性能的同时降低计算成本。

Method: DRIFT 的核心设计包括：1）使用参数量小于 150M 的轻量语言模型，将中间驾驶表征投影到语言嵌入空间，实现视觉问答与动作注释；2）提出运行时灵活的规划模块，利用从语言模型蒸馏得到的代理推理特征进行训练；3）推理时可由真实语言模型或代理推理特征提供条件，从而在部署资源紧张时绕过语言模型。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 上，DRIFT 仅使用 255M 参数，通过控制语言模型与代理推理特征的交错使用比例，达到最高 Driving Score 97.03 和平均成功率 89.39%，性能与最强非语言模型相当，同时显著小于现有 VLM 方案。论文还在真实数据集 NAVSIM 和 AlpaSim 上展示了具有竞争力且计算成本更低的结果。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，端到端自动驾驶中的语言模型能力可以被高效蒸馏为代理推理特征，从而在场景描述、可解释性和部署灵活性之间取得平衡。DRIFT 使 VLM 式推理能够在实际自动驾驶系统中按需启用或关闭，为轻量化视觉-语言驾驶系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The integration of vision-language models (VLMs) into end-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving has demonstrated strong potential by providing scene description and reasoning capabilities. However, VLMs are computationally expensive, and switching language reasoning on and off at runtime without hurting closed-loop performance has not been demonstrated, which hinders practical adoption. To address these issues, we propose DRIFT, an E2E autonomous driving framework that integrates a lightweight language model (LM) for visual question answering and action commentary. By efficiently projecting intermediate driving representations into the language embedding space, we demonstrate that a compact LM (<150M parameters) can achieve strong scene description and action commentary. Furthermore, we introduce a runtime-flexible planning module that is trained on surrogate reasoning features distilled from the LM and can be conditioned on either the LM or the surrogate reasoning features at inference. This design enables bypassing the LM entirely when required by deployment constraints, without sacrificing driving performance. With only 255M parameters, DRIFT reaches up to a Driving Score (DS) of 97.03 and a Success Rate of 89.39% (mean over three runs) on Bench2Drive by interleaving its LM and surrogate reasoning at a chosen reasoning rate. This is on par with the strongest non-language models while being far more compact than existing VLM-based models. DRIFT further achieves competitive results on the real-world NAVSIM and AlpaSim benchmarks at significantly lower computational cost than existing VLM-based approaches. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [142] [Generalization of Text Line Segmentation for HTR in Historical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24726)
*Gayan H. Pathirage,Stephan M. Unter,Simon Corbillé,Elisa H. Barney Smith*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究 Hi-SAM、YOLO 和 RT-DETR 等先进目标检测模型在历史文档文本行自动分割中的泛化能力，并进一步评估少样本微调效果；结果显示分割后的文本经 CRNN 识别可达到 2.48%–7.07% 的 CER，同时给出了训练与推理的资源和时间开销分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 博物馆和档案馆通常无法为每一批新收藏的大量文档人工标注充足样本，因此文本行分割模型需要在新数据集、不同版式和语言条件下具备良好泛化能力；同时需要解决传统目标检测指标 mAP 不足以充分评估文本行分割质量的问题。

Method: 选取 Hi-SAM、YOLO、RT-DETR 等状态先进目标检测模型，用于历史文档中文本行的自动分割；在九个视觉版式和语言差异显著的数据集上进行评估；设置零样本泛化与少样本微调两种场景，仅用目标数据集少量样本微调网络；对分割出的文本行使用 CRNN 进行识别，以字符错误率 CER 评价实际可读性；最后量化不同使用场景下训练和推理的计算资源与时间需求。

Result: 所研究的文本行分割模型具备面向新历史文档数据集的泛化潜力，少样本微调可进一步提升适配效果；根据不同数据集，CRNN 对分割文本行的识别 CER 为 2.48% 至 7.07% 每页，足以支持阅读理解和简单文本检索；论文还明确了模型训练与推理在不同资源约束下的成本，为模型与硬件选择提供依据。

Conclusion: 将先进目标检测模型应用于历史文档文本行分割具有实用价值，尤其在无法大规模标注的档案场景中，泛化和少样本微调策略可显著降低部署成本；但仅用 mAP 评估文本分割质量并不充分，应结合下游识别错误或实际任务效果进行评价。

Abstract: In this paper we investigate the generalization capability of state-of-the-art object detection models (Hi-SAM, YOLO, and RT-DETR) for the automatic segmentation of text lines in historical documents. Generalization is needed because it is not practical for museums and archives to produce large amounts of labeled samples for each new collection. We also evaluate the few-shot setting by fine-tuning the networks with only a small number of training samples per target dataset. To this end, we use nine different datasets that differ significantly in visual layout and language. Additionally, we discuss why mean average precision (mAP), a classic evaluation metric for object detection, is insufficient for assessing the quality of a text line segmentation model. Depending on the dataset, our CRNN models achieve character error rates (CER) ranging from 2.48% to 7.07% per page for the segmented text lines. This is sufficient for reading comprehension and simple text searches. Finally, we quantify the resource and time requirements of training and inference in different use case settings. This forms the basis for more informed decisions about model and hardware choices based on available resources.

</details>


### [143] [HandWorldBench: A Unified Benchmark for Hand-Centric World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24731)
*Zhaolu Wang,Jiacheng Xu,Lihong Wang,Shuqing Wang,Jianjie Fang,Ziyou Wang,Xvyuan Liu,Rongze Tang,Yiding Ma,Xin Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Wei Wu,Chen Gao,Yong Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 HandWorldBench，一个面向手部中心世界模型的系统性评测基准，用于评估人类手、仿真灵巧手和真实灵巧手在手物交互场景中的建模能力，涵盖视觉质量、手部保真度、外观一致性、指令跟随、物理一致性和时间连贯性六个维度。通过数据整理管线从超过 600 小时素材中提取 20,500 个高质量第一视角手物交互片段，并人工筛选 500 个覆盖七类任务的片段进行评测。对 11 个模型的统一评测显示，更强的手部姿态控制并不必然带来一致的对象状态演化和持久性，部分专用手控模型在灵巧手任务上甚至弱于通用文/图生视频模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在细粒度具身任务中的进展为手物交互建模提供了新可能，而手物交互要求模型同时理解手部动作如何改变对象状态，以及这些状态如何随时间保持和演化。现有评测体系尚未系统覆盖这些以手部为中心、跨本体和跨仿真/真实场景的关键能力，导致难以准确判断不同视频生成或世界模型在灵巧交互任务上的真实水平。

Method: 作者构建统一基准 HandWorldBench，从三类本体出发进行评估：人类手、仿真灵巧手和真实灵巧手。评测围绕六个维度展开：视觉质量、手部保真度、外观一致性、指令跟随、物理一致性和时间连贯性。数据方面，建立面向手物交互片段的数据整理管线，从超过 600 小时的第一视角视频中提取并筛选高质量片段，得到 20,500 个片段；随后人工选择 500 个覆盖七类任务家族的片段用于最终评测。最后，在统一协议下对 11 个模型进行基准测试。

Result: 实验揭示了若干重要现象：首先，更强的手部姿态控制能力并不稳定转化为更合理的对象状态演化、对象状态持久性或跨本体鲁棒性；其次，一些专门针对手部控制优化的模型在灵巧手任务上的表现可能不如通用的文本/图像生成视频模型。这表明当前模型在手物交互世界建模方面仍存在明显缺口，尤其是在物理一致性、时间连贯性和跨本体泛化方面。

Conclusion: HandWorldBench 为手部中心世界模型提供了统一、细粒度且面向具身智能的评测框架，能够系统揭示模型在视觉、手部形态、指令跟随、物理规律和时间连贯性等方面的不足。该基准表明，仅优化手控或视觉生成质量不足以实现可靠的手物交互世界建模，未来需要更加注重对象状态演化、物理一致性和跨灵巧手本体的泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in world models are opening new possibilities for fine-grained embodied tasks, with hand-object interaction offering a particularly demanding setting. Modeling these interactions involves capturing how hand actions change objects and how the resulting states persist over time. Yet systematic evaluation tailored to these hand-centric capabilities remains underdeveloped. To address this gap, we introduce HandWorldBench, a unified benchmark that systematically evaluates hand-centric world models across human hands, simulated dexterous hands, and real dexterous hands along six dimensions: visual quality, hand fidelity, appearance consistency, instruction following, physical consistency, and temporal coherence. To construct the benchmark, we develop a data curation pipeline that extracts hand-object interaction clips and screens them for quality, yielding 20,500 high-quality egocentric clips from over 600 hours of footage. We then manually select 500 clips covering seven task families for evaluation. Benchmarking eleven models under a unified protocol reveals that stronger hand-pose control does not consistently translate into coherent object-state evolution and persistence or robust cross-embodiment performance, with several specialized hand-control models underperforming general-purpose text/image-to-video generators on dexterous-hand tasks.

</details>


### [144] [Jeveration: System One Models Now Generate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24738)
*Yuhe Liu,Qi Li,Shihua Zhang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Jeveration，一种基于冻结视觉语言模型的“系统一”式单次前向生成框架，可在约78毫秒内生成512×512图像，并统一支持文生图、图生图和密集预测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型通常依赖多步迭代解码，速度慢且难以实现单次并行生成；而一个文本提示可对应多种合理图像，如何让并行预测在全局上一致、又保持生成多样性，是单次前向生成的关键挑战。

Method: 作者复用冻结视觉语言模型的视觉语义知识进行整体图像生成。为协调所有位置的并行预测，引入共享随机状态作为条件，使同一次采样产生连贯但不同的图像；同时，由于直接图像监督下模型容易忽略该状态，先学习离散图像码分布以引导单次生成，并将文生图、图生图和密集预测统一到同一架构和同一检查点中。

Result: 实验显示，Jeveration生成512×512图像仅需78毫秒，相比现有统一模型最高快122倍，同时保持具有竞争力的图像质量和密集预测性能。

Conclusion: 该工作证明“系统一”式快速并行决策范式可迁移到高质量图像生成，通过共享随机状态和离散图像码先验有效解决单次前向生成的全局一致性难题，为高效统一视觉生成与理解提供了新方向。

Abstract: System One models such as Jev return decisions through fast, parallel prediction. In this paper, we introduce Jeveration, showing that the same decision paradigm can also generate high-quality images in a single forward pass. Jeveration builds on a frozen vision-language model, repurposing its visual-semantic knowledge for holistic image generation. The key challenge is to maintain global consistency. A single prompt admits many valid images, yet parallel predictions must converge on a coherent whole. We therefore condition all positions on a shared random state to coordinate parallel predictions, so that each draw yields a different but coherent image. Since the model tends to ignore this state under direct image supervision, we first learn a distribution over discrete image codes to guide single-pass generation. Beyond text-to-image generation, we further unify image-to-image generation and dense prediction within the same architecture, so that one checkpoint supports all three tasks, each in one forward pass. Experiments show that Jeveration generates a 512x512 image in 78 ms, up to 122x faster than existing unified models, while maintaining competitive image quality and dense prediction performance.

</details>


### [145] [A$^3$Bench: Compositional Reasoning over Aggressive Interactions in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24762)
*Austin C Baker,Raiyaan Abdullah,Daniel Zeev Yoffe,Shruti Vyas,Yogesh S Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 A^3Bench，用于评估多模态大语言模型在视频攻击性场景中对“角色—动作—关系”进行细粒度绑定推理的能力，发现现有模型显著依赖粗略暴力识别而难以稳定区分施暴者、受害者与旁观者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频多模态基准和暴力/犯罪数据集主要关注是否发生攻击行为，缺少对动作主体、受事者、旁观者以及参与者关系绑定的系统评测。作者希望揭示多模态大语言模型在真实攻击性互动中角色推理的不足。

Method: 构建包含攻击性动作、参与者角色和复杂干扰场景的视频基准 A^3Bench；评测 17 个多模态模型，考察模型能否联合识别攻击动作、区分施暴者/受害者/旁观者并正确绑定动作与角色；进一步比较模型规模和通用推理模型的表现，并探索简单的 role-graph prompting 策略以促进结构化角色关系推理。

Result: 模型在细粒度攻击性场景理解上表现不佳；随着推理复杂度增加，性能持续下降，常见错误包括混淆施暴者、受害者和旁观者。单纯扩大模型规模或引入通用推理能力提升有限。角色图提示策略能够改善组合式推理表现。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大语言模型尚不能可靠完成攻击性视频中的角色感知推理，研究重点应从粗粒度暴力检测推进到更细粒度的视频角色—动作—关系理解，role-aware reasoning 是未来重要方向。

Abstract: Recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance on general video understanding benchmarks, yet their ability to reason about participant roles in aggressive interactions remains largely unexplored. Existing violence and crime datasets primarily evaluate whether aggression occurs, without testing whether models can identify who performed the action, who received it, and how participants are related within the scene. To address this gap, we introduce A$^3$Bench, a benchmark to study aggressive action and association in videos. The benchmark evaluates whether models can jointly recognize aggressive actions, distinguish aggressors from victims and bystanders, and correctly bind actions to participants under challenging distractor settings. We benchmark 17 multimodal models and find that they struggle substantially on fine-grained aggressive-scene understanding. Performance degrades consistently as reasoning complexity increases, with models frequently confusing aggressors with victims or bystanders, revealing a systematic failure in role-action association rather than coarse aggression recognition alone. We further observe that increasing model scale or generic reasoning models experience limited improvement. Finally, we explore a simple role-graph prompting strategy that encourages structured reasoning over participant interactions, leading to improvements in compositional reasoning. Our findings highlight that reliable aggressive-scene understanding remains a major challenge for current MLLMs and motivate future research on role-aware video reasoning beyond coarse violence detection. Code and dataset: this https URL.

</details>


### [146] [Read Together, Answer Apart: How Earlier Questions Help Vision-Language Models Look](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24785)
*Xinyi Guo,Kaile Huang,Xitong Ling,Mingyi He,Chende Zheng,Jianyu Xu,Chao He,Dongqing Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉语言模型在同一提示中共同提问多道题的“co-asking”现象，发现其净准确率变化来自两种相反效应：早期问题在共享prefill中帮助后续问题定位图像信息，而答案解码阶段对其他题干token的注意会引入干扰；据此提出训练自由的“Read Together, Answer Apart”解码掩码，保留共享视觉阅读并隔离各答案解码，从而显著提升co-asking准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评测常在同一提示中询问同一图像多个问题，但不同研究对co-asking带来的是准确率提升还是幻觉增加存在矛盾，且多数只在提示级报告总体结果，没有跟踪单个问题答案如何由正确变错误或错误变正确。作者希望解释co-asking中“修复错误”与“破坏正确答案”并存的原因，并找到可操作的机制证据与改进方法。

Method: 作者将每道题的单问题答案与co-asked答案配对，在Densely Captioned Images数据集上评测22个开源VLM模型（1B至35B）。对于支持自定义注意力掩码的14个模型，进一步通过注意力读出、层窗口干预和消融实验，分析同一图像中早期问题在prefill阶段是否影响后续问题对图像目标区域的注意。最后提出“Read Together, Answer Apart”训练自由解码掩码，使模型共享同一图像的prefill阅读，但在生成每个答案时屏蔽对其他问题题干token的注意力。

Result: 在DCI上，所有模型在co-asking时修复了15.4%至29.8%的单问题错误，但正确变错误的比例从弱模型的30.8%下降到强模型的4.7%。注意力分析表明，早期同图问题在早中层prefill中将视觉信息读入自身question tokens，帮助后续问题将图像注意集中到所问目标；该视觉帮助会随问题累积，即使目标区域不重叠也出现。阻止早期问题关注图像会降低所有14个模型的目标定位和准确率。提出的解码掩码在DCI上将14个模型中12个的co-asking准确率提高，最高+4.71个百分点，缩小70%与单独提问相比的池化差距；在WebUI、FinTabNet、GQA上也分别在14个模型中的10、8、5个模型取得提升，最高+11.4、+5.3、+1.1个百分点。

Conclusion: Co-asking并非单一有利或有害机制，而是共享prefill带来的视觉信息增益与共享解码中的题干干扰损失并存。通过保留跨问题的视觉信息传递、同时切断答案对其他题干token的依赖，训练自由的注意力掩码可以显著降低co-asking损害并提升多问题VLM问答准确率。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are often asked several questions about the same image in a single prompt (co-asking), yet existing evaluations report conflicting accuracy gains and hallucinations at the prompt level without tracking how individual answers change. By pairing each question's single-question and co-asked answers across 22 open-weight VLMs (1B-35B), we find that erratic net shifts reflect two opposing effects: on Densely Captioned Images (DCI), every model repairs 15.4%-29.8% of its single-question errors under co-asking, while the share of correct answers turned wrong falls from 30.8% on weaker models to 4.7% on stronger ones. Across all 14 models supporting custom attention masks, we show through attention readouts and layer-window interventions that earlier same-image questions read visual information into their question tokens in the early-to-middle layers during prefill, helping later questions concentrate image attention onto their queried targets. This visual help accumulates across the scene even across non-overlapping regions, whereas blocking earlier questions from attending to the image lowers both target localization and accuracy in all 14 models. While cross-question help is established during shared question reading, part of the co-asking damage enters later during answer decoding when the decoder attends to other question stems. We therefore introduce Read Together, Answer Apart, a training-free decoding mask that keeps shared prefill intact and isolates each answer from other stems, improving co-asking accuracy on 12 of 14 models on DCI (up to +4.71 percentage points, closing 70% of the pooled gap to asking alone) and on 10, 8, and 5 of the 14 models on WebUI, FinTabNet, and GQA (up to +11.4, +5.3, and +1.1 points).

</details>


### [147] [Training-free motion statistics match self-supervised behavior encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24792)
*Ziwen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需训练的动物行为表征基线：按帧测量通用运动特征、在固定时间窗内汇总并用 PCA 降维。在多个基准上与自监督行为编码器公平比较后，该基线在多数任务上优于 BAMS、hBehaveMAE 等编码器，表明当前自监督行为编码器尚未稳定超过其训练所用的手工运动特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物行为分析中，自监督编码器通常以手工构建的运动特征为输入或训练目标，但其是否真正优于这些特征本身仍缺乏公平验证。作者希望检验：在统一协议下，自监督表征是否比简单、免训练的手工特征基线更有优势，从而判断这类复杂模型的真实增益。

Method: 采用预注册比较协议：所有模型使用冻结表征、相同维度表征和共享分类器。基线方法不做端到端训练，而是计算每帧通用运动测量值，在固定时间窗内聚合，并用 PCA 降维。将该基线与重新训练和公开的行为编码器 BAMS、hBehaveMAE 比较，并评估标注者变化、少样本新行为学习、多种子重训等鲁棒性条件。数据包括 CalMS21、MABe22 小鼠三联组、MABe22 蝇群和 Shot7M2。进一步测试直接使用个体自身坐标系下原始关键点并做相同窗口汇总的更简单变体。

Result: 在 CalMS21 上，该免训练基线达到 85.8 macro F1，高于最佳编码器的 82.4；在四段标注视频条件下持平，在跨标注者和少样本学习场景中领先。MABe22 小鼠三联组上达到 83.7，高于公开 hBehaveMAE 的 80.3，差距 +3.4（95% 区间 +2.1 至 +4.7）。MABe22 蝇群 50 个任务上达到 51.6 mean F1，高于 BAMS 的 48.2。Shot7M2 上达到 68.8，高于 hBehaveMAE 的 64.7，但 hBehaveMAE 在极短动作上更好。短窗口承载主要性能，原始关键点在部分小鼠和 Shot7M2 数据集上也能匹敌或超过编码器，但在蝇群上仍依赖手工测量带来约 5.3 点提升。

Conclusion: 在当前这些行为分析基准上，现有自监督行为编码器尚未证明优于它们所学习的手工运动特征基线。论文建议未来评估行为编码器时必须报告此类强免训练基线，以避免复杂模型收益被高估；短窗口运动统计量是许多数据集上性能的主要来源。

Abstract: Self-supervised encoders for animal behavior are often trained on, or fed, hand-built movement features. We ask whether they beat those ingredients when compared fairly. A training-free representation, made of generic per-frame measurements summarized over fixed time windows and reduced with PCA, is compared with retrained and released encoders (BAMS, hBehaveMAE) under a preregistered protocol: frozen representations of equal size and one shared classifier. On CalMS21 it reaches 85.8 macro F1 against 82.4 for the best encoder, ties with four labelled videos, and leads when the annotator changes or new behaviors must be learned from a few videos; the reading holds for encoders retrained with three seeds. On the MABe22 mouse triplets it scores 83.7 against 80.3 for the released hBehaveMAE (gap +3.4, 95% interval +2.1 to +4.7). On a second species, the MABe22 fly groups, the same recipe scores 51.6 mean F1 over 50 tasks against 48.2 for BAMS, the best published method. On Shot7M2, the synthetic basketball benchmark built by hBehaveMAE's authors, it scores 68.8 against 64.7 for hBehaveMAE, the best method in that paper, though hBehaveMAE remains better on the shortest movements. Short time windows carry most of the result. Raw keypoints in each body's own frame, summarized over the same windows, already match or beat the encoders on both mouse datasets and on Shot7M2; on the fly groups they fall short, and the measurements add 5.3 points. On these benchmarks, current behavior encoders have yet to beat the hand-built features they learn from, and such baselines should be reported alongside them.

</details>


### [148] [The Elephant in the Question: Unified Models Picture the Concepts Behind Their Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24793)
*Tobias Braun,Marcus Rohrbach,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 V-lens，利用统一多模态模型中图像生成器与文本隐状态的连接，免训练地可视化 token 的中间表示；进一步通过 lens tuning 监督隐状态，显著提升指代关系可视化正确率，并改善文本答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型输出背后的中间推理步骤通常难以直接观察。统一多模态模型同时具备文本理解和图像生成能力，其图像生成器可能访问文本处理过程中形成的表示，因此可用于将抽象隐状态转化为可解释的视觉内容，帮助检查模型内部的推理与概念关联。

Method: 作者提出 V-lens，在无需额外训练解码器的情况下，将选定 token 的 hidden state 输入统一模型的图像生成通路进行可视化。实验覆盖 4 个扩散式统一模型和 2 个纯自回归统一模型，并在不同上下文、token 位置和模型深度下进行控制分析。随后提出 lens tuning，使用目标图像监督通过 V-lens 读出的特定 hidden state。

Result: V-lens 生成的图像能够揭示连接问题与答案的关键中间概念，即使这些概念未在问题中显式提及。可视化内容会随上下文、token 位置和层深变化。在 BAGEL、SenseNova-U1 和 HunyuanImage-3.0 上，对代词指代对象进行可视化监督后，正确可视化率从 2%-11% 提升到 86%-94%；同时，仅通过视觉监督隐状态而未直接监督答案 token，也提升了文本回答表现。

Conclusion: 统一多模态模型的生成通路可作为解释器，直接可视化文本处理过程中的中间概念。V-lens 为模型内部推理检查提供了免训练解释工具，而 lens tuning 表明视觉化监督可强化隐状态中的关键概念表示，并潜在提升语言问答能力。

Abstract: Visualizing intermediate representations could help us inspect the reasoning steps underlying a model output. Unified multimodal models offer this opportunity by combining understanding and generation in one architecture. Their image generators can access representations formed while processing text. We introduce the V-lens, which uses this connection to visualize a chosen token's hidden state without training a decoder. Across four diffusion-based and two purely autoregressive unified models, the resulting images reveal key intermediate concepts that connect a question to its answer even when the concepts are never named explicitly. Controlled tests show how this content changes with context, token position, and depth. We then introduce lens tuning, which supervises selected hidden states with pictures through the V-lens. Across BAGEL, SenseNova-U1, and HunyuanImage-3.0, the rate of correct visualizations of pronoun referents rises from 2-11% to 86-94%. Surprisingly, such visual supervision also improves text answers without supervising the answer tokens directly. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [149] [Ultimate3D: Teaching Multimodal LLMs 3D Camera-Object Relations by Large-Scale Visual Instruction Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24795)
*Liu He,Yizhi Song,Ruiqi Xu,Baris Sarper Tezcan,Xiao Zeng,Albert Y.C. Chen,Lu Xia,Shashwat Verma,Sankalp Dayal,Min Sun,Cheng-Hao Kuo,Daniel Aliaga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于3D资产、渲染与扩散模型、以及LLM自动生成文本指令的合成数据生成流水线，用于构建大规模、具有精确相机-物体关系标注的视觉指令数据集Ultimate3D，从而显著提升多模态大语言模型在相机-物体关系识别任务上的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在图像中准确理解相机与物体之间关系的能力较弱，尤其是在物体朝向、相机视角和相机景别等方面。这主要由于训练数据中缺少多样化的相机-物体关系及其对应的精细文本描述。

Method: 作者构建了一个合成视觉指令数据生成框架：以3D资产为输入，通过渲染模型和基于扩散的图像生成模型生成照片级真实图像，并保留精确的相机-物体关系；同时利用LLM生成用于指导图像生成和视觉指令微调的文本提示。基于该流程构建了包含240K视觉问答样本的Ultimate3D数据集及配套基准。

Result: 在Ultimate3D上微调的MLLM在相机-物体关系识别任务上显著优于多个商业模型，平均准确率提升29.7%，说明精确3D Grounding合成数据能有效提升模型对视角、朝向和景别等空间摄影关系的理解能力。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了大规模3D-grounded合成视觉指令数据对于提升MLLM空间摄影理解能力的重要性。Ultimate3D及其基准为后续MLLM在视觉问答、空间推理、摄影理解和具身视觉等方向的应用提供了重要数据与评测基础。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) struggle with accurately capturing camera-object relations, especially for object orientation, camera viewpoint, and camera shots. This stems from the fact that existing MLLMs are trained on images with limited diverse camera-object relations and corresponding textual descriptions. To address this, we propose a synthetic generation pipeline to create large-scale 3D visual instruction datasets. Our framework takes 3D assets as input and uses rendering and diffusion-based image generation models to create photorealistic images preserving precise camera-object relations. Additionally, large language models (LLMs) are used to generate text prompts for guiding visual instruction tuning and controlling image generation. We create Ultimate3D, a large-scale 3D-grounded 2D visual instruction dataset. Ultimate3D consists of 240K VQAs with precise camera-object annotations, and corresponding benchmark. MLLMs fine-tuned on our proposed dataset outperform commercial models by a large margin, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 29.7% on camera-object relation recognition tasks. Our code, dataset, and benchmark will contribute to broad MLLM applications.

</details>


### [150] [Edge-Efficient Joint RAW Image Compression and Restoration for Smart Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24805)
*Yunong Sun,Sicheng Chen,Fei Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出面向智能穿戴设备的非对称RAW压缩-复原框架：端侧轻量编码器将噪声RAW压缩为比特流，后端解码器联合去噪和去马赛克，并直接优化传输表示以重建干净图像；通过知识蒸馏进一步降低端侧计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能穿戴设备依赖摄像头感知环境，但其小型传感器常产生噪声RAW图像，且端侧算力和功耗有限，难以高质量完成图像复原。现有方案中，直接传输RAW会导致传输成本高，而常规压缩又可能为传感器噪声浪费码率，造成压缩与复原目标不匹配。

Method: 作者设计端到端联合优化框架：端侧使用轻量级RAW编码器生成比特流，后端大容量解码器对编码表示进行联合去噪与去马赛克重建。编码器通过知识蒸馏训练更小的模型以适应更严格的算力预算，并在真实与合成RAW数据上评估率失真/复原性能和端侧延迟。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在率复原性能上优于级联压缩-复原流水线和已学习压缩基线。在SIDD数据集上，约0.25 bpp时PSNR较级联基线提升超过1.5 dB。方法还在真实商业智能眼镜采集的12 MP RAW图像上验证；蒸馏后的编码器在智能手机和智能眼镜硬件上对256×256输出块实现相对端侧NAFNet的27.6倍NPU推理加速。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将RAW压缩与复原联合优化、并根据重建目标学习传输表示，可同时降低穿戴设备端计算负担和无线传输开销，同时保持较高图像质量，适合资源受限的智能眼镜等应用。

Abstract: Smart wearable devices, such as augmented reality glasses, increasingly rely on cameras to understand and interact with their surroundings. However, their compact sensors often capture noisy RAW images, while limited compute and power make high-quality on-device restoration difficult. This creates an emerging need to reduce both local computation and transmission cost without sacrificing image quality. Two common solutions are to offload restoration or to compress the raw data before transmission. The first makes RAW transmission costly; the second reduces the payload but can spend bitrate preserving sensor noise. We address this fundamental mismatch with an asymmetric framework for joint RAW compression and restoration that directly optimizes the transmitted representation for clean image reconstruction. A lightweight on-device encoder compresses noisy RAW measurements into a bitstream, while a higher-capacity backend decoder jointly denoises and demosaics the encoded representation. We use knowledge distillation to train a smaller encoder for tighter compute budgets. Experiments on real and synthetic data show improved rate-restoration performance over strong cascaded compression-restoration pipelines and learned compression baselines. On SIDD, our method improves PSNR by more than 1.5 dB over cascaded baselines at approximately 0.25 bits per pixel. We further validate the method on real 12 MP RAW images captured with commercial smart glasses and measure sender-side latency on both smartphone and smart-glasses hardware. The distilled encoder achieves a 27.6$\times$ NPU inference speedup over on-device NAFNet for 256$\times$256 output patches. The code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [151] [ReCoCo: From Patches to Compositions for Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24806)
*Juan Gutiérrez,Saman Zadtootaghaj,José Luis Blanco-Murillo,Sara Atito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReCoCo（Regional Composition Consistency），将自监督视觉表征中的跨视图一致性扩展到区域层级。它通过将增强视图共享内容表示为 patch 级预测在学习到的视觉原型上的组合，并对齐这些组合，使模型无需显式建立局部对应关系或语义分组即可学习强密集特征。ReCoCo 可与 teacher-student 自蒸馏结合，且不增加额外可学习参数，在分割、聚类、区域检索等密集任务上显著提升，同时保持图像级识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习已经能学习强大的图像级视觉表征，但学习高质量的密集视觉特征仍然困难。常见自监督方法依赖增强视图之间的一致性，这种图像级监督信号有效，但将其推广到局部视觉内容时存在挑战。现有方法通常需要在不同视图之间匹配单个 patch，或识别连贯区域，这引入了局部对应估计的不确定性，并预设了视觉内容应该如何分组，限制了密集特征学习的效果和稳定性。

Method: 作者提出 ReCoCo，一种原则性的区域组成一致性方法。其核心思想是：不要求不同视图之间建立显式局部对应，也不要求预先进行语义区域划分或实例分离。模型在 teacher-student 自蒸馏框架下，对增强视图的 patch 级预测进行组合，形成对共享区域内容的表示。具体来说，图像被划分为 patch，每个 patch 的预测被表示为一组学习到的视觉原型的组合；两个增强视图共享的局部区域可以通过这些 patch 级原型组合来对齐。通过最小化或匹配这些区域级组合表示，模型能够从包含多个视觉概念的复杂区域中学习，而无需事先将它们拆分为具体物体或语义类别。该方法不引入额外可学习参数，可直接集成到现有自监督框架中。

Result: 大量实验表明，ReCoCo 在冻结骨干网络的密集表征任务中取得显著提升，包括语义/实例分割、局部区域聚类和区域检索。同时，它在图像级分类、检索等全局识别任务上保持竞争性性能，缓解了传统自监督方法中密集特征质量与全局图像级表征质量之间常见的权衡问题。

Conclusion: ReCoCo 通过“区域组成一致性”将自监督学习中的跨视图一致性从图像级扩展到区域级，避开了显式局部对应和语义分组这两个关键困难。它以无额外参数的 teacher-student 框架实现强密集特征学习，同时保持全局识别能力，为视觉自监督学习提供了更通用且实用的区域级监督方案。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning has produced powerful visual representations, yet learning strong dense features remains challenging. While consistency between augmented views provides strong image-level supervision, extending this principle to local visual content is less straightforward. Existing approaches often match individual patches across views or identify coherent regions, introducing uncertainties in correspondence estimation and assumptions about how visual content should be grouped. We introduce ReCoCo (Regional Composition Consistency), a principled approach that extends cross-view consistency to the regional level without requiring such correspondences or semantic grouping. ReCoCo represents the content shared between augmented views through compositions of their patch-level predictions over learned visual prototypes. By aligning these compositions, the model learns from regions containing multiple visual concepts without requiring them to be separated beforehand. ReCoCo integrates naturally with teacher-student self-distillation and introduces no additional learned parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate substantial improvements in frozen-feature segmentation, clustering, and regional retrieval while maintaining competitive image-level recognition, alleviating the trade-off between dense and global representation quality.

</details>


### [152] [Does the Action Matter? Probing Action-Conditioned Semantic Forecasting as a World Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24840)
*Binay Kumar Singh,Niels Da Vitoria Lobo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究自动驾驶世界模型在动作条件语义预测中的“动作依赖性”问题，提出通过测试时干预动作来评估模型预测是否真正依赖自车动作；实验表明动作输入虽可提升精度，但模型对动作的依赖程度有限，且精度增益不能充分说明动作被强利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶世界模型评估主要关注预测保真度或规划性能，较少验证预测结果是否真正依赖所输入的动作。若模型主要依赖历史场景而非动作条件，则其用于反事实推理和模型预测控制的能力可能被高估。因此需要直接衡量动作条件预测模型的动作依赖性。

Method: 论文研究动作条件的语义预测任务：根据历史语义分割和自车动作预测未来语义分割。比较采用特征级动作条件和输入级动作条件的预测模型，以及无动作输入的同构模型。通过在测试时替换或干预动作，并用标准分割指标评估预测变化，以量化动作依赖性。方法先在具有已知动作依赖动态的合成环境中验证，再应用于 Cityscapes 和 BDD100K 的真实驾驶数据，包括数据集内以及跨车辆平台的评估。

Result: 实验显示，正确执行的动作在 Cityscapes 上最多提升中期 mIoU 2.5 个点，在 BDD100K 上最多提升 0.6 个点；而错误动作可使 mIoU 分别下降最多 23.2 点和 7.0 点，甚至低于无动作输入模型。在真实数据中，特征级条件化比输入级条件化对动作的依赖高 1.6 倍，且两者精度相近；在历史不能决定动作的合成环境中，两者动作依赖性相近。

Conclusion: 精度提升只能说明模型可能利用了动作信息，但不能说明其预测在多大程度上真正依赖动作。动作条件世界模型需要单独的动作依赖性评估，尤其是在自动驾驶反事实推理和规划场景中。特征级动作条件化在真实数据上可能比输入级条件化更有效地促使模型使用动作，但历史场景的强预测性也可能削弱动作依赖。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used in autonomous driving to predict how a scene evolves under the ego vehicle's actions, supporting counterfactual reasoning and model-based planning. However, existing evaluations focus on prediction fidelity and planning performance, and rarely verify that the predictions depend on the conditioning action. In this paper, we study action dependence in action-conditioned semantic forecasting, which predicts future semantic segmentation from past segmentations and the ego action. We compare a forecaster with feature-wise and input-level action conditioning against an identical model without action input, and measure action dependence with test-time action interventions scored by standard segmentation metrics. The evaluation is validated in a synthetic environment with known action-dependent dynamics and applied to Cityscapes and BDD100K, both within each dataset and across the two vehicle platforms. Experiments show that the executed action improves mid-term mIoU by at most 2.5 points on Cityscapes and 0.6 points on BDD100K, whereas a wrong action reduces it by up to 23.2 and 7.0 points, below the model without action input. On real data, feature-wise conditioning depends 1.6 times more on the action than input-level conditioning at similar accuracy, whereas both depend on it equally in the synthetic environment, where the history does not determine the action. These results show that an accuracy gain indicates that a driving world model uses its action, but not how strongly its forecasts depend on it. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [153] [Gaspard: Expressive unified speech and gesture synthesis with continuous autoregressive flow-matching and decoupled classifier-free guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24862)
*Téo Guichoux,Théodor Lemerle,Catherine Pelachaud,Nicolas Obin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Gaspard，一个将语音与协同手势生成统一建模的连续自回归流匹配模型，通过模态专用预测头和解耦无分类器引导提升生成同步性、表现力并缓解手势动态坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音—手势生成方法多采用级联管线：先生成语音，再用语音驱动手势。这种方式使语音生成与手势生成彼此独立，难以建模人类交流中语音与手势之间高度协调、双向耦合的时序关系。

Method: 作者提出 Gaspard，基于连续自回归生成和 next-step flow-matching 预测，将语音与手势作为统一生成流进行建模，并在大规模多模态数据集 Seamless Interaction 上训练。考虑到语音和手势在时间粒度与动态特性上的异质性，模型使用模态专用预测头，并引入对两条生成流分别控制的解耦 classifier-free guidance。

Result: 客观评估和主观感知评估表明，Gaspard 在单一模态基线中表现较强的手势生成能力，并且相比匹配条件的级联系统能够生成高度同步的语音与手势。

Conclusion: Gaspard 证明了统一联合生成语音与手势的可行性。相比级联方法，该模型能更好地建模模态间协调性；模态专用头与解耦引导有助于保持生成稳定性和表现力。

Abstract: Human communication involves highly coordinated multimodal signals such as speech and co-speech gestures. Existing approaches for generating both modalities commonly follow a cascaded pipeline, where speech is generated first and subsequently used to drive gesture generation; speech generation is therefore independent of gestures. We introduce Gaspard, a unified speech--gesture generation model based on continuous autoregressive generation with next-step flow-matching predictions, and trained on Seamless Interaction, a large-scale multimodal dataset. The heterogeneous temporal properties of speech and gesture motivate modality-specific prediction heads and decoupled classifier-free guidance over the two generation streams. This guidance rebalances the influence of speech and gesture at inference time, improving expressivity while mitigating dynamics collapse in continuous autoregressive gesture generation. Objective and perceptual evaluations show that Gaspard is a strong gesture generation model against unimodal baselines and that it produces highly synchronous speech and gestures compared to a matched cascaded system.

</details>


### [154] [NereidVLA: Learning to Understand and Navigate the Underwater World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24864)
*Chenjie Zhao,Zhiliang Zhu,Quan Chen,Yuquan Xue,Xingxue Shi,Puyue Wang,Zhiqiang Zhao,Kangye Zheng,Boyang Xu,Mingwei Lin,Dejun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出NereidVLA，先通过大规模海洋视觉语言数据学习空间语义，再用仿真水下轨迹适配为语言条件三维导航模型，实现闭环水下导航并在真实水池中取得较高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下导航需要将视觉空间线索转化为运动控制，但海洋视觉语言数据集中通常缺少与机器人轨迹成对的监督信号，因此难以直接把海洋场景中的空间语义知识迁移到机器人导航任务中。

Method: 作者提出NereidVLA：首先构建Sea2Know阶段，在约两百万条海洋视觉语言样本上训练目标定位、空间关系和场景结构理解能力，不使用机器人运动配对数据；随后在模拟水下机器人动力学下，利用专家轨迹进行导航适配。模型输入RGB历史帧和语言指令，输出离散运动token，并解码为局部三维轨迹用于闭环跟踪。

Result: 在独立六维水下导航理解基准、离线预测和闭环导航评估中，海洋空间预训练在匹配的训练与执行设置下将仿真导航成功率从72.4%提升到84.4%。在真实实验室水池机器人测试中，NereidVLA无需在测试环境微调即可达到78.75%成功率，高于对比方法InternVLA-N1的47.50%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，仅依靠海洋空间语义监督即可有效支持下游机器人运动学习，使视觉语言模型迁移到真实水下三维导航任务中具备可行性。

Abstract: Underwater navigation requires translating visual spatial cues into motion, yet marine vision-language datasets rarely include paired robot trajectories. We present NereidVLA, a vision-language-action model that transfers marine spatial knowledge to language-conditioned 3D navigation. We first train Sea2Know on approximately two million marine examples to learn target grounding, spatial relations, and scene structure without paired robot-motion supervision. We then adapt it using expert trajectories executed under simulated underwater vehicle dynamics. Given RGB histories and a language instruction, NereidVLA predicts discrete motion tokens that decode into local 3D trajectories for closed-loop tracking. Evaluation combines an independent six-dimensional underwater navigation understanding benchmark with offline prediction and closed-loop navigation. Under matched navigation training and execution settings, marine spatial pretraining increases simulation success from 72.4% to 84.4%. Without fine-tuning on the real test environment, NereidVLA achieves 78.75% success in laboratory-pool robot trials, compared with 47.50% for InternVLA-N1. These results show that marine spatial supervision can support downstream motion learning and physical underwater navigation.

</details>


### [155] [PrismRestore: A Compact Restoration Toolbox for Agentic Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24915)
*Zhaocheng Yu,Kui Jiang,Jing Cao,Yiang Chen,Xiaocheng Feng,Junjun Jiang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出 PrismRestore，通过从单一冻结的全合一图像复原模型中提取可调用复原动作库，并结合强化学习策略进行组合优化，在不增加多个复原骨干网络或任务适配器的情况下处理混合退化图像复原任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Agentic image restoration 方法通常需要保存多个专用复原网络以应对混合退化，导致存储和推理开销较高。该工作希望利用单一通用复原模型内部的任务相关通道响应特性，构建轻量、可组合且推理更高效的复原代理。

Method: 作者提出 PrismRestore。首先离线进行 task-prior search，基于激活和梯度统计识别具有相似响应模式的共享模型通道，并对非代表性通道搜索任务特定调制系数，保留代表性通道不做调制，从而形成紧凑的可调用动作库。随后训练状态条件化策略，使用 Group Relative Policy Optimization 学习选择和组合这些动作，其目标在同一输入采样的轨迹组内分别归一化保真度和感知反馈，并融合终端评估与基于逐步质量变化的 return-to-go。

Result: 在合成复合退化和真实世界多任务基准实验中，PrismRestore 相较于评测的全合一基线方法取得提升，复原质量与多模型 agent 方法相当，同时仅使用单一复原骨干网络，并且推理速度优于所评测的 agentic 基线。

Conclusion: PrismRestore 证明可以从单一冻结的全合一复原模型中构建可组合动作先验库，并通过状态条件化策略实现有效图像复原动作选择与组合，从而在保持复原性能的同时降低模型存储和多骨干推理成本。

Abstract: Agentic image restoration composes operations to handle mixed degradations, yet its toolbox often requires storing multiple restoration networks. We propose PrismRestore, which constructs a compact bank of callable restoration actions from a single frozen all-in-one model. Our approach builds on task-dependent yet overlapping channel responses in the shared model. An offline task-prior search uses activation and gradient statistics to group channels with similar response profiles, then searches task-specific modulation coefficients over non-representative channels while leaving representative channels unmodulated. The resulting prior bank enables explicit action selection and composition without additional restoration backbones or task-specific adapter modules. A state-conditioned policy learns to compose these actions using Group Relative Policy Optimization. Its objective separately normalizes fidelity and perceptual feedback within groups of trajectories sampled for the same input, and combines terminal evaluation with return-to-go derived from stepwise quality changes. Experiments on synthetic composite-degradation and real-world multi-task benchmarks show improvements over the evaluated all-in-one baselines and restoration quality competitive with multi-model agents. PrismRestore achieves these results with a single restoration backbone and faster inference than the evaluated agentic baselines.

</details>


### [156] [Unified Law for Visual Tasks (The Four Laws of Computer Vision)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24917)
*Sen Fang,Yalin Feng,Yancheng Zhang,Yanxin Zhang,Zonglin Lyu,Hongbin Zhong,Liwei Che,Rui Wu,Chen Chen,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出“视觉任务统一法则”（Unified Law for Visual Tasks）及其细化的“计算机视觉四法则”，并构建统一训练框架与“世界快照模型”（World Snapshot Models, WSMs），试图以单一框架覆盖几乎所有视觉与可视化任务。其核心假设是：图像可视为世界信息的快照，图像处理和图像生成可分别理解为对世界信息的处理与生成；若所有视觉任务都能被转化为生成任务，则可用统一模型完成多数视觉任务。论文在超过1000个训练和评估的视觉任务上验证了该假设，并提出面向未来视觉任务研究和应用的统一训练框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算机视觉中的任务类型繁多，包括识别、检测、分割、生成、编辑、可视化等，传统方法往往为不同任务设计不同模型、损失函数或训练流程。受大语言模型在自然语言处理中实现统一建模的启发，作者希望探索视觉任务是否存在类似的统一范式：如果图像承载世界信息，那么许多视觉任务本质上都可以被重新表述为基于图像的生成或映射问题，从而用一个框架统一处理。

Method: 作者提出“视觉任务统一法则”，并进一步划分为“计算机视觉四法则”。其方法论起点是：将现实世界信息编码为图像，即“世界快照”（World Snapshots），进而把图像处理视为对世界信息的处理，把图像生成视为世界快照的生成。在此基础上，作者将大量视觉与可视化任务重新形式化为生成任务，并提出“世界快照模型”（WSMs）作为统一方法。论文还构建了一个统一训练框架，使单一框架能够训练和执行多种视觉任务，并通过大规模任务实验进行验证。

Result: 论文在超过1000个训练和评估的视觉任务上进行验证，这些任务被定义为不同的输入—输出映射关系。实验结果支持作者提出的核心假设，即绝大多数视觉任务可以通过统一的生成式框架进行处理。论文因此展示了统一视觉任务建模的可行性，并为后续视觉任务研究与应用提供了统一的训练框架。

Conclusion: 该论文的核心贡献是提出了一种面向视觉任务的统一理论视角和训练框架：通过将图像视为“世界快照”，并将多数视觉任务重新表述为生成任务，可以使用类似大语言模型的方式统一处理大量视觉问题。结果表明，这一思路在大规模任务上具有可行性，可能推动计算机视觉从任务专用模型向统一视觉模型发展。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a Unified Law for Visual Tasks, which could be further divided into Four Laws of Computer Vision, with the goal of enabling a single framework or method to uniformly train on and perform nearly all visual tasks. Our starting point is an inspiration and hypothesis: if the world information is encoded into image, which we call World Snapshots, then image processing is world processing, and image generation is World Snapshot generation. Furthermore, if all visual and visualization tasks can be reformulated as generation tasks, then a unified method, World Snapshot Models (WSMs), can perform the vast majority of visual tasks, much as large language models have largely unified natural language processing. This paper validates these laws across more than 1,000 trained and evaluated visual tasks (defined by distinct input-output mappings) and presents a unified training framework for future visual-task research and applications.

</details>


### [157] [UltraTex: Aligning Physical Knowledge for High-Fidelity PBR Texture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24941)
*Jingzhi Bao,Xiaohe Ma,Hong Li,Yuanming Hu,Xiaoguang Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UltraTex 提出了一种级联多视角扩散框架，用于从单张参考图像生成物理正确的基础渲染（PBR）纹理贴图。该方法通过联合预测反照率与世界空间法线、结合视觉语言模型（VLM）的材料语义先验，以及采用光度表征对齐来引导金属度和粗糙度图的生成，从而提升了重光照（relighting）场景下的物理真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张参考图像生成PBR纹理具有高度歧义性，因为观察到的外观（appearance）将材质身份、表面反射率和光照条件纠缠在一起。现有的生成方法虽然能生成逼真的反照率贴图，但在金属度和粗糙度属性的推断上表现不佳，导致物体在改变光照进行重渲染时物理保真度不足。

Method: UltraTex采用级联的多视角扩散生成策略：1. 联合预测反照率图与世界空间着色法线，以生成细节更丰富的反照率；2. 在生成的反照率、参考图像及材料语义先验的约束下预测金属度-粗糙度（MR）图；3. 引入轻量级适配器，将视觉语言模型（VLM）的隐藏层特征转化为内在材料表征Token，为MR估计提供部件级的知识锚点；4. 提出“光度表征对齐”技术，将中间扩散特征引导至受控光照下的真实材料渲染结果，使金属度和粗糙度的变化能通过高光强度、镜面锐度及局部反射对比度被观测到，从而在不使用显式光照监督的情况下实现精细化的材质预测。

Result: 大量实验证明，UltraTex在视觉保真度、材质准确性以及物理合理性方面，均优于现有的开源方法以及商业方法。

Conclusion: 将物理知识（如光度表征对齐）和语义先验（如VLM特征）整合到多视角扩散模型中，能有效解决单图像PBR生成的歧义性问题，显著提升纹理资产的材质属性和重光照表现力。

Abstract: Generating physically based rendering (PBR) texture maps for a 3D mesh from a single reference image is highly ambiguous, as the observed appearance entangles material identity, surface reflectance, and illumination. While current generative methods produce realistic albedo maps, they still struggle to infer accurate metallic and roughness attributes for relighting. We present UltraTex, a cascaded multi-view framework that moves beyond appearance matching by integrating physical knowledge into multi-view diffusion-based PBR generation. UltraTex first jointly predicts albedo and world-space shading normals to generate more detailed albedo maps, and then predicts metallic-roughness (MR) maps conditioned on the generated albedo, reference image, and material semantic priors. To inject material semantics, a lightweight adapter transforms hidden-layer features of a vision-language model (VLM) into intrinsic material representation tokens, providing part-level knowledge anchors for MR estimation. To model material-light interaction, photometric representation alignment guides intermediate diffusion features toward ground-truth material renderings under controlled lighting. This makes metallic and roughness variations observable through highlight intensity, specular sharpness, and local reflectance contrast, providing stronger guidance for fine-grained material prediction without explicit lighting supervision. Extensive experiments show that UltraTex generates high-quality textures and outperforms existing open-source and commercial methods in visual fidelity, material accuracy, and physical plausibility.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [RoboBuilder: Agentic Robot Co-Design with Real-world Video Feedback in the Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23855)
*Ghadi Nehme,Dahyun Nam,Akshay Govind Srinivasan,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RoboBuilder是一个用于评估AI智能体能否协同设计机器人硬件与程序并在真实世界中运行的基准；实验显示智能体能完成多数简单障碍并提出新颖机构，但复杂任务与虚实迁移仍显著受限，闭环反馈可改善真实失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI编码智能体评测多停留在模拟或虚拟环境，难以衡量其面向真实物理系统的工程能力；该工作旨在检验智能体能否从零件数据、仿真通过设计出发，实际制造并通过机器人课程，并量化“仿真成功”到“真实成功”之间的差距。

Method: 构建RoboBuilder基准，让编码智能体基于标准零件、电机和定制3D打印部件共同设计机器人硬件与控制程序；所有通过仿真测试的设计都需在实体赛道上验证。实验覆盖四个前沿编码智能体、三类难度的十个障碍，共进行16,427次仿真、生成12,774种不同机器人并制作135台实体机器人。智能体还可通过请求测量数据和观看真实运行视频来学习仿真器误差，并在看到失败视频后重新设计机器人。

Result: 仅靠仿真不足：在公开零件数据下，15个初始通过仿真的设计均未能通过真实赛道。整体表现上，智能体能解决几乎所有简单障碍，并设计出搜索基线难以找到的新机构；但在更高难度、要求单一机器人跨越全部障碍以及真实世界泛化方面仍易失败，所有难度的仿真通过设计在真实环境中只有66%保持成功。闭环反馈有效：观看真实失败视频后，智能体可重新设计并有时校正仿真器，减少72%的一级和二级失败。

Conclusion: RoboBuilder为衡量AI智能体设计真实机器人能力提供了关键基准，表明当前前沿编码智能体具备一定实体工程设计潜力，但虚实差距仍是主要瓶颈；基于真实运行观测的闭环迭代能部分缩小该差距，推动智能体向能工程化真实机器的方向发展。

Abstract: Can AI agents design robots that work in the real world? We introduce RoboBuilder, a benchmark in which coding agents co-design a robot's hardware and program from standard parts, motors and a custom 3D-printed part, and every design that passes in simulation is built and tested on a physical course. Simulation alone is not enough: under public part data, none of 15 initial passing designs crossed the real course, so an agent learns the simulator from measurements it requests and videos of real runs. Across four frontier coding agents, ten obstacles at three difficulty levels, 16,427 simulations of 12,774 distinct robots and 135 physical robots built in an estimated 240 hours, agents solve nearly all easy obstacles and invent mechanisms our search baselines do not reach, but falter at harder levels, when one robot must cross every obstacle, and in the real world, where 66% of simulated passes hold across all levels. Closing the loop helps: shown a video of its real failure, an agent redesigns the robot, sometimes correcting its simulator, and removes 72% of level-1 and level-2 failures. RoboBuilder measures progress toward agents that engineer real machines. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [RageVLA: Augmentation-Trained Perception and Action for State-of-the-Art Robustness on LIBERO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23517)
*Amrit Lal Singh,Smarak Kanjilal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RageVLA 提出将视觉-语言-动作模型中的“感知-动作”关系解耦：一方面对 LIBERO 演示做鲁棒增广，另一方面用冻结 DINOv3 特征与 TinyCLIP 嵌入向策略提供物体级场景表示。该方法仅用 0.21B 参数就在 LIBERO-PRO 上取得新的 VLA SOTA（0.72），并通过消融证明策略确实依据被感知到的物体位置行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在 LIBERO 上看似饱和，但在 LIBERO-PRO 中一旦物体位置改变或指令要求新操作就会显著退化。作者认为问题不在模型规模，而在策略将“做什么”与训练时的物体位置、周围场景和指令措辞过度绑定，缺乏对场景的解耦、可泛化表示。

Method: 方法包括两部分。1）数据增广：按通用规则重排 LIBERO 演示，包括移动和旋转所有物体与固定装置、将任务迁移到其他环境和表面、以及改写每条指令。2）解耦感知编码：基于冻结 DINOv3 特征训练感知引擎，输出物体列表及其位置、夹爪注视内容的紧凑编码，并结合 TinyCLIP 提取的图像和指令嵌入；随后从头训练一个 flow-matching diffusion transformer 作为动作模型，在该解耦视图上执行任务。

Result: RageVLA 以 0.21B 参数在 LIBERO-PRO 上达到 0.72，优于最佳已发表 VLA 模型的 0.53，更大变体达到 0.73。LIBERO-Object 测试显示：当输入给策略的物体位置被打乱而相机图像仍正确时，成功率从 0.965 降至 0.215；当目标物体被位移 28 cm 时，RageVLA 仍有 0.865 的成功率，而在同一评测框架下的 π0.5 仅为 0.105。

Conclusion: 提升 VLA 在空间扰动和指令变化下的泛化，关键可能不是扩大规模，而是切断动作输出对训练时具体布局、背景和措辞的依赖。鲁棒数据增广与物体级解耦感知编码能使策略真正基于“看到了什么、在哪里”进行行动，从而显著增强 LIBERO-PRO 上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models look saturated on LIBERO, yet most of them fall apart on LIBERO-PRO as soon as an object moves or the instruction asks for something new. We argue that what is missing is not scale but decoupling. In these policies, what to do is tied to where things were during training, to the scene around them and to the wording of the instruction. We cut these ties in two places. In the data, LIBERO demonstrations are re-arranged by general rules: every object and fixture is moved and rotated, tasks are carried into other environments and onto other surfaces, and every instruction is paraphrased. In the policy, a perception engine on frozen DINOv3 features, trained on this augmented data, hands the action model the scene in decoupled form: a list of objects with their positions, and a compact code of what the gripper is looking at, next to image and instruction embeddings from TinyCLIP. A flow-matching diffusion transformer, trained from scratch, acts on this view. We call this combination of Robust Augmentation and Grounded Encoding RageVLA. With 0.21 billion parameters, RageVLA sets a new state of the art on LIBERO-PRO among VLA policies, scoring 0.72 against 0.53 for the best published model, and a larger variant reaches 0.73. Two further tests on LIBERO-Object show that the policy acts on what it perceives. When the object positions it is given are shuffled while the camera images stay correct, its success falls from 0.965 to 0.215. When the target object is displaced by 28 cm, it still succeeds in 0.865 of the episodes, where $\pi_{0.5}$, run through the same harness, succeeds in 0.105.

</details>


### [160] [How Uncertain Is My Action? Self-Correcting Visual Planning with Frozen World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23620)
*Siqing Li,Luming Yang,Yijia Jiao,Junze Deng,Alper Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种“反向运行”冻结潜在世界模型的方法，用于检测视觉规划器在执行过程中出现的动作/驱动偏移，并利用该偏移分数进行自我校正。实验表明该方法在模拟与真实机器人上均能显著提升控制成功率并降低执行误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的潜在世界模型配合模型预测控制可在部署阶段作为视觉规划器使用，但实际执行动作可能与模型指令动作不一致。由于世界模型被冻结，它本身无法察觉这种“动作接口”层面的偏移，而传统残差能量又会混淆模型误差与执行偏移，导致校正困难。

Method: 作者让冻结世界模型反向推断：对任意假设的驱动偏移，预测在该偏移下本应观测到的结果，并与实际观测比较，从而构造“偏移分数”。该分数只保留残差中能够由驱动偏移解释的部分。随后进行局部最优检测、Cramér-Rao误差估计、识别主导偏移生成方向，并在报警触发时对规划器进行补偿校正。

Result: 在PushT、TwoRoom和OGBench-Cube上使用冻结潜在世界模型测试，检测性能与理论预测误差控制在0.05 AUROC以内。自我校正将PushT成功率从0.650提升至0.862，Cube任务从0.378提升至0.432，与已知真实偏移的oracle水平相当，并优于多种常见测试时自适应方法。在真实机械臂上，该方法识别出关节编码器无法记录的基座关节偏移，将可达误差从36 mm降至3–5 mm；在随机且隐藏的真实偏移条件下，估计可恢复真实偏移校正带来误差降低的92–94%。

Conclusion: 冻结世界模型不仅可以用于前向预测未来，还可以充当滤波与检验器，用于检测动作接口不确定性、判断动作有效性并进行错误校正。该工作展示了通过模型反演与统计检验实现视觉规划器自我诊断和自我修复的可行路径。

Abstract: A frozen latent world model combined with sampling-based model predictive control is a visual planner. At deployment, the executed action can depart from the commanded one, and the frozen model has no way to notice. We show that the model can be run in reverse: for any hypothesized actuation shift, it predicts what it should have observed under that shift, and comparing this prediction with what was actually observed quantifies the shift and lets the planner correct itself. Unlike the residual energy, which mixes the shift with the model's own error, the score keeps only the part of the residual that a shift can produce. This shift score detects the shift with a locally optimal test, estimates it with Cramer-Rao error bars, names the dominant generator, and compensates the planner once an alarm fires. On PushT, TwoRoom and OGBench-Cube with a frozen latent world model, measured detection follows the prediction to within 0.05 AUROC. Self-correction raises success from 0.650 to 0.862 on PushT and from 0.378 to 0.432 on the harder Cube task, levels with an oracle that is told the true shift at the same block, better than five common test-time adaptation methods. On a real robotic arm, the score identifies a base-joint offset that no joint encoder registers and reduces the reach error from 36 mm to 3-5 mm, and under shifts drawn at random and sealed before the session, estimates recover 92-94% of the error reduction achieved with the true shift. The broader implication is that a frozen world model can act as a filter by predicting the future, testing action-interface uncertainty and validity, and finally correcting failures.

</details>


### [161] [Facet-SLAM: Real-Time Triangle Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23935)
*Edward Beng Wai Tan,Siew-Kei Lam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Facet-SLAM 是首个面向 RGB-D 相机的实时三角形 splatting SLAM 系统，通过解耦跟踪与建图实现高效几何配准和可直接三角化的表面地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 3D Gaussian splatting 的稠密 SLAM 渲染质量高但表面几何差，需要额外提取或融合网格；三角形 splatting 可直接三角化为网格，但已有方法依赖光度跟踪，速度不足以满足传感器频率下的实时定位。

Method: 提出 Facet-SLAM，将跟踪器与建图器职责分离：跟踪器利用融合深度设置每个三角形质心，并以 GICP 进行几何配准；建图器通过变量替换限制为非平移更新，避免三角形偏离观测表面导致配准目标不可靠。

Result: 系统可在桌面 GPU 上以约 100 FPS 实时运行，在 Jetson AGX Orin 上达到传感器频率；相比光度方法速度提升超过 20 倍，渲染质量相当，并显著提升表面精度，Replica 数据集上 Delaunay 网格的 Chamfer 距离从 1.55 cm 降至 0.86 cm。

Conclusion: Facet-SLAM 证明了三角形 splatting 可用于实时 RGB-D SLAM，在保持高质量渲染的同时直接获得连通网格并提升几何精度，代码将开源。

Abstract: Dense SLAM systems based on 3D Gaussian splatting build maps that render photorealistically, but their surface geometry is poor, and obtaining a mesh requires extraction or fusion stages. Triangle splatting avoids this with surface primitives whose vertices can be triangulated directly into a connected mesh, yet SLAM with this representation has so far relied on photometric tracking, which is too slow for localization at sensor rate. We present Facet-SLAM, the first real-time triangle splatting SLAM system for RGB-D cameras. Fast geometric registration is the natural alternative, but it is not sufficient alone, since the mapper's optimization displaces triangles from the observed surface and makes them unreliable registration targets. We therefore separate the two roles: the tracker sets each triangle's centroid from fused depth and registers frames to these centroids by GICP, while the mapper is restricted to non-translating updates via a change of variables. Our system achieves competitive tracking accuracy at around 100 FPS on a desktop GPU and runs at sensor rate on a Jetson AGX Orin. Compared with the photometric approach, it is over 20 times faster, matches rendering quality, and improves surface accuracy, reducing the chamfer distance of the Delaunay mesh on Replica from 1.55 to 0.86 cm. We will release the implementation at this https URL.

</details>


### [162] [WAM-Policy: World Action Models with On-Policy Distillation for Quadrupedal Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24011)
*Junlin Long,Xu Deng,Yiran Wang,Yue Yang,Yang Zhao,Zeyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 WAM-Policy，将面向机械臂预训练的世界动作模型迁移并加速为四足机器人语言条件 loco-manipulation 策略，在仿真与真实平台上取得较高成功率和显著推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人可在行走的同时利用前腿操作物体，但现有 loco-manipulation 策略通常不支持语言指令控制；世界动作模型可作为语言条件操作策略，但其通常为机械臂预训练，且推理采样速度慢，难以直接用于四足机器人。

Method: WAM-Policy 分两阶段：首先通过跨形态策略适配将机械臂中心的 WAM 迁移到四足机器人，并用 on-policy 自蒸馏将迁移后的教师策略压缩为只需一次视频输入和两个动作步骤的学生策略；其次对教师策略进行任务特定强化学习微调，再通过多教师 on-policy 蒸馏，将多个慢速专家策略合并为一个少步学生策略。

Result: 在包含 10 个仿真任务、1000 条无需遥操作合成演示的 QLM-Bench 上，迁移后的策略成功率达到 77.0%，OPSD 学生策略保持 76.7% 且采样速度提升 14 倍；进一步结合任务特定强化学习和 M-OPD 后成功率提升至 85.0%，并在真实 Unitree Go2 Air 上完成部署。

Conclusion: WAM-Policy 成功将语言条件世界动作模型扩展到四足 loco-manipulation 场景，在保持较高任务性能的同时显著降低推理成本，为低成本、可部署、可语言控制的四足操作策略提供了有效路径。
