Abstract: Arm-free quadrupeds can perform loco-manipulation, walking on three legs while manipulating with a front leg, yet no existing policy for it follows language instructions. World action models (WAMs) have emerged as effective language-conditioned manipulation policies, but are pretrained for robot arms and sample slowly. WAM-Policy turns one WAM into a quadrupedal loco-manipulation policy via two stages. The first, Cross-Morphology Policy Adaptation and Acceleration, adapts the arm-centric WAM to quadrupeds and distills the transferred policy into a student with one video and two action steps by on-policy self-distillation (OPSD). This student, however, is bounded by its teacher, which only imitates demonstrations. The second stage, Task-Specific Improvement and Merging, therefore tunes the teacher by reinforcement learning (RL) and merges the slow specialists into one few-step student by multi-teacher on-policy distillation (M-OPD). On QLM-Bench, ten simulated tasks with 1,000 demonstrations synthesized without teleoperation, the transferred policy reaches 77.0%, and the OPSD student keeps 76.7% while sampling 14 times faster. Task-specific RL and M-OPD lift it to 85.0%. We also deploy the OPSD student on a real Unitree Go2 Air. WAM-Policy thus achieves a stronger quadrupedal policy at a fraction of the inference cost.

</details>


### [163] [SemPhys: Semantic-Physical Co-Learning for Humanoid Motion Generation via Differentiable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24074)
*Zejian Kang,Yeqiang Wang,Zekai Chen,Boyang Shen,Mingyu Ma,Bingqian Wu,Fan Zhou,Jianjie Zhang,Chentao Shen,Wentao Yang,Rui Qian,Xiangru Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 SemPhys 框架，主张在自然语言驱动人形机器人全身运动时，应从机器人“实际执行后的完整运动”层面评估语义对齐，并通过可微世界模型、跟踪控制器预测与语义反馈来优化运动生成器，使生成参考更接近执行效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类与机器人协作中，自然语言可用于直接指挥人形机器人完成全身动作，避免手动设计关节轨迹。常见方法是先根据指令生成运动学参考轨迹，再依赖跟踪控制器执行。但机器人在接触约束下维持平衡时，实际执行动作会偏离参考轨迹，进而改变其表达的动作语义。因此，仅评价参考轨迹的语义正确性不足，需要在物理执行后的完整运动上评估并优化语义一致性。

Method: SemPhys 通过模拟执行产生的语义反馈来训练运动生成器。其核心组件包括一个可微世界模型和一个固定的跟踪控制器，用于预测已记录执行轨迹中的短片段。框架将该预测片段插回原始位置，对由执行结果构成的完整轨迹进行指令-动作评分，并通过预测片段反向传播训练信号。为防止优化过程中生成结果漂移过大，SemPhys 还引入成对锚定损失，使新参考轨迹保持在初始生成器对应轨迹附近，从而稳定适配过程。

Result: 在 BONES-SEED 数据集上的实验表明，SemPhys 能显著保持从参考运动到执行运动之间的指令检索准确率，说明其在物理执行层面具有更好的语义对齐能力。相比所评估的其他方法，SemPhys 在执行运动上的检索准确率最高。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了人形机器人运动生成中的一个关键问题：参考轨迹语义正确并不等于执行轨迹语义一致。通过模拟执行、可微预测、指令级语义评分和反向传播，SemPhys 可将执行偏差纳入训练闭环，提高实际动作与语言指令之间的一致性。其方法为自然语言控制全身机器人动作提供了更贴近物理执行结果的优化范式。

Abstract: In human-robot collaboration, natural language lets people direct a humanoid's whole-body movements without programming joint trajectories. A common approach generates a kinematic reference from an instruction and relies on a tracking controller to execute it. However, as the controller maintains balance under contact constraints, the executed motion can depart from the reference in ways that change the action it conveys. Our central insight is that semantic alignment must be evaluated directly on the complete motion the robot physically executes. Building on this insight, we introduce \textbf{SemPhys}, a framework that trains motion generators using semantic feedback from simulated executions. A differentiable world model and a fixed tracking controller predict a short segment of a recorded execution. SemPhys inserts this prediction at its original position, scores the complete trajectory against the instruction, and backpropagates through the predicted segment. A paired anchoring loss stabilizes adaptation by keeping each generated reference close to its counterpart from the initial generator. On BONES-SEED, SemPhys largely preserves instruction retrieval accuracy from reference to execution and achieves the highest retrieval accuracy on executed motions among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [164] [Ego2X: Contact-aware Transfer from Egocentric Human Data to Any Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24138)
*Xinke Lyu,Bowen Yang,Zhicheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Ego2X是一种以物体和接触为中心的单目第一视角视频转换框架，将人类egocentric视频转化为可执行机器人数据，并基于CORE表示显著提升接触与物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人交互数据采集慢且成本高。现有ego-to-robot方法主要依据视觉外观评估保真度，容易忽略物体运动和接触驱动的物理合理性，导致手指悬空、夹爪空闭等失败。

Method: 提出Ego2X框架和CORE（Contact-centric Object-interaction REpresentation）：记录接触在物体表面的锚点、相对力、抓取模式和交互状态。利用CORE进行物体相对约束下的手物漂移校正、面向机器人需实现接触的逆运动学引导，并在臂擦除和机器人合成过程中保持被抓物体。

Result: 在HOT3D上，接触驱动校正使接触下手部深度误差降低33.7%；在EgoDex上，接触驱动重定向实现62.9%的标注接触，高于官方HuRo流水线的27.9%；在RoboTwin 2.0中，仅用Ego2X episode后训练X-VLA，可使200个配对场景中的任务成功率从40.5%零样本提升至67.0%。

Conclusion: 该研究证明以物体运动和接触关系建模是egocentric视频到机器人数据转换的关键，可提升物理可执行性和下游操作学习性能；作者将开源转换流程和带CORE标注的数据集。

Abstract: Embodied models depend on real interaction data, yet collecting it on physical robots is slow and labor-intensive. Recent ego-to-robot pipelines retarget first-person view human videos to robot data via transferring hand and arm motion. They judge fidelity mainly by visual appearance. However, trajectories often fail in terms of physics: fingers hover beside objects, and grippers close on empty space. We argue that how the object moves and the contacts that drive the motion count. Thus, we present Ego2X, an object-centric framework that converts monocular egocentric video into physically executable robot data across dexterous hands and parallel grippers. Ego2X centers on CORE (Contact-centric Object-interaction REpresentation), which records each contact on the object by its surface anchor, as well as relative force, grasp mode, and interaction state. Throughout the pipeline, CORE corrects hand-object drift via object-relative constraints, steers inverse kinematics toward contacts the robot must realize, and preserves held objects during arm erasure and robot compositing. On HOT3D, contact-driven correction reduces in-contact hand depth error by 33.7%, while on EgoDex, contact-driven retargeting realizes 62.9% of annotated contacts, surpassing the official HuRo pipeline (27.9%). In RoboTwin 2.0, post-training X-VLA on Ego2X episodes alone, without simulator demonstrations, raises task success rate from 40.5% zero-shot to 67.0% across 200 paired scenes. We will publicly release our conversion pipeline and CORE-annotated dataset for dexterous hands and parallel grippers.

</details>


### [165] [Efficient World-Model Planning via Physics-Guided Search Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24171)
*Hongyu Zhang,Xiaoshuai Hao,Heye Huang,Qingwen Meng,Wenjun Shi,Jianao Xu,Xin Hu,Xuanru Jin,Jingyi Zhang,Baicang Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向自动驾驶规划的物理引导搜索蒸馏方法 PGSD，通过可执行性筛选、搜索教师生成可行修正轨迹以及未来条件修复学生，实现单视角下基于世界模型反馈的可靠轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型可预测未来场景并评估候选轨迹，但准确预测未来并不等同于满足道路规则。当所有候选轨迹都越界时，单纯重排序无法选出可行方案，因此需要结合执行反馈和道路合规性验证来修复不可行轨迹。

Method: PGSD 是单视角规划框架，包含三个组件：Execution-Aware Selection 在模拟执行中检查自车道路合规性，并选择得分最高且可行的轨迹；Clearance-Aware Search Teacher 生成池外修正轨迹，并选择执行中边界净距更大的示范以缓解模仿误差；Future-Conditioned Repair Student 基于奖励器特征和执行反馈预测一条修正轨迹，并用单次 rollout 验证。预训练规划器和奖励器冻结，不在线生成未来场景。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v1 上，单视角 PGSD 达到 90.97 PDMS，超过对比的单视角规划方法，并保留搜索教师相对执行感知筛选 73.2% 的性能增益；在 NAVSIM v2 navhard 上达到 38.96 EPDMS，较 WorldDrive 提升 11.24 分，即 40.5%。学生模型将最多十次搜索 rollout 压缩为每次修复触发一次的单次验证 rollout。HUGSIM Hard 样例提供零样本迁移的定性证据。

Conclusion: PGSD 通过引入执行反馈和物理合规性验证，弥补了世界模型仅靠未来预测和候选重排的不足，提升了单视角自动驾驶规划的道路可行性和性能，同时通过蒸馏降低搜索代价。

Abstract: World models support autonomous driving by evaluating candidate trajectories through predicted future scene evolution. However, accurate future prediction does not ensure road-compliant execution; when all candidates violate road boundaries, re-ranking cannot select a feasible trajectory. We propose Physics-Guided Search Distillation (PGSD), a single-view planning framework that combines future representations and execution feedback for verified beyond-pool repair through three components: (1) Execution-Aware Selection checks ego-vehicle road compliance under simulated execution and selects the highest-scoring feasible trajectory; (2) a Clearance-Aware Search Teacher generates beyond-pool corrections and selects demonstrations with larger minimum road-boundary clearance during execution to buffer imitation errors; and (3) a Future-Conditioned Repair Student predicts one correction from rewarder features and execution feedback for single-rollout verification. The pretrained planner and rewarder remain frozen; no future scenes are generated online. On NAVSIM v1, single-view PGSD achieves 90.97 PDMS, exceeding all compared prior single-view planners and retaining 73.2% of the search teacher's PDMS gain over execution-aware selection. On NAVSIM v2 navhard, it reaches 38.96 EPDMS, improving on WorldDrive by 11.24 points (40.5%). The student replaces up to ten search rollouts with a single verification rollout per repair trigger. HUGSIM Hard examples provide qualitative evidence of zero-shot transfer.

</details>


### [166] [Towards Pose-Robust Few-Shot Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24360)
*Wenhao Li,Zhongyi Han,Yuhao Wei,Liang Han,Yilong Yin,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出 WACO，一种面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）机器人操作策略的“世界锚定规范化”方法，用于解决微调后策略默认相机和机器人基座位姿不变的问题。WACO 利用机械臂自身几何作为度量标定目标、静态场景作为世界锚点，仅凭单帧 RGB-D 图像即可估计相机和基座位姿偏移，并将图像、状态、动作和起始位姿统一到训练时的标定视角与标定基座坐标系中。实验表明 WACO 在模拟和真实 UR5e 机器人上显著提升了 VLA 策略对相机/基座位姿变化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人操作策略通常通过少量演示数据微调 VLA 模型获得，但该策略会隐式记住数据采集时的相机—机器人基座外参关系。一旦三脚架被碰动、相机发生位移，或机器人基座重新停靠并产生厘米级偏移，策略就会错误定位目标点。论文认为相机移动和基座移动本质上是同一类问题：机器人记住了固定的相机到基座外参，而实际外参发生变化。传统视角鲁棒方法往往依赖扰动训练数据或测试时标定，缺少一种无需额外采集数据、无需现场标定、同时可区分相机移动与机器人基座移动的通用方法。

Method: WACO 的核心思想是“世界锚定的规范化”（world-anchored canonicalization）。首先，机器人自身提供可解算的度量信息：通过前向运动学（forward kinematics）将机械臂按当前关节角放置，使其成为一个已知尺度和几何结构的标定目标。其次，静态场景作为世界锚点，用于判断当前视觉偏移究竟是相机移动还是机器人基座移动。基于单帧 RGB-D 图像，WACO 无需真值外参或额外训练数据即可估计相机与基座两类位姿偏移。随后，系统将观测图像规范化到采集/标定时的视角，同时将机器人状态、动作和起始位姿规范化到标定基座坐标系中。最后，WACO 使用其估计器产生的伪影对未修改的 VLA 策略进行微调，使策略能够容忍估计误差并完成从原始输入到规范化输入的适应。论文还提出 LIBERO-PoseShift 基准，用于测试 RGB VLA 模型在相机偏移、基座偏移及复合偏移下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PoseShift、LIBERO-Plus 相机轨迹以及四个真实 UR5e 机器人任务上，WACO 相比标准少样本微调流程和已有视角鲁棒方法均取得更高成功率。模拟环境中，WACO 将位姿偏移条件下的成功率提升 13.2 个百分点，并恢复到相对于拥有真值外参的 oracle 方法之间约 90% 的性能差距；真实机器人实验中，成功率提升 36.7 个百分点，恢复到真实场景性能差距的 87%。在“不使用扰动训练数据且不进行测试时标定”的方法中，WACO 建立了新的 state-of-the-art。

Conclusion: 论文表明，VLA 操作策略对相机和机器人基座位姿变化的脆弱性可统一归因于相机—基座外参的隐性记忆问题。WACO 通过机械臂几何作为度量标定、静态场景作为世界锚点，实现了从单帧 RGB-D 图像对相机和基座偏移的无需真值外参估计，并将观测与动作统一到标定坐标系，使未修改 VLA 策略在部署扰动下保持鲁棒。该方法无需额外扰动训练数据，也不需要测试时标定，为真实机器人少样本策略的位姿鲁棒性提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a vision-language-action (VLA) model on a few dozen demonstrations is the standard way to put a manipulation policy on a robot, yet the resulting policy silently assumes that the camera and the robot base stay exactly where they were during data collection: a bumped tripod or a base re-docked a few centimetres away makes it reach for the wrong place. We show that both failures are one phenomenon, a change of the camera-to-base extrinsic that the policy has memorised, and that the robot carries everything needed to undo it. We introduce WACO, a world-anchored canonicalization framework for VLA models: the arm, placed by forward kinematics at the measured joint angles, serves as a metric calibration target, and the static scene serves as the world anchor that tells a moved camera from a moved robot. From a single RGB-D frame, WACO estimates both shifts without ground-truth extrinsics or extra data, canonicalises the image to the calibration viewpoint and the state, action and start pose to the calibration base frame, and fine-tunes the unmodified policy under the artefacts of its own estimator. We also introduce LIBERO-PoseShift, the first benchmark for RGB VLAs with graded camera, base and compound shifts. Across LIBERO-PoseShift, the LIBERO-Plus camera track and four real-world tasks on a UR5e robot, WACO consistently outperforms the standard few-shot recipe and prior viewpoint-robust methods, raising shifted success by 13.2 points in simulation and by 36.7 points on the real robot, recovering 90% of the simulation gap to a ground-truth oracle and 87% of the real-robot gap, and establishing a new state of the art among methods that use no perturbed training data, with no test-time calibration. Project page: this https URL.

</details>


### [167] [RECLAIM: Robot-Vision-Informed Adversarial Patch Defense via Embodied Attention Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24494)
*Jiayou Zhu,Peng Guo,Litian Zhang,Xinze Shi,Yuting Zhang,Zelong Yu,Yize Tian,Jirong Liu,Zifeng Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RECLAIM是一种免训练、推理时防御方法，利用冻结视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型自身对局部视觉扰动引起的注意力变化反馈，结合机器人几何信息定位对抗贴片并执行局部净化，从而在闭环具身操控任务中对抗攻击并恢复任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体依赖感知-动作闭环执行操作，对抗贴片可通过污染视觉输入破坏动作预测，并进一步改变后续观测，形成攻击与交互耦合的闭环威胁。现有防御通常需要额外训练或辅助模型，难以在保持策略冻结和推理轻量性的同时有效应对动态具身场景中的视觉扰动。

Method: RECLAIM不修改VLA策略参数，而是在推理阶段使用冻结VLA的注意力响应：对图像进行局部视觉干预，观察注意力变化，将其作为“在哪里净化、如何净化”的反馈信号。方法进一步结合机器人几何信息优化贴片定位并选择局部处理策略；当智能体动作改变观测场景时，对定位与净化结果进行重新验证，形成面向闭环操控的视觉净化防御。

Result: 在来自四种对抗攻击方法的贴片攻击、多个操控任务套件和冻结VLA控制条件下，RECLAIM在95%的评测任务上至少有一次成功rollout；在LIBERO-Object任务上将无防御时的0% rollout成功率提升至70.0%。平均决策时间为每控制步548.9毫秒，表明其具有较低推理开销。

Conclusion: 策略驱动的视觉净化是保护具身智能体免受对抗贴片攻击的有效途径。RECLAIM通过利用冻结VLA内部注意力反馈和机器人几何信息，实现了无需额外训练、推理时部署的闭环防御，能够定位并净化局部视觉扰动，从而恢复具身操控任务的成功执行。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies control embodied agents through a perception-action loop grounded in physical interaction, where adversarial patches can corrupt action predictions and thereby alter subsequent observations. To mitigate these threats, many existing defenses rely on policy adaptation or auxiliary models, but require additional training or inference. We introduce RECLAIM, a training-free, inference-time defense that uses a frozen VLA's own attention response to local visual interventions to guide purification during closed-loop embodied manipulation. Our key insight is that attention changes induced by local edits provide feedback for choosing where and how to purify as the robot interacts with its environment. RECLAIM combines this feedback with robot geometry to refine patch localization and select local treatments, then revalidates them as the agent's embodied actions change the observed scene. We evaluate RECLAIM against patches generated by four adversarial attack methods across multiple manipulation task suites under frozen VLA control. Under adversarial patch attacks, RECLAIM succeeds in at least one rollout on 95% of the evaluated tasks and raises rollout success on LIBERO-Object from 0% without defense to 70.0%. Its mean decision time is only 548.9 ms per control step. These results highlight policy-guided visual purification as an effective approach to defending embodied agents against adversarial patches and recovering closed-loop task execution.

</details>


### [168] [$Z$-sight: Unifying Vision-Language-Action and Latent World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24940)
*Junjie Ye,Yiqi Zhao,Muchen Xu,Celina Shiyu Wang,Hao Dong,Ruohai Ge,Rong Xue,Benjamin J Kolligs,Aditya Dutt,Sasanka Nagavalli,Nassim A. Samad,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh,Yue Wang,Yuliang Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Z-sight，一种统一的机器人基础模型，将视觉-语言-动作建模与生成式潜在世界建模结合，在 VLM 的原生视觉 token 空间中直接预测未来视觉状态并联合生成连续动作，避免视频生成或外部编码器带来的额外成本，并在多项机器人任务上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界-动作模型能够联合预测未来画面和动作，有利于机器人控制，但通常继承视频生成模型的高计算开销。已有潜在空间策略虽然避免像素级生成，但往往只是对齐中间特征，或依赖冻结的目标编码器，使未来预测与 VLM 当前感知所处的原生特征空间脱节，难以实现真正统一的世界建模与动作生成。

Method: Z-sight 将未来预测直接放在 VLM 感知当前输入所使用的视觉 token 空间中进行。模型采用 mixture-of-transformers 架构，把 VLM、前瞻预测专家和连续动作专家耦合在一起。在推理阶段，Z-sight 联合去噪未来空间 token 和动作，不需要像素重建，也不依赖外部编码器。该模型同时使用人类视频和机器人轨迹进行训练。

Result: Z-sight 在 SIMPLE 基准上达到 81.9% 成功率，相比最强基线提升 9.6 个百分点；在六个 Unitree G1 任务上达到 73.3% 成功率，相比基线提升 20.0 个百分点，同时每个动作 chunk 推理时间为 110 ms。模型在桌面操作基准上也保持有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: Z-sight 表明，将世界模型的未来预测、VLM 感知和动作生成统一到同一视觉 token 空间，可以在避免昂贵视频生成的同时提升机器人控制性能。其 mixture-of-transformers 联合去噪机制为高效、可扩展的视觉-语言-动作机器人基础模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce $Z$-sight, a unified robot foundation model that couples vision-language-action (VLA) and generative latent world modeling. Video-based world action models (WAMs) show the value of joint future-and-action generation for control, yet inherit the heavy cost of video generators. Recent latent-space policies avoid pixel generation, yet typically align intermediate features or rely on frozen target encoders, decoupling future prediction from the VLM's native feature space. $Z$-sight instead predicts the future directly in the visual token space in which its VLM perceives the present. Coupling a foresight expert and a continuous action expert with the VLM in a mixture-of-transformers, $Z$-sight jointly denoises future spatial tokens and actions at inference without pixel reconstruction or external encoders. Trained on human video and robot trajectories, $Z$-sight achieves 81.9% success on SIMPLE (+9.6 points over the strongest baseline) and 73.3% across six Unitree G1 tasks (+20.0 points) at 110 ms per chunk, while remaining competitive on tabletop benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Counterfactual World Models: JEPAs That Reason About "What If"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24778)
*Randall Balestriero,Yann LeCun,Kerda Varaku*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需反事实数据、额外参数或重新训练的反事实世界模型方法：利用物理系统中“作用力分离”和二阶交互假设，从观测事实转移结果出发，结合世界模型在反事实动作与事实动作下的预测差异来估计反事实结局，并在像素级仿真和规划任务中显著提升反事实预测与后见规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界模型通常只能观测部分环境状态。隐藏扰动会改变动作后果，因此决策归因需要回答“在相同扰动下，如果采取不同动作会发生什么”。论文认为传统仅基于事实转移训练的世界模型无法独立回答反事实问题，因此需要一种从事实数据中近似估计反事实结果的方法，以区分糟糕决策与糟糕运气，并提升基于世界模型的规划与归因能力。

Method: 论文首先证明仅用事实转移训练的世界模型无法自行回答反事实问题。随后针对许多物理系统提出基于“自然不跳跃”的近似：若动作与扰动作为分离作用力，其交互在时间上为二阶效应，则对于时长为 h 的转移，反事实结果可近似为观测结果加上世界模型在反事实动作与事实动作下预测之差。该方法无需反事实数据、额外参数或重新训练。理论方面给出相对扰动方差 O(h^2) 的反事实误差上界，并匹配由未配对事实数据得到的极小极大下界，同时刻画不连续、坐标折叠和缩放指令的扰动等失败情况。实验使用 JEPA 世界模型和冻结图像编码器，从像素输入评估反事实 R^2 和后见规划成功率。

Result: 在四个渲染仿真器上，该方法将反事实 R^2 从 38%–46% 提升到 86%–96%；在第五个水下航行器任务上，使用多 16 倍事实数据仍达到 90%–94%。使用 DINOv2 与 MAE 冻结编码器验证有效，并且 DINOv3、SigLIP 2、CLIP 和 EVA-02 等冻结编码器在相同仿真器上达到 81%–95%。在后见规划中，该方法能在 35%–69% 的事件中找到本可到达目标的动作，而知道仿真器但不知道结果的 oracle planner 仅为 14%–23%。

Conclusion: 在动作与扰动分离且交互为二阶的物理系统中，事实观测与世界模型的动作预测差足以近似反事实结果。该方法以极低工程成本实现反事实世界模型，具有可证明的局部近似保证，并在像素级仿真和决策归因规划中显著优于传统事实世界模型。

Abstract: World models operate in a world they cannot fully observe. When a hidden disturbance, such as a current pushing a boat, changes the outcome of an action, a counterfactual question follows: what would have happened under a different action, subject to the same disturbance? Its answer separates bad decisions from bad luck. We prove that world models trained on factual transitions cannot answer it on their own, whatever their data or capacity. For many physical systems, the observed outcome and a simple property of the dynamics suffice: natura non facit saltus, nature makes no leaps. When actions and disturbances act as separate forces, their interaction is second-order in time, so the counterfactual outcome is, to first order, the observed outcome plus the difference between the world model's predictions under the alternative and factual actions. This yields a counterfactual world model with no counterfactual data, extra parameters, or retraining. For transitions of duration $h$, we prove an $O(h^2)$ bound on the counterfactual error relative to the disturbance variance, establish a matching minimax lower bound from unpaired factual data, and characterize failures at discontinuities, coordinate folds, and disturbances that scale the command. We validate these predictions from pixels with JEPA world models on frozen DINOv2 and masked autoencoder (MAE) encoders. On four rendered simulators, our approach improves counterfactual $R^2$ from $38$--$46\%$ to $86$--$96\%$ with the same world models; on a fifth, an underwater vehicle, it reaches $90$--$94\%$ with sixteen times more factual data. Four further frozen encoders, DINOv3, SigLIP~2, CLIP and EVA-02, give $81$--$95\%$ on the same simulators. In hindsight planning, it finds the action that would have reached the goal in $35$--$69\%$ of events, against $14$--$23\%$ for an oracle planner that knows the simulator but not the outcome.

</details>
